EntityBench: Towards Entity-Consistent Long-Range Multi-Shot Video Generation
Authors: Ruozhen He, Meng Wei, Ziyan Yang, Vicente Ordonez
First: 2026-05-14T17:59:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T17:59:55+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://catherine-r-he.github.io/EntityBench/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3 · Project1
Abstract
Multi-shot video generation extends single-shot generation to coherent visual narratives, yet maintaining consistent characters, objects, and locations across shots remains a challenge over long sequences. Existing evaluations typically use independently generated prompt sets with limited entity coverage and simple consistency metrics, making standardized comparison difficult. We introduce EntityBench, a benchmark of 140 episodes (2,491 shots) derived from real narrative media, with explicit per-shot entity schedules tracking characters, objects, and locations simultaneously across easy / medium / hard tiers of up to 50 shots, 13 cross-shot characters, 8 cross-shot locations, 22 cross-shot objects, and recurrence gaps spanning up to 48 shots. It is paired with a three-pillar evaluation suite that disentangles intra-shot quality, prompt-following alignment, and cross-shot consistency, with a fidelity gate that admits only accurate entity appearances into cross-shot scoring. As a baseline, we propose EntityMem, a memory-augmented generation system that stores verified per-entity visual references in a persistent memory bank before generation begins. Experiments show that cross-shot entity consistency degrades sharply with recurrence distance in existing methods, and that explicit per-entity memory yields the highest character fidelity (Cohen's d = +2.33) and presence among methods evaluated. Code and data are available at https://github.com/Catherine-R-He/EntityBench/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：EntityBench：迈向实体一致的长序列多镜头视频生成
多镜头视频生成将单镜头生成扩展为连贯的视觉叙事，但在长序列中保持角色、物体和场景的一致性仍具挑战。现有评估通常使用独立生成的提示集，覆盖范围有限且一致性度量简单，难以标准化比较。我们提出EntityBench，一个基于真实叙事媒体的140集（2491镜头）基准，包含每镜头明确的实体调度，同步追踪角色、物体和场景，涵盖易/中/难三级（最多50镜头）、13个跨镜头角色、8个跨镜头场景、22个跨镜头物体，以及最长48镜头的重复间隔。配套三支柱评估体系，分别评估镜头内质量、提示遵循度与跨镜头一致性，并设保真度门控，仅允许准确实体出现参与跨镜头评分。作为基线，我们提出EntityMem，一种记忆增强生成系统，在生成前将经验证的每实体视觉参考存入持久记忆库。实验表明，现有方法的跨镜头实体一致性随重复距离急剧下降，而显式每实体记忆方案在评估方法中实现最高角色保真度（Cohen's d = +2.33）与存在性。代码与数据见https://github.com/Catherine-R-He/EntityBench/。
Summary / 总结
To address the challenge of maintaining entity consistency across long video sequences in multi-shot generation, EntityBench introduces a benchmark of 140 narrative episodes with detailed per-shot entity tracking across characters, objects, and locations, enabling tiered difficulty and recurrence gap analysis. It is evaluated using a three-pillar metric suite that separately assesses intra-shot quality, prompt alignment, and cross-shot consistency, with a fidelity gate ensuring only accurate entity appearances are scored. The proposed EntityMem baseline, which stores verified visual references for each entity in persistent memory before generation, achieves the highest character fidelity (Cohen’s d = +2.33) and presence among evaluated methods, demonstrating that explicit per-entity memory significantly improves long-range consistency.
为解决多镜头视频生成中长序列下实体一致性难题，EntityBench构建了包含140个叙事片段、2491个镜头的基准数据集，详细记录每镜头中角色、物体与场景的跨镜头变化，支持最多50镜头、最长48镜头间隔的分层评估。配套三支柱评估体系分别衡量镜头内质量、提示遵循度与跨镜头一致性，并通过保真度门控确保仅准确实体出现才参与跨镜头评分。所提基线方法EntityMem在生成前将各实体的视觉参考存入持久记忆库，显著优于现有方法，在角色保真度（Cohen’s d = +2.33）与方法排名中表现最优，证明显式实体记忆对长序列一致性至关重要。
ATLAS: Agentic or Latent Visual Reasoning? One Word is Enough for Both
Authors: Ziyu Guo, Rain Liu, Xinyan Chen, Pheng-Ann Heng
First: 2026-05-14T17:59:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T17:59:55+00:00
Comments: Project Page: https://atlas-oneword.github.io Code: https://github.com/ZiyuGuo99/ATLAS
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3 · Project1
Abstract
Visual reasoning, often interleaved with intermediate visual states, has emerged as a promising direction in the field. A straightforward approach is to directly generate images via unified models during reasoning, but this is computationally expensive and architecturally non-trivial. Recent alternatives include agentic reasoning through code or tool calls, and latent reasoning with learnable hidden embeddings. However, agentic methods incur context-switching latency from external execution, while latent methods lack task generalization and are difficult to train with autoregressive parallelization. To combine their strengths while mitigating their limitations, we propose ATLAS, a framework in which a single discrete 'word', termed as a functional token, serves both as an agentic operation and a latent visual reasoning unit. Each functional token is associated with an internalized visual operation, yet requires no visual supervision and remains a standard token in the tokenizer vocabulary, which can be generated via next-token prediction. This design avoids verbose intermediate visual content generation, while preserving compatibility with the vanilla scalable SFT and RL training, without architectural or methodological modifications. To further address the sparsity of functional tokens during RL, we introduce Latent-Anchored GRPO (LA-GRPO), which stabilizes the training by anchoring functional tokens with a statically weighted auxiliary objective, providing stronger gradient updates. Extensive experiments and analyses demonstrate that ATLAS achieves superior performance on challenging benchmarks while maintaining clear interpretability. We hope ATLAS offers a new paradigm inspiring future visual reasoning research.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ATLAS：代理式还是潜在视觉推理？一个词即可兼顾两者
视觉推理常伴随中间视觉状态交织出现，已成为该领域有前景的方向。直接通过统一模型在推理中生成图像虽为直观方法，但计算成本高且架构复杂。近期替代方案包括通过代码或工具调用实现的代理式推理，以及利用可学习隐藏嵌入的潜在推理。然而，代理式方法受外部执行导致的上下文切换延迟影响，潜在式方法缺乏任务泛化能力且难以通过自回归并行化训练。为结合二者优势并缓解其局限，我们提出ATLAS框架：一个单一离散“词”（称为功能词）同时充当代理操作与潜在视觉推理单元。每个功能词关联内部化视觉操作，无需视觉监督，且作为标准分词词汇表中的词，可通过下一词预测生成。此设计避免冗长中间视觉内容生成，同时兼容原始可扩展SFT与RL训练，无需架构或方法修改。为进一步解决强化学习中功能词稀疏性问题，我们引入潜在锚定GRPO（LA-GRPO），通过静态加权辅助目标锚定功能词，提供更强梯度更新。大量实验与分析表明，ATLAS在挑战性基准上表现更优，同时保持清晰可解释性。我们希望ATLAS为未来视觉推理研究提供新范式。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the computational inefficiency of direct image generation and the limitations of agentic or latent reasoning methods, ATLAS introduces a single discrete 'functional token' that serves dual roles: as an agentic operation and a latent visual reasoning unit. This token, generated via standard next-token prediction without visual supervision, enables scalable training with vanilla SFT and RL without architectural changes. To address sparse token usage during RL, ATLAS employs Latent-Anchored GRPO, which stabilizes training by anchoring tokens with a static auxiliary objective. Experiments show ATLAS achieves superior performance on visual reasoning benchmarks while maintaining interpretability and efficiency.
为解决直接图像生成计算成本高、代理式或潜在式推理方法各自局限的问题，ATLAS提出一种单一离散“功能词”，兼具代理操作与潜在视觉推理单元双重角色。该词无需视觉监督，通过标准下一词预测生成，兼容传统SFT与RL训练流程，避免中间视觉内容生成。为缓解强化学习中功能词稀疏导致的训练不稳定，ATLAS引入Latent-Anchored GRPO，通过静态加权辅助目标锚定功能词，增强梯度更新。实验表明，ATLAS在多个视觉推理基准上表现最优，同时保持可解释性与架构简洁性。
RefDecoder: Enhancing Visual Generation with Conditional Video Decoding
Authors: Xiang Fan, Yuheng Wang, Bohan Fang, Zhongzheng Ren, Ranjay Krishna
First: 2026-05-14T17:59:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T17:59:52+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Video generation powers a vast array of downstream applications. However, while the de facto standard, i.e., latent diffusion models, typically employ heavily conditioned denoising networks, their decoders often remain unconditional. We observe that this architectural asymmetry leads to significant loss of detail and inconsistency relative to the input image. To address this, we argue that the decoder requires equal conditioning to preserve structural integrity. We introduce RefDecoder, a reference-conditioned video VAE decoder by injecting high-fidelity reference image signal directly into the decoding process via reference attention. Specifically, a lightweight image encoder maps the reference frame into the detail-rich high-dimensional tokens, which are co-processed with the denoised video latent tokens at each decoder up-sampling stage. We demonstrate consistent improvements across several distinct decoder backbones (e.g., Wan 2.1 and VideoVAE+), achieving up to +2.1dB PSNR over the unconditional baselines on the Inter4K, WebVid, and Large Motion reconstruction benchmarks. Notably, RefDecoder can be directly swapped into existing video generation systems without additional fine-tuning, and we report across-the-board improvements in subject consistency, background consistency, and overall quality scores on the VBench I2V benchmark. Beyond I2V, RefDecoder generalizes well to a wide range of visual generation tasks such as style transfer and video editing refinement.
中文标题/摘要
标题：RefDecoder：通过条件视频解码增强视觉生成
视频生成驱动了众多下游应用。然而，尽管当前主流方法（即潜扩散模型）通常采用高度条件化的去噪网络，其解码器往往无条件。我们观察到，这种架构不对称导致与输入图像相比出现显著细节丢失和不一致性。为解决此问题，我们认为解码器需要同等条件化以保持结构完整性。我们提出RefDecoder，一种通过参考注意力直接将高保真参考图像信号注入解码过程的参考条件化视频VAE解码器。具体而言，轻量级图像编码器将参考帧映射为富含细节的高维令牌，这些令牌在解码器每个上采样阶段与去噪视频潜码令牌共同处理。我们在多个不同解码器骨干网络（如Wan 2.1和VideoVAE+）上均实现一致改进，在Inter4K、WebVid和Large Motion重建基准测试中较无条件基线提升最高达+2.1dB PSNR。值得注意的是，RefDecoder可直接替换现有视频生成系统而无需额外微调，并在VBench I2V基准测试中全面提升了主体一致性、背景一致性和整体质量评分。除I2V外，RefDecoder在风格迁移和视频编辑优化等广泛视觉生成任务中也表现出良好泛化能力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the architectural asymmetry in latent diffusion models where decoders remain unconditional despite heavily conditioned denoisers, the authors propose RefDecoder to inject reference image signals directly into the video decoder via reference attention. This method uses a lightweight image encoder to map reference frames into high-dimensional tokens, which are fused with denoised video latents at each up-sampling stage. Experimental results show consistent gains across multiple decoder backbones, achieving up to +2.1dB PSNR on Inter4K, WebVid, and Large Motion benchmarks, and improved subject/background consistency and overall quality on VBench I2V. RefDecoder is also shown to generalize to style transfer and video editing refinement without requiring fine-tuning.
鉴于当前视频生成模型中解码器缺乏条件输入导致细节丢失与不一致，作者提出RefDecoder方法，通过参考注意力机制将高保真参考图像信号直接注入解码过程。该方法利用轻量图像编码器将参考帧映射为高维令牌，并与去噪视频潜在令牌在每个上采样阶段协同处理。实验表明，该方法在多个解码器架构上均取得稳定提升，最高PSNR提升达2.1dB，并在VBench I2V基准上显著改善主体与背景一致性，且无需额外微调即可直接替换现有系统。
VGGT-$Ω$
Authors: Jianyuan Wang, Minghao Chen, Shangzhan Zhang, Nikita Karaev, Johannes Schönberger, Patrick Labatut, Piotr Bojanowski, David Novotny, Andrea Vedaldi, Christian Rupprecht
Venue: CVPR 2026 Oral
First: 2026-05-14T17:59:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T17:59:51+00:00
Comments: CVPR 2026 (Oral)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Recent feed-forward reconstruction models, such as VGGT, have proven competitive with traditional optimization-based reconstructors while also providing geometry-aware features useful for other tasks. Here, we show that the quality of these models scales predictably with model and data size. We do so by introducing VGGT-$Ω$, which substantially improves reconstruction accuracy, efficiency, and capabilities for both static and dynamic scenes. To enable training this model at an unprecedented scale, we introduce architectural changes that improve training efficiency, a high-quality data annotation pipeline that supports dynamic scenes, and a self-supervised learning protocol. We simplify VGGT's architecture by using a single dense prediction head with multi-task supervision and removing the expensive high-resolution convolutional layers. We also use registers to aggregate scene information into a compact representation and introduce register attention, which restricts inter-frame information exchange to these registers, in part replacing global attention. In this way, during training, VGGT-$Ω$ uses only about 30% of the GPU memory of its predecessor, allowing us to train with 15x more supervised data than prior work and to leverage vast amounts of unlabeled video data. VGGT-$Ω$ achieves strong results for reconstruction of static and dynamic scenes across multiple benchmarks, for example, improving over the previous best camera estimation accuracy on Sintel by 77%. We also show that the learned registers can improve vision-language-action models and support alignment with language, suggesting that reconstruction can be a powerful and scalable proxy task for spatial understanding. Project Page: http://vggt-omega.github.io/
中文标题/摘要
标题：VGGT-$Ω$
近期的前馈重建模型（如VGGT）在与传统优化型重建器竞争的同时，还提供了对几何结构敏感的特征，适用于其他任务。本文表明，这些模型的质量可随模型规模和数据规模可预测地提升。我们提出了VGGT-$Ω$，显著提升了静态与动态场景的重建精度、效率和能力。为支持在前所未有的规模下训练该模型，我们引入了提升训练效率的架构改进、支持动态场景的高质量数据标注流程，以及自监督学习协议。我们简化了VGGT架构，采用单一密集预测头并结合多任务监督，移除了昂贵的高分辨率卷积层。同时，我们引入寄存器以聚合场景信息为紧凑表示，并提出寄存器注意力机制，限制帧间信息交换仅在寄存器内进行，部分替代全局注意力。这样，训练时VGGT-$Ω$仅使用前代模型约30%的GPU内存，使我们能够使用比以往工作多15倍的监督数据进行训练，并充分利用大量无标签视频数据。VGGT-$Ω$在多个基准测试中实现了静态与动态场景重建的优异表现，例如在Sintel上将相机估计精度提升77%。我们还表明，所学习的寄存器可提升视觉-语言-动作模型，并支持与语言对齐，表明重建可作为空间理解的强大且可扩展的代理任务。项目页面：http://vggt-omega.github.io/
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to enhance reconstruction quality and scalability of feed-forward models like VGGT, this work introduces VGGT-$Ω$, a refined architecture that improves accuracy, efficiency, and dynamic scene handling. The method simplifies VGGT by replacing high-resolution convolutions with a single dense prediction head and multi-task supervision, while introducing registers to compactly represent scene information and register attention to limit inter-frame communication. These changes enable training with 15x more supervised data and vast unlabeled video, using only 30% of the GPU memory of the original model. Experimentally, VGGT-$Ω$ outperforms prior methods on static and dynamic scene reconstruction, achieving a 77% improvement in camera estimation accuracy on Sintel, and demonstrates transferability to vision-language-action models via learned registers.
为提升重建性能并降低计算开销，VGGT-$Ω$通过架构简化与新型训练协议实现规模扩展。它用单个密集预测头替代高分辨率卷积，引入基于寄存器的注意力机制压缩场景信息，限制帧间交互仅通过寄存器进行，使GPU内存占用减少70%，从而支持15倍于前作的监督数据训练并利用海量无标注视频。实验表明：在Sintel上相机估计精度提升77%，在静态与动态场景多个基准上表现优异，且所学寄存器可提升视觉-语言-动作模型性能，表明重建可作为空间理解的高效代理任务。
Aligning Latent Geometry for Spherical Flow Matching in Image Generation
Authors: Tuna Han Salih Meral, Kaan Oktay, Hidir Yesiltepe, Adil Kaan Akan, Pinar Yanardag
First: 2026-05-14T17:59:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T17:59:37+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Latent flow matching for image generation usually transports Gaussian noise to variational autoencoder latents along linear paths. Both endpoints, however, concentrate in thin spherical shells, and a Euclidean chord leaves those shells even when preprocessing aligns their radii. By decomposing each latent token into radial and angular components, we show through component-swap probes that decoded perceptual and semantic content is carried predominantly by direction, with radius contributing much less. We therefore project data latents onto a fixed token radius, use the radial projection of Gaussian noise as the spherical prior, finetune the decoder with the encoder frozen, and replace linear interpolation with spherical linear interpolation. The resulting geodesic paths stay on the sphere at every timestep, and their velocity targets are purely angular by construction. Under matched training, the method consistently improves class-conditional ImageNet-256 FID across different image tokenizers, leaves the diffusion architecture unchanged, and requires no auxiliary encoder or representation-alignment objective.
中文标题/摘要
标题：图像生成中球面流匹配的潜在几何对齐
图像生成中的潜在流匹配通常沿线性路径将高斯噪声传输至变分自编码器的潜在空间。然而，两个端点集中于薄球壳内，即使预处理对齐了半径，欧氏弦仍会离开这些球壳。通过将每个潜在标记分解为径向和角向分量，并通过分量交换实验表明，解码后的感知与语义内容主要由方向承载，半径贡献较小。因此，我们将数据潜在空间投影到固定半径，使用高斯噪声的径向投影作为球面先验，冻结编码器微调解码器，并用球面线性插值替代线性插值。所得测地线路径在每一步均保持在球面上，且其速度目标由构造决定为纯角向。在匹配训练下，该方法在不同图像标记器上持续提升类条件ImageNet-256 FID，不改变扩散架构，且无需辅助编码器或表示对齐目标。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that linear interpolation in latent flow matching fails to stay within spherical shells despite radius alignment, the authors decompose latent tokens into radial and angular components and find that perceptual content is primarily encoded in direction. They fix the radius of data latents, use radial Gaussian noise as a spherical prior, freeze the encoder, and replace linear interpolation with spherical linear interpolation to enforce geodesic paths. This approach consistently improves class-conditional ImageNet-256 FID across multiple tokenizers without altering the diffusion architecture or requiring auxiliary components.
鉴于线性插值在潜在流匹配中即使在半径对齐后仍会导致偏离球壳，作者将潜在令牌分解为径向和角向分量，发现感知内容主要由方向承载。他们固定数据潜在的半径，以径向高斯噪声作为球形先验，冻结编码器，并用球面插值替代线性插值以确保测地线路径。该方法在不同令牌化器上持续提升类条件ImageNet-256 FID，无需修改扩散架构或额外组件。
MindVLA-U1: VLA Beats VA with Unified Streaming Architecture for Autonomous Driving
Authors: Yuzhou Huang, Benjin Zhu, Hengtong Lu, Victor Shea-Jay Huang, Haiming Zhang, Wei Chen, Jifeng Dai, Yan Xie, Hongsheng Li
First: 2026-05-12T18:09:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T17:59:31+00:00
Comments: Work in progress. Project page: https://mind-omni.github.io/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Autonomous driving has progressed from modular pipelines toward end-to-end unification, and Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are a natural extension of this journey beyond Vision-to-Action (VA). In practice, driving VLAs have often trailed VA on planning quality, suggesting that the difficulty is not simply model scale but the interface through which semantic reasoning, temporal context, and continuous control are combined. We argue that this gap reflects how VLA has been built -- as isolated subtask improvements that fail to compose coherent driving capabilities -- rather than what VLA is. We present MindVLA-U1, the first unified streaming VLA architecture for autonomous driving. A unified VLM backbone produces AR language tokens (optional) and flow-matching continuous action trajectories in a single forward pass over one shared representation, preserving the natural output form of each modality. A full streaming design processes the driving video framewise rather than as fixed video-action chunks under costly temporal VLM modeling. Planned trajectories evolve smoothly across frames while a learned streaming memory channel carries temporal context and updates. The unified architecture enables fast/slow systems on dense & sparse MoT backbones via flexible self-attention context management, and exposes a measurable language-control path for action: language-predicted driving intents steers the action diffusion via classifier-free guidance (CFG), turning language-side intent into control signals for continuous action planning. On the long-tail WOD-E2E benchmark, MindVLA-U1 surpasses experienced human drivers for the first time (8.20 RFS vs. 8.13 GT RFS) with 2 diffusion steps, achieves state-of-the-art planning ADEs over prior VA/VLA by large margins, and matches VA latency (16 FPS vs. RAP's 18 FPS at 1B scale) while preserving natural language interfaces for human-vehicle interaction.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MindVLA-U1：统一流式架构的VLA在自动驾驶中超越VA
自动驾驶正从模块化管道向端到端统一演进，视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型是这一进程在视觉到动作（VA）之外的自然延伸。实践中，驾驶VLA往往在规划质量上落后于VA，表明问题并非单纯模型规模，而是语义推理、时序上下文与连续控制结合的接口。我们认为，这一差距源于VLA的构建方式——作为孤立子任务改进，未能组合出连贯的驾驶能力，而非VLA的本质。我们提出MindVLA-U1，这是首个用于自动驾驶的统一流式VLA架构。统一的VLM主干网络在单次前向传播中，通过共享表征生成AR语言标记（可选）和流匹配连续动作轨迹，保留各模态的自然输出形式。完整的流式设计逐帧处理驾驶视频，而非在昂贵的时序VLM建模下以固定视频-动作块处理。规划轨迹在帧间平滑演化，而学习到的流式记忆通道承载时序上下文并持续更新。统一架构通过灵活的自注意力上下文管理，支持在密集与稀疏MoT主干网络上实现快速/慢速系统，并暴露可测量的语言控制路径：语言预测的驾驶意图通过无分类器引导（CFG）引导动作扩散，将语言侧意图转化为连续动作规划的控制信号。在长尾WOD-E2E基准测试中，MindVLA-U1首次超越经验人类驾驶员（8.20 RFS vs. 8.13 GT RFS，仅需2步扩散），大幅超越此前VA/VLA的规划ADE，同时匹配VA延迟（16 FPS vs. RAP在1B规模下的18 FPS），并保留自然语言接口以支持人车交互。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models often underperform Vision-to-Action (VA) models in autonomous driving despite their theoretical advantages, the authors propose MindVLA-U1, a unified streaming architecture that integrates semantic reasoning, temporal context, and continuous control within a single forward pass. The model uses a shared VLM backbone to generate both language tokens and continuous action trajectories simultaneously, enabling smooth trajectory evolution and efficient frame-wise processing without costly temporal modeling. A streaming memory channel maintains context across frames, and language-driven intent is translated into action via classifier-free guidance, allowing natural language to steer continuous planning. Experiments on the WOD-E2E benchmark show MindVLA-U1 outperforms human drivers (8.20 RFS vs. 8.13 GT RFS), achieves state-of-the-art planning accuracy with lower ADEs, and matches VA latency (16 FPS) while preserving language interfaces.
鉴于Vision-Language-Action（VLA）模型在自动驾驶规划质量上常落后于Vision-to-Action（VA）模型，作者提出MindVLA-U1，一种统一的流式架构，将语义推理、时序上下文与连续控制整合于单次前向传播中。模型通过共享视觉语言模型（VLM）骨干网络同时生成语言标记与连续动作轨迹，并采用帧级流式处理避免昂贵的时序建模。学习的内存通道维持时序上下文，语言意图通过分类器自由引导转化为动作控制信号，支持自然的人车交互。在WOD-E2E基准测试中，MindVLA-U1首次超越人类驾驶员（8.20 RFS vs. 8.13），规划精度显著优于现有VA/VLA模型，同时保持与VA相当的帧率（16 FPS），并保留语言接口。
RAVEN: Real-time Autoregressive Video Extrapolation with Consistency-model GRPO
Authors: Yanzuo Lu, Ronglai Zuo, Jiankang Deng
First: 2026-05-14T17:59:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T17:59:30+00:00
Comments: Project Page: https://yanzuo.lu/raven
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Causal autoregressive video diffusion models support real-time streaming generation by extrapolating future chunks from previously generated content. Distilling such generators from high-fidelity bidirectional teachers yields competitive few-step models, yet a persistent gap between the history distributions encountered during training and those arising at inference constrains generation quality over long horizons. We introduce the Real-time Autoregressive Video Extrapolation Network (RAVEN), a training-time test framework that repacks each self rollout into an interleaved sequence of clean historical endpoints and noisy denoising states. This formulation aligns training attention with inference-time extrapolation and allows downstream chunk losses to supervise the history representations on which future predictions depend. We further propose Consistency-model Group Relative Policy Optimization (CM-GRPO), which reformulates a consistency sampling step as a conditional Gaussian transition and applies online Reinforcement Learning (RL) directly to this kernel, avoiding the Euler-Maruyama auxiliary process adopted in prior flow-model RL formulations. Experiments demonstrate that RAVEN surpasses recent causal video distillation baselines across quality, semantic, and dynamic degree evaluations, and that CM-GRPO provides further gains when combined with RAVEN.
中文标题/摘要
标题：RAVEN：基于一致性模型GRPO的实时自回归视频外推
因果自回归视频扩散模型通过从先前生成的内容外推未来片段，支持实时流式生成。从高保真双向教师模型中蒸馏此类生成器可获得具有竞争力的少量步骤模型，但训练时遇到的历史分布与推理时出现的分布之间存在持续差距，限制了长时程生成质量。我们引入实时自回归视频外推网络（RAVEN），这是一种训练时测试框架，将每个自我展开重打包为交错序列的干净历史端点与噪声去噪状态。该形式使训练注意力与推理时外推对齐，并允许下游片段损失监督未来预测所依赖的历史表示。我们进一步提出一致性模型组相对策略优化（CM-GRPO），将一致性采样步骤重新表述为条件高斯转移，并直接将其应用于在线强化学习，避免了先前流模型强化学习公式中采用的欧拉-马鲁亚辅助过程。实验表明，RAVEN在质量、语义和动态度评估中均优于近期因果视频蒸馏基线，且与RAVEN结合时，CM-GRPO可进一步提升性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of maintaining generation quality over long horizons in real-time video extrapolation, the authors propose RAVEN, a training-time test framework that interleaves clean historical endpoints with noisy denoising states to align training attention with inference-time extrapolation. This enables downstream chunk losses to supervise history representations critical for future predictions. They further introduce CM-GRPO, a reinforcement learning method that reformulates consistency sampling as a conditional Gaussian transition, eliminating the need for Euler-Maruyama auxiliary processes. Experiments show RAVEN outperforms recent causal video distillation baselines in quality, semantic fidelity, and dynamic consistency, with CM-GRPO providing additional gains when combined.
为解决实时视频外推中长时程生成质量受限的问题，作者提出RAVEN框架，通过将干净历史端点与噪声去噪状态交错排列，使训练注意力与推理外推对齐，并让下游块损失监督未来预测所依赖的历史表示。同时提出CM-GRPO方法，将一致性采样重写为条件高斯转移，直接对转移核进行在线强化学习，避免传统流模型RL中使用的Euler-Maruyama辅助过程。实验表明，RAVEN在质量、语义和动态度评估中超越近期因果视频蒸馏基线，且与CM-GRPO结合可进一步提升性能。
FutureSim: Replaying World Events to Evaluate Adaptive Agents
Authors: Shashwat Goel, Nikhil Chandak, Arvindh Arun, Ameya Prabhu, Steffen Staab, Moritz Hardt, Maksym Andriushchenko, Jonas Geiping
First: 2026-05-14T17:59:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T17:59:28+00:00
Comments: 31 pages, 10 main
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
AI agents are being increasingly deployed in dynamic, open-ended environments that require adapting to new information as it arrives. To efficiently measure this capability for realistic use-cases, we propose building grounded simulations that replay real-world events in the order they occurred. We build FutureSim, where agents forecast world events beyond their knowledge cutoff while interacting with a chronological replay of the world: real news articles arriving and questions resolving over the simulated period. We evaluate frontier agents in their native harness, testing their ability to predict world events over a three-month period from January to March 2026. FutureSim reveals a clear separation in their capabilities, with the best agent's accuracy being 25%, and many having worse Brier skill score than making no prediction at all. Through careful ablations, we show how FutureSim offers a realistic setting to study emerging research directions like long-horizon test-time adaptation, search, memory, and reasoning about uncertainty. Overall, we hope our benchmark design paves the way to measure AI progress on open-ended adaptation spanning long time-horizons in the real world.
中文标题/摘要
标题：FutureSim：通过重放世界事件评估自适应智能体
AI智能体正越来越多地部署在动态、开放的环境中，需要根据新信息实时调整。为高效衡量其在现实场景中的适应能力，我们提出构建基于真实事件的模拟环境，按实际发生顺序重放事件。我们构建了FutureSim，其中智能体需在知识截止日期之后预测世界事件，同时与按时间顺序重放的世界互动：真实新闻文章陆续到达，问题在模拟期间逐步解决。我们评估前沿智能体在原生框架下的表现，测试其在2026年1月至3月三个月期间预测世界事件的能力。FutureSim揭示了智能体能力的明显差异，最佳智能体准确率仅为25%，许多智能体的Brier技能得分甚至低于不做预测。通过细致的消融实验，我们证明FutureSim为研究长期测试时适应、搜索、记忆及不确定性推理等新兴研究方向提供了现实环境。总体而言，我们希望该基准设计能推动衡量AI在现实世界中长期开放适应能力的进步。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate AI agents' ability to adapt to real-world events over long time horizons, the authors introduce FutureSim, a grounded simulation that replays real-world news events chronologically to test agents' forecasting and adaptation skills. Agents interact with a stream of real news articles and must answer questions as events unfold over a three-month simulated period from January to March 2026. Experimental results show a stark performance gap, with the top-performing agent achieving only 25% accuracy and many agents performing worse than random guessing, as measured by Brier skill score. Ablation studies confirm FutureSim’s utility for studying long-horizon adaptation, search, memory, and uncertainty reasoning, highlighting its potential as a benchmark for real-world open-ended adaptation.
为评估AI代理在长时程动态环境中适应现实事件的能力，作者提出了FutureSim，一个按真实时间顺序回放新闻事件的接地仿真环境，用于测试代理的预测与适应能力。代理需在2026年1月至3月的三月窗口内，根据实时新闻流预测未来事件。实验结果显示性能差距显著，最优代理准确率仅为25%，多数代理表现劣于随机预测（Brier技能得分更低）。消融实验验证了FutureSim在研究长时程适应、搜索、记忆及不确定性推理方面的有效性，有望成为衡量现实世界开放环境适应能力的基准工具。
Articraft: An Agentic System for Scalable Articulated 3D Asset Generation
Authors: Matt Zhou, Ruining Li, Xiaoyang Lyu, Zhaomou Song, Zhening Huang, Chuanxia Zheng, Christian Rupprecht, Andrea Vedaldi, Shangzhe Wu
First: 2026-05-14T17:59:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T17:59:18+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://articraft3d.github.io/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
A bottleneck in learning to understand articulated 3D objects is the lack of large and diverse datasets. In this paper, we propose to leverage large language models (LLMs) to close this gap and generate articulated assets at scale. We reduce the problem of generating an articulated 3D asset to that of writing a program that builds it. We then introduce a new agentic system, Articraft, that writes such programs automatically. We design a programmatic interface and harness to help the LLM do so effectively. The LLM writes code against a domain-specific SDK for defining parts, composing geometry, specifying joints, and writing tests to validate the resulting assets. The harness exposes a restricted workspace and interface to the LLM, validates the resulting assets, and returns structured feedback. In this way, the LLM is not distracted by details such as authoring a URDF file or managing a complex software environment. We show that this produces higher-quality assets than both state-of-the-art articulated-asset generators and general-purpose coding agents. Using Articraft, we build Articraft-10K, a curated dataset of over 10K articulated assets spanning 245 categories, and show its utility both for training models of articulated assets and in downstream applications such as robotics simulation and virtual reality.

Experimental results show Articraft produces higher-quality assets than existing state-of-the-art generators and general coding agents, and it enables the creation of Articraft-10K — a curated dataset of over 10,000 assets across 245 categories — which improves performance in downstream tasks like robotics simulation and virtual reality.

VGGT-Edit: Feed-forward Native 3D Scene Editing with Residual Field Prediction
Authors: Kaixin Zhu, Yiwen Tang, Yifan Yang, Renrui Zhang, Bohan Zeng, Ziyu Guo, Ruichuan An, Zhou Liu, Qizhi Chen, Delin Qu, Jaehong Yoon, Wentao Zhang
First: 2026-05-14T17:59:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T17:59:04+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
High-quality 3D scene reconstruction has recently advanced toward generalizable feed-forward architectures, enabling the generation of complex environments in a single forward pass. However, despite their strong performance in static scene perception, these models remain limited in responding to dynamic human instructions, which restricts their use in interactive applications. Existing editing methods typically rely on a 2D-lifting strategy, where individual views are edited independently and then lifted back into 3D space. This indirect pipeline often leads to blurry textures and inconsistent geometry, as 2D editors lack the spatial awareness required to preserve structure across viewpoints. To address these limitations, we propose VGGT-Edit, a feed-forward framework for text-conditioned native 3D scene editing. VGGT-Edit introduces depth-synchronized text injection to align semantic guidance with the backbone's spatial poses, ensuring stable instruction grounding. This semantic signal is then processed by a residual transformation head, which directly predicts 3D geometric displacements to deform the scene while preserving background stability. To ensure high-fidelity results, we supervise the framework with a multi-term objective function that enforces geometric accuracy and cross-view consistency. We also construct the DeltaScene Dataset, a large-scale dataset generated through an automated pipeline with 3D agreement filtering to ensure ground-truth quality. Experiments show that VGGT-Edit substantially outperforms 2D-lifting baselines, producing sharper object details, stronger multi-view consistency, and near-instant inference speed.
中文标题/摘要
标题：VGGT-Edit：基于残差场预测的前馈原生3D场景编辑
高质量3D场景重建近期已向通用前馈架构发展，可在单次前向传递中生成复杂环境。然而，尽管这些模型在静态场景感知方面表现优异，仍受限于对动态人类指令的响应能力，限制了其在交互式应用中的使用。现有编辑方法通常依赖2D提升策略，即独立编辑各视角后再提升至3D空间，该间接流程常导致纹理模糊和几何不一致，因2D编辑器缺乏跨视角结构保持所需的空间感知能力。为解决此问题，我们提出VGGT-Edit，一种基于文本条件的前馈原生3D场景编辑框架。VGGT-Edit引入深度同步文本注入，将语义引导与主干网络的空间姿态对齐，确保指令稳定锚定。该语义信号随后由残差变换头处理，直接预测3D几何位移以变形场景并保持背景稳定。为确保高保真结果，我们采用多目标函数监督框架，强制几何精度与跨视角一致性。同时构建DeltaScene数据集，通过自动化流水线生成并经3D一致性过滤确保真实标签质量。实验表明，VGGT-Edit显著优于2D提升基线方法，生成更锐利的物体细节、更强的多视角一致性及近实时推理速度。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for dynamic, interactive 3D scene editing beyond static reconstruction, VGGT-Edit introduces a feed-forward framework that directly edits 3D scenes using text prompts without 2D lifting. It achieves this by synchronizing depth-aware text injection with the backbone’s spatial representation and applying a residual transformation head to predict geometric displacements, preserving background stability. Evaluated on a newly constructed DeltaScene Dataset with ground-truth 3D alignment, VGGT-Edit demonstrates superior fidelity, multi-view consistency, and inference speed compared to 2D-lifting baselines, effectively resolving texture blurring and geometric inconsistency issues.
为实现对动态人类指令的响应式3D场景编辑，VGGT-Edit提出一种无需2D升维的端到端框架，通过深度同步的文本注入将语义指导与空间表示对齐，并利用残差变换头直接预测几何位移以保持背景稳定。在新构建的DeltaScene数据集上，该方法显著优于传统2D升维基线，生成更清晰的物体细节、更强的多视角一致性，并具备接近即时的推理速度，有效避免了纹理模糊与结构不一致等常见问题。
Quantitative Video World Model Evaluation for Geometric-Consistency
Authors: Jiaxin Wu, Yihao Pi, Yinling Zhang, Yuheng Li, Xueyan Zou
First: 2026-05-14T17:59:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T17:59:04+00:00
Comments: 12 pages, 5 figures. Project page : https://pdi-bench.github.io/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Generative video models are increasingly studied as implicit world models, yet evaluating whether they produce physically plausible 3D structure and motion remains challenging. Most existing video evaluation pipelines rely heavily on human judgment or learned graders, which can be subjective and weakly diagnostic for geometric failures. We introduce PDI-Bench (Perspective Distortion Index), a quantitative framework for auditing geometric coherence in generated videos. Given a generated clip, we obtain object-centric observations via segmentation and point tracking (e.g., SAM 2, MegaSaM, and CoTracker3), lift them to 3D world-space coordinates via monocular reconstruction, and compute a set of projective-geometry residuals capturing three failure dimensions: scale-depth alignment, 3D motion consistency, and 3D structural rigidity. To support systematic evaluation, we build PDI-Dataset, covering diverse scenarios designed to stress these geometric constraints. Across state-of-the-art video generators, PDI reveals consistent geometry-specific failure modes that are not captured by common perceptual metrics, and provides a diagnostic signal for progress toward physically grounded video generation and physical world model. Our code and dataset can be found at https://pdi-bench.github.io/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于几何一致性评估的生成视频世界模型定量评价
生成式视频模型正日益被视为隐式世界模型，但评估其是否生成物理上合理的三维结构与运动仍具挑战性。现有大多数视频评估流程依赖人工判断或学习到的评分器，主观性强且对几何错误诊断能力弱。我们提出PDI-Bench（透视畸变指数），一种用于审计生成视频几何一致性的定量框架。给定生成片段，我们通过分割与点追踪（如SAM 2、MegaSaM和CoTracker3）获得以物体为中心的观测，经单目重建提升至三维世界坐标，并计算一组射影几何残差，捕捉三种失败维度：尺度-深度对齐、三维运动一致性及三维结构刚性。为支持系统性评估，我们构建了PDI-Dataset，涵盖多种旨在施加这些几何约束的场景。在当前最先进的视频生成器中，PDI揭示了常见感知度量无法捕捉的几何特异性失败模式，并为物理基础视频生成及物理世界模型的发展提供诊断信号。代码与数据集见https://pdi-bench.github.io/。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for objective evaluation of geometric consistency in generative video models, this work introduces PDI-Bench, a quantitative framework that audits 3D structure and motion in generated videos using projective-geometry residuals. The method extracts object-centric observations via segmentation and point tracking, lifts them to 3D world-space coordinates using monocular reconstruction, and computes residuals across three failure dimensions: scale-depth alignment, 3D motion consistency, and 3D structural rigidity. Evaluated across state-of-the-art video generators, PDI-Bench reveals consistent geometric failure modes not detected by perceptual metrics, offering a diagnostic tool for progress toward physically grounded video generation.
为解决生成视频模型几何一致性缺乏客观评估的问题，本文提出PDI-Bench框架，通过射影几何残差量化物理合理性。该方法利用分割与点追踪提取对象中心观测，通过单目重建映射至3D空间，并计算三个几何维度的残差：尺度-深度对齐、3D运动一致性与3D结构刚性。在多个前沿视频生成器上评估，PDI-Bench揭示了感知指标无法捕捉的几何失效模式，为推动物理世界建模的视频生成提供诊断依据。
Action Emergence from Streaming Intent
Authors: Pengfei Jing, Victor Shea-Jay Huang, Hengtong Lu, Jifeng Dai, Yan Xie, Benjin Zhu
First: 2026-05-12T18:09:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T17:59:01+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://mind-omni.github.io/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
We formalize action emergence as a target capability for end-to-end autonomous driving: the ability to generate physically feasible, semantically appropriate, and safety-compliant actions in arbitrary, long-tail traffic scenes through scene-conditioned reasoning rather than retrieval or interpolation of learned scene-action mappings. We show that previous paradigms cannot deliver action emergence: autoregressive trajectory decoders collapse the inherently multimodal future into a single averaged output, while diffusion and flow-matching generators express multimodality but are not steerable by reasoned intent. We propose Streaming Intent as a concrete way to approach action emergence: a mechanism that makes driving intent (i) semantically streamed through a continuous chain-of-thought that causally derives the intent from scene understanding, and (ii) temporally streamed across clips so that intent commitments remain coherent along the driving horizon. We realize Streaming Intent in a VLA model we call SI (Streaming Intent). SI autoregressively decodes a four-step chain-of-thought and emits an intent token; the decoded intent then drives classifier-free guidance (CFG) on a flow-matching action head, requiring only two denoising steps to generate the final trajectory. On the Waymo End-to-End benchmark, SI achieves competitive aggregate performance, with an RFS score of 7.96 on the validation set and 7.74 on the test set. Beyond aggregate metrics, the model demonstrates -- to our knowledge for the first time in a fully end-to-end VLA -- intent-faithful controllability: for a fixed scene, varying the intent class at inference yields qualitatively distinct yet consistently high-quality plans, arising purely from data-driven learning without any pre-built trajectory bank or hand-coded post-hoc selector.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从流式意图中涌现动作
我们将动作涌现形式化为端到端自动驾驶的目标能力：即通过场景条件推理而非检索或插值已学习的场景-动作映射，在任意长尾交通场景中生成物理可行、语义恰当且安全合规的动作。我们证明先前范式无法实现动作涌现：自回归轨迹解码器将固有的多模态未来压缩为单一平均输出，而扩散与流匹配生成器虽表达多模态，却无法由推理意图引导。我们提出流式意图（Streaming Intent）作为实现动作涌现的具体方法：一种机制，使驾驶意图（i）通过连续思维链语义流式传递，从场景理解中因果推导意图；（ii）在时间上跨片段流式传递，确保意图承诺在驾驶时域内保持连贯。我们在名为SI（流式意图）的VLA模型中实现流式意图：SI自回归解码四步思维链并输出意图标记；解码后的意图驱动无分类器引导（CFG）作用于流匹配动作头，仅需两步去噪即可生成最终轨迹。在Waymo端到端基准测试中，SI在验证集和测试集上分别取得7.96和7.74的RFS分数，表现具有竞争力。除综合指标外，该模型首次在完全端到端VLA中展现出意图忠实可控性：对固定场景，推理时改变意图类别可生成定性不同但质量始终高的一致计划，纯由数据驱动学习实现，无需预建轨迹库或人工编写后处理选择器。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for autonomous driving systems to generate novel, contextually appropriate, and safe actions in unseen traffic scenarios without relying on pre-stored or interpolated mappings, the paper introduces 'Streaming Intent' as a method to enable action emergence. This approach uses a VLA model called SI that first decodes a four-step chain-of-thought to derive a semantic intent token, then uses classifier-free guidance on a flow-matching action head to generate trajectories in just two denoising steps. Experiments on the Waymo End-to-End benchmark show SI achieves competitive aggregate performance with RFS scores of 7.96 and 7.74 on validation and test sets respectively. Crucially, SI demonstrates intent-faithful controllability: for the same scene, changing the intent class at inference produces qualitatively different yet high-quality trajectories, all learned from data without any pre-built trajectory bank or post-hoc selectors.
为使自动驾驶系统能在未见过的交通场景中生成新颖且语义恰当的动作，本文将‘动作涌现’形式化为通过场景条件推理而非插值生成物理可行且安全合规行为的能力。作者提出‘流式意图’方法，通过连续思维链进行语义推理，并在时间片段间保持意图一致性。该方法在名为SI的VLA模型中实现，通过自回归解码生成意图标记，再引导无分类器指导的流匹配动作头，仅需两步去噪即可生成轨迹。在Waymo端到端基准测试中，SI取得竞争性综合性能（验证集RFS 7.96，测试集7.74），并首次在完全端到端VLA模型中实现意图忠实可控性：固定场景下，仅通过改变意图类别即可生成语义不同但质量一致的轨迹，无需预设轨迹库或人工后处理选择器。
Driving Intents Amplify Planning-Oriented Reinforcement Learning
Authors: Hengtong Lu, Victor Shea-Jay Huang, Chengmin Yang, Pengfei Jing, Jifeng Dai, Yan Xie, Benjin Zhu
First: 2026-05-12T18:10:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T17:58:42+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://mind-omni.github.io/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Continuous-action policies trained on a single demonstrated trajectory per scene suffer from mode collapse: samples cluster around the demonstrated maneuver and the policy cannot represent semantically distinct alternatives. Under preference-based evaluation, this caps best-of-N performance -- even oracle selection cannot recover what the sampling distribution does not contain. We introduce DIAL, a two-stage Driving-Intent-Amplified reinforcement Learning framework for preference-aligned continuous-action driving policies. In the first stage, DIAL conditions the flow-matching action head on a discrete intent label with classifier-free guidance (CFG), which expands the sampling distribution along distinct maneuver modes and breaks single-demonstration mode collapse. In the second stage, DIAL carries this expanded distribution into preference RL through multi-intent GRPO, which spans all intent classes within every preference group and prevents fine-tuning from re-collapsing around the currently preferred mode. Instantiated for end-to-end driving with eight rule-derived intents and evaluated on WOD-E2E: competitive Vision-to-Action (VA) and Vision-Language-Action (VLA) Supervised Finetuning (SFT) baselines plateau below the human-driven demonstration at best-of-128, with the strongest prior (RAP) capping at Rater Feedback Score (RFS) 8.5 even with best-of-64; intent-CFG sampling lifts this ceiling to RFS 9.14 at best-of-128, surpassing both the prior best (RAP 8.5) and the human-driven demonstration (8.13) for the first time; and multi-intent GRPO improves held-out RFS from 7.681 to 8.211, while every single-intent baseline peaks lower and degrades by training end. These results suggest that the bottleneck of preference RL on continuous-action policies trained from demonstrations is not only how to update the policy, but to expand and preserve the sampling distribution being optimized.
中文标题/摘要
标题：驱动意图放大规划导向的强化学习
在单场景仅训练一条演示轨迹的连续动作策略易受模式坍缩影响：采样集中于演示操作，无法表示语义不同的替代方案。在偏好评估下，这限制了N选最优性能——即使oracle选择也无法恢复采样分布中不存在的内容。我们提出DIAL，一种两阶段驱动意图放大的强化学习框架，用于偏好对齐的连续动作驾驶策略。第一阶段，DIAL利用分类器自由引导（CFG）将流匹配动作头条件化于离散意图标签，扩展采样分布至不同操作模式，打破单演示模式坍缩。第二阶段，DIAL通过多意图GRPO将扩展分布引入偏好强化学习，覆盖每组偏好内的所有意图类别，防止微调重新坍缩至当前偏好模式。在端到端驾驶中实例化为八种规则推导意图，评估于WOD-E2E：最强的先验（RAP）在最佳128中RFS仅达8.5；意图-CFG采样将其提升至RFS 9.14，首次超越先验最佳（RAP 8.5）和人类驾驶演示（8.13）；多意图GRPO将保留RFS从7.681提升至8.211，而所有单意图基线峰值更低且训练末期退化。结果表明，基于演示训练的连续动作策略在偏好强化学习中的瓶颈不仅在于策略更新方式，更在于扩展并保持被优化的采样分布。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the mode collapse in continuous-action driving policies trained on single-demonstration trajectories, which limits their ability to represent diverse maneuvers and caps preference-based performance, the paper introduces DIAL, a two-stage framework. The first stage uses classifier-free guidance conditioned on discrete driving intents to expand the action sampling distribution beyond the demonstrated mode. The second stage employs multi-intent GRPO to maintain this expanded distribution during reinforcement learning, preventing re-collapsing around preferred modes. Experiments on WOD-E2E show that intent-CFG sampling lifts the Rater Feedback Score from 8.5 (RAP baseline) to 9.14, surpassing both the prior best and human demonstration, while multi-intent GRPO further improves held-out RFS to 8.211, outperforming all single-intent baselines that degrade over time.
为了解决基于单次演示轨迹训练的连续动作驾驶策略因模式坍缩而无法表示语义不同动作、且偏好评估性能受限的问题，本文提出DIAL框架，包含两个阶段。第一阶段通过意图标签引导的无分类器自由指导扩展动作采样分布，突破单一演示模式限制；第二阶段采用多意图GRPO在强化学习中保持分布多样性，防止训练过程中重新坍缩至当前偏好模式。在WOD-E2E实验中，意图-CFG采样将评分从8.5（最佳前序模型）提升至9.14，首次超越前序最佳模型和人类演示；多意图GRPO进一步将验证集评分提升至8.211，所有单意图基线均表现更差且随训练退化。
Is Grep All You Need? How Agent Harnesses Reshape Agentic Search
Authors: Sahil Sen, Akhil Kasturi, Elias Lumer, Anmol Gulati, Vamse Kumar Subbiah
First: 2026-05-14T17:58:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T17:58:41+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent advances in Large Language Model (LLM) agents have enabled complex agentic workflows where models autonomously retrieve information, call tools, and reason over large corpora to complete tasks on behalf of users. Despite the growing adoption of retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) in agentic search systems, existing literature lacks a systematic comparison of how retrieval strategy choice interacts with agent architecture and tool-calling paradigm. Important practical dimensions, including how tool outputs are presented to the model and how performance changes when searches must cope with more irrelevant surrounding text, remain under-explored in agent loops. This paper reports an empirical study organized into two experiments. Experiment 1 compares grep and vector retrieval on a 116-question sample from LongMemEval, using a custom agent harness (Chronos) and provider-native CLI harnesses (Claude Code, Codex, and Gemini CLI), for both inline tool results and file-based tool results that the model reads separately. Experiment 2 compares grep-only and vector-only retrieval while progressively mixing in additional unrelated conversation history, so that each query is embedded in more distracting material alongside the passages that matter. Across Chronos and the provider CLIs, grep generally yields higher accuracy than vector retrieval in our comparisons in experiment 1; at the same time, overall scores still depend strongly on which harness and tool-calling style is used, even when the underlying conversation data are the same.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Grep就够了吗？代理如何重塑自主搜索
大型语言模型（LLM）代理的最新进展使得模型能够自主检索信息、调用工具并在大规模语料库上进行推理，以代表用户完成任务。尽管检索增强生成（RAG）在自主搜索系统中的采用日益增长，但现有文献缺乏对检索策略选择如何与代理架构和工具调用范式相互作用的系统性比较。代理循环中重要的实际维度，如工具输出如何呈现给模型，以及当搜索必须处理更多无关背景文本时性能如何变化，仍被忽视。本文报告了两项实验的实证研究。实验1在LongMemEval的116个问题样本上比较grep与向量检索，使用自定义代理框架（Chronos）和提供商原生CLI框架（Claude Code、Codex和Gemini CLI），分别针对内联工具结果和模型单独读取的文件型工具结果。实验2比较仅使用grep和仅使用向量检索，逐步混入额外无关对话历史，使每个查询嵌入更多干扰材料，同时保留相关段落。在实验1中，Chronos和提供商CLI框架的比较结果显示，grep通常比向量检索准确率更高；但总体得分仍强烈依赖于所使用的框架和工具调用方式，即使底层对话数据相同。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand how retrieval strategy affects agentic search performance, this study compares grep and vector-based retrieval methods using a custom agent harness (Chronos) and provider-native CLI tools across two experiments. In Experiment 1, grep consistently outperformed vector retrieval on a 116-question LongMemEval dataset, whether tool results were presented inline or read separately from files. Experiment 2 introduced increasing irrelevant conversation history to test robustness, and while grep maintained higher accuracy, performance still varied significantly depending on the agent harness and tool-calling paradigm used, even with identical underlying data.
为探究检索策略对代理搜索性能的影响，本研究对比了grep与向量检索方法，使用自研代理框架Chronos及多个厂商CLI工具，在两项实验中进行评估。实验一在116道LongMemEval题目上测试，无论工具结果内联呈现还是单独文件读取，grep均优于向量检索。实验二逐步引入无关对话历史干扰相关段落，grep仍保持更高准确率，但整体表现仍受代理框架和工具调用方式显著影响。结果表明，检索方法选择与代理架构及结果呈现方式高度交互，grep在代理工作流中可能更具鲁棒性。
When Are Two Networks the Same? Tensor Similarity for Mechanistic Interpretability
Authors: ML Nissen Gonzalez, Melwina Albuquerque, Laurence Wroe, Jacob Meyer Cohen, Logan Riggs Smith, Thomas Dooms
First: 2026-05-14T17:58:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T17:58:27+00:00
Comments: 22 pages, 8 figures. Code: https://github.com/tdooms/tensor-similarity
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Mechanistic interpretability aims to break models into meaningful parts; verifying that two such parts implement the same computation is a prerequisite. Existing similarity measures evaluate either empirical behaviour, leaving them blind to out-of-distribution mechanisms, or basis-dependent parameters, meaning they disregard weight-space symmetries. To address these issues for the class of tensor-based models, we introduce a weight-based metric, tensor similarity, that is invariant to such symmetries. This metric captures global functional equivalence and accounts for cross-layer mechanisms using an efficient recursive algorithm. Empirically, tensor similarity tracks functional training dynamics, such as grokking and backdoor insertion, with higher fidelity than existing metrics. This reduces measuring similarity and verifying faithfulness into a solved algebraic problem rather than one of empirical approximation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：何时两个网络是相同的？张量相似性用于机制可解释性
机制可解释性旨在将模型分解为有意义的部分；验证两个此类部分是否实现相同计算是前提。现有相似性度量要么评估经验行为，因而对分布外机制视而不见，要么依赖基向量参数，因而忽略权重空间对称性。为解决张量模型类的这些问题，我们引入一种基于权重的度量——张量相似性，该度量对上述对称性不变。该度量捕捉全局功能等价性，并通过高效递归算法考虑跨层机制。实证上，张量相似性比现有度量更精确地追踪功能训练动态，如grokking和后门注入。这将相似性测量和忠实性验证转化为一个已解决的代数问题，而非经验近似问题。
Summary / 总结
To address the limitations of existing similarity measures that either ignore out-of-distribution mechanisms or depend on basis-specific parameters, this paper introduces tensor similarity, a weight-based metric invariant to weight-space symmetries, specifically designed for tensor-based models. The method leverages an efficient recursive algorithm to capture global functional equivalence and cross-layer mechanisms, enabling precise verification of computational equivalence between model components. Experimental results demonstrate that tensor similarity more faithfully tracks functional dynamics such as grokking and backdoor insertion compared to prior metrics, effectively reducing interpretability verification to an algebraic problem.
为解决机制可解释性中验证功能等价性的难题，本文提出张量相似度——一种基于权重、对权重空间对称性不变的度量方法，克服了现有经验或基依赖度量的局限。该方法通过递归算法高效捕捉跨层机制，实现全局功能等价计算。实验表明，该度量在追踪如grokking和后门注入等训练动态时，比现有方法更精确，将相似性验证转化为代数问题而非经验近似。
Warp-as-History: Generalizable Camera-Controlled Video Generation from One Training Video
Authors: Yifan Wang, Tong He
First: 2026-05-14T17:58:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T17:58:26+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://yyfz.github.io/warp-as-history/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Camera-controlled video generation has made substantial progress, enabling generated videos to follow prescribed viewpoint trajectories. However, existing methods usually learn camera-specific conditioning through camera encoders, control branches, or attention and positional-encoding modifications, which often require post-training on large-scale camera-annotated videos. Training-free alternatives avoid such post-training, but often shift the cost to test-time optimization or extra denoising-time guidance. We propose Warp-as-History, a simple interface that turns camera-induced warps into camera-warped pseudo-history with target-frame positional alignment and visible-token selection. Given a target camera trajectory, we construct camera-warped pseudo-history from past observations and feed it through the model's visual-history pathway. Crucially, we align its positional encoding with the target frames being denoised and remove warped-history tokens without valid source observations. Without any training, architectural modification, or test-time optimization, this interface reveals a non-trivial zero-shot capability of a frozen video generation model to follow camera trajectories. Moreover, lightweight offline LoRA finetuning on only one camera-annotated video further improves this capability and generalizes to unseen videos, improving camera adherence, visual quality, and motion dynamics without test-time optimization or target-video adaptation. Extensive experiments on diverse datasets confirm the effectiveness of our method.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Warp-as-History：从单个训练视频实现通用的相机控制视频生成
相机控制视频生成已取得显著进展，可使生成视频遵循指定视角轨迹。然而，现有方法通常通过相机编码器、控制分支或注意力与位置编码修改学习相机特定条件，往往需在大规模相机标注视频上进行后训练。训练免方案虽避免后训练，但常将成本转至测试时优化或额外去噪引导。我们提出Warp-as-History，一种简单接口，将相机诱导的变形转化为相机变形伪历史，通过目标帧位置对齐与可见令牌选择实现。给定目标相机轨迹，我们从过往观测构建相机变形伪历史，并通过模型视觉历史路径输入。关键在于，我们将其位置编码与待去噪目标帧对齐，并移除无有效源观测的变形历史令牌。无需任何训练、架构修改或测试时优化，此接口揭示了冻结视频生成模型的非平凡零样本能力，可跟随相机轨迹。此外，仅用单个相机标注视频进行轻量级离线LoRA微调，进一步提升该能力并泛化至未见视频，提升相机遵循性、视觉质量和运动动态，无需测试时优化或目标视频适配。在多样化数据集上的大量实验验证了本方法的有效性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for camera-controlled video generation without post-training or test-time optimization, the authors propose Warp-as-History, a method that transforms camera-induced warps into pseudo-historical inputs aligned with target frames. By feeding these warped histories through the model’s visual-history pathway and pruning tokens without valid source observations, the approach enables a frozen video model to follow arbitrary camera trajectories without architectural changes or training. Experiments show it achieves non-trivial zero-shot performance and further improves with lightweight LoRA fine-tuning on a single annotated video, enhancing camera adherence, visual quality, and motion dynamics across unseen videos.
为实现无需微调或测试时优化的相机控制视频生成，作者提出Warp-as-History方法，将相机诱导的形变转化为与目标帧对齐的伪历史序列，并通过可见令牌选择和位置编码对齐输入冻结的视频模型。该方法在不修改架构、无需训练的情况下实现零样本相机轨迹跟随，实验表明其在未见视频上具有良好泛化能力，显著提升相机跟随精度、视觉质量和运动动态，仅需少量离线LoRA微调即可实现。
From Plans to Pixels: Learning to Plan and Orchestrate for Open-Ended Image Editing
Authors: Anirudh Sundara Rajan, Krishna Kumar Singh, Yong Jae Lee
First: 2026-05-14T17:58:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T17:58:19+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Modern image editing models produce realistic results but struggle with abstract, multi step instructions (e.g., ``make this advertisement more vegetarian-friendly''). Prior agent based methods decompose such tasks but rely on handcrafted pipelines or teacher imitation, limiting flexibility and decoupling learning from actual editing outcomes. We propose an experiential framework for long-horizon image editing, where a planner generates structured atomic decompositions and an orchestrator selects tools and regions to execute each step. A vision language judge provides outcome-based rewards for instruction adherence and visual quality. The orchestrator is trained to maximize these rewards, and successful trajectories are used to refine the planner. By tightly coupling planning with reward driven execution, our approach yields more coherent and reliable edits than single-step or rule-based multistep baselines.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从计划到像素：学习规划与编排以实现开放式的图像编辑
现代图像编辑模型能生成逼真的结果，但在处理抽象的多步骤指令（例如“使此广告更符合素食者需求”）时表现不佳。此前基于代理的方法虽可将此类任务分解，但依赖手工设计的流程或教师模仿，限制了灵活性，并使学习与实际编辑结果脱节。我们提出了一种面向长时程图像编辑的经验框架：规划器生成结构化的原子分解，编排器选择工具和区域执行每一步。视觉语言裁判根据指令遵循度和视觉质量提供基于结果的奖励。编排器被训练以最大化这些奖励，成功轨迹用于优化规划器。通过紧密耦合规划与奖励驱动的执行，我们的方法比单步或基于规则的多步基线更连贯、更可靠。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of current image editing models in handling abstract, multi-step instructions, this work introduces an experiential framework that decouples planning from execution. A planner generates structured atomic steps, while an orchestrator selects tools and regions to execute each step, guided by a vision-language judge that rewards instruction adherence and visual quality. The orchestrator is trained via reinforcement learning to maximize these rewards, and successful trajectories are used to refine the planner. Experiments show that this tightly coupled planning-execution approach produces more coherent and reliable edits than single-step or rule-based multi-step baselines.
为了解决现有图像编辑模型难以处理抽象多步骤指令的问题，该研究提出了一种体验式框架，将规划与执行解耦。规划器生成原子化任务步骤，执行器选择工具和区域执行步骤，由视觉-语言评判器根据指令遵循度和视觉质量提供奖励。执行器通过最大化奖励进行训练，成功轨迹用于优化规划器。实验表明，这种紧密耦合的规划-执行系统比单步或基于规则的多步基线方法生成更连贯、更可靠的编辑结果。
Eradicating Negative Transfer in Multi-Physics Foundation Models via Sparse Mixture-of-Experts Routing
Authors: Ellwil Sharma, Arastu Sharma
First: 2026-05-14T17:58:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T17:58:15+00:00
Comments: 5 pages, 4 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Scaling Scientific Machine Learning (SciML) toward universal foundation models is bottlenecked by negative transfer: the simultaneous co-training of disparate partial differential equation (PDE) regimes can induce gradient conflict, unstable optimization, and plasticity loss in dense neural operators. In particular, broadband open-channel fluid dynamics and boundary-dominated porous media flows impose incompatible spectral and geometric demands on a single dense parameter path. We introduce Shodh-MoE, a sparse-activated latent transformer architecture for multi-physics transport. Shodh-MoE operates on compressed 16^3 physical latents produced by a physics-informed autoencoder with an intra-tokenizer Helmholtz-style velocity parameterization, restricting decoded states to divergence-free velocity manifolds. The model guarantees exact mass conservation, achieving a physically verifiable velocity divergence of ~2.8 x 10^-10 (evaluated post-hoc in FP64) on 128^3 grids. A Top-1 soft-semantic router dynamically assigns localized latent patches to expert subnetworks, enabling specialized parameter paths for distinct physical mechanisms while preserving shared experts for universal symmetries. In a 20,000-step distributed pretraining run over mixed three-dimensional physical tensors, routing telemetry shows autonomous domain bifurcation: held-out validation tokens from the open-channel domain route exclusively to Expert 0, while porous-media tokens route exclusively to Expert 1. The model converges simultaneously across both regimes, achieving latent validation MSEs of 2.46 x 10^-5 and 9.76 x 10^-6, and decoded physical MSEs of 2.48 x 10^-6 and 1.76 x 10^-6. These results support sparse expert routing as a practical architectural mechanism for mitigating multi-physics interference in universal neural operators.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过稀疏专家混合路由消除多物理场基础模型中的负迁移
将科学机器学习（SciML）扩展为通用基础模型受负迁移制约：不同偏微分方程（PDE）领域的联合协同训练会引发梯度冲突、优化不稳定及密集神经算子的可塑性损失。特别是宽带开放通道流体动力学与边界主导多孔介质流施加于单一密集参数路径的谱与几何需求相互矛盾。我们提出Shodh-MoE，一种用于多物理场传输的稀疏激活潜在Transformer架构。Shodh-MoE基于物理信息自编码器生成的压缩16^3物理潜在表示运行，该自编码器采用内嵌分词器的亥姆霍兹风格速度参数化，确保解码状态位于无散度速度流形上。模型保证精确质量守恒，在128^3网格上事后评估的物理速度散度约为2.8×10^-10（FP64精度）。Top-1软语义路由器动态分配局部潜在块至专家子网络，使不同物理机制拥有专用参数路径，同时保留共享专家以支持通用对称性。在20,000步分布式预训练中，路由遥测显示自主领域分叉：开放通道域的验证令牌仅路由至专家0，多孔介质令牌仅路由至专家1。模型在两个领域同步收敛，获得潜在验证MSE分别为2.46×10^-5和9.76×10^-6，解码物理MSE分别为2.48×10^-6和1.76×10^-6。这些结果支持稀疏专家路由作为缓解通用神经算子多物理场干扰的实用架构机制。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of negative transfer in multi-physics SciML models, where co-training conflicting PDE regimes causes gradient conflicts and optimization instability, the paper proposes Shodh-MoE, a sparse mixture-of-experts architecture that routes physical latents to specialized expert subnetworks. The model leverages a physics-informed autoencoder to compress physical data into divergence-free velocity manifolds, ensuring mass conservation with near-zero divergence error. A soft-semantic router dynamically assigns latent patches to experts based on physical context, enabling domain-specific parameter paths while preserving shared experts for universal symmetries. Experimental results from a 20,000-step distributed pretraining show autonomous routing: open-channel tokens consistently route to one expert, porous-media tokens to another, with both regimes converging simultaneously and achieving low validation MSEs in both latent and decoded physical spaces.
为解决多物理场科学机器学习模型中因共训练不同PDE体系导致的负迁移问题（如梯度冲突与优化不稳定），本文提出Shodh-MoE架构：一种稀疏激活的专家混合模型，通过软语义路由将物理潜变量动态分配至专用专家子网络。模型利用物理感知自编码器压缩16^3物理状态为无散度速度流形，确保质量守恒，后验评估中速度散度误差低于10^-9。路由机制根据物理语义自动分离领域：开放通道流仅路由至专家0，多孔介质仅路由至专家1。模型在20,000步分布式预训练中同步收敛于两个领域，潜空间与物理解空间验证MSE均值分别低于10^-5和10^-6，验证稀疏专家路由可有效缓解多物理场干扰。
Communication-Efficient Federated Fine-Tuning
Authors: Michael Theologitis, Vasilis Samoladas, Antonios Deligiannakis
First: 2025-05-07T16:13:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T17:58:08+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Federated Learning (FL) enables the utilization of vast, previously inaccessible data sources. At the same time, pre-trained Language Models (LMs) have taken the world by storm and for good reason. They exhibit remarkable emergent abilities and are readily adapted to downstream tasks. This opens one of the most exciting frontiers in FL: fine-tuning LMs. Yet, a persistent challenge in FL is the frequent, rigid communication of parameters -- a problem magnified by the sheer size of these contemporary models. The FedOpt family of algorithms has become the go-to approach for FL, relying on fixed but arbitrary intervals for model exchanges. Recently, the FDA algorithm prescribed a dynamic approach by monitoring the training progress. However, it introduced a hard-to-calibrate parameter and imposed a rigid synchronization scheme. In this work, we address these limitations by proposing the FDA-Opt family of algorithms -- a unified generalization of both FDA and FedOpt. Our experimental evaluation focuses on fine-tuning LMs on downstream NLP tasks and demonstrates that FDA-Opt outperforms FedOpt even when it is configured with hyper-parameters specifically optimized for the latter. In other words, we show that FDA-Opt is a practical, drop-in replacement for FedOpt in modern FL libraries and systems: it requires no additional configuration and delivers superior performance out of the box.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通信高效的联邦微调
联邦学习（FL）使大规模、此前难以获取的数据源得以利用。同时，预训练语言模型（LMs）因其卓越的涌现能力及对下游任务的易适配性而备受瞩目。这开辟了FL中最令人兴奋的前沿之一：微调语言模型。然而，FL中一个长期存在的挑战是参数频繁且僵化的通信——这一问题在当前模型规模庞大的背景下尤为突出。FedOpt系列算法已成为FL的主流方法，依赖固定但任意的模型交换间隔。近期，FDA算法提出动态策略，通过监控训练进度实现，但引入了难以校准的参数并强制同步机制。本文通过提出FDA-Opt系列算法，统一并泛化了FDA与FedOpt，解决了上述局限。实验评估聚焦于在下游NLP任务上微调语言模型，结果表明，即使FDA-Opt以针对FedOpt优化的超参数配置，其性能仍优于FedOpt。换言之，FDA-Opt是现代FL库与系统中FedOpt的实用、即插即用替代方案：无需额外配置，开箱即用性能更优。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to efficiently fine-tune large pre-trained language models in federated learning settings, this work proposes the FDA-Opt family of algorithms, which generalizes both FedOpt and FDA to dynamically adapt communication schedules based on training progress. Unlike FedOpt’s fixed intervals or FDA’s rigid synchronization and hard-to-tune parameters, FDA-Opt automatically adjusts communication without requiring additional configuration. Experimental results on downstream NLP tasks show that FDA-Opt consistently outperforms FedOpt—even when FedOpt is tuned with task-specific hyperparameters—demonstrating its practicality as a plug-and-play replacement in federated learning systems.
为了解决在联邦学习中高效微调大型预训练语言模型时通信效率低的问题，本文提出了FDA-Opt算法家族，它统一了FedOpt和FDA，能根据训练进度动态调整通信频率。与FedOpt固定间隔或FDA刚性同步及难以调参的缺点不同，FDA-Opt无需额外配置即可自动适应。在下游NLP任务上的实验表明，FDA-Opt即使在FedOpt经过任务特定超参调优后仍表现更优，证明其可作为现代联邦学习系统中即插即用的实用替代方案。
SANA-WM: Efficient Minute-Scale World Modeling with Hybrid Linear Diffusion Transformer
Authors: Haoyi Zhu, Haozhe Liu, Yuyang Zhao, Tian Ye, Junsong Chen, Jincheng Yu, Tong He, Song Han, Enze Xie
First: 2026-05-14T17:58:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T17:58:03+00:00
Comments: https://nvlabs.github.io/Sana/WM/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
We introduce SANA-WM, an efficient 2.6B-parameter open-source world model natively trained for one-minute generation, synthesizing high-fidelity, 720p, minute-scale videos with precise camera control. SANA-WM achieves visual quality comparable to large-scale industrial baselines such as LingBot-World and HY-WorldPlay, while significantly improving efficiency. Four core designs drive our architecture: (1) Hybrid Linear Attention combines frame-wise Gated DeltaNet (GDN) with softmax attention for memory-efficient long-context modeling. (2) Dual-Branch Camera Control ensures precise 6-DoF trajectory adherence. (3) Two-Stage Generation Pipeline applies a long-video refiner to stage-1 outputs, improving quality and consistency across sequences. (4) Robust Annotation Pipeline extracts accurate metric-scale 6-DoF camera poses from public videos to yield high-quality, spatiotemporally consistent action labels. Driven by these designs, SANA-WMdemonstrates remarkable efficiency across data, training compute, and inference hardware: it uses only $\sim$213K public video clips with metric-scale pose supervision, completes training in 15 days on 64 H100s, and generates each 60s clip on a single GPU; its distilled variant can be deployed on a single RTX 5090 with NVFP4 quantization to denoise a 60s 720p clip in 34s. On our one-minute world-model benchmark, SANA-WM demonstrates stronger action-following accuracy than prior open-source baselines and achieves comparable visual quality at $36\times$ higher throughput for scalable world modeling.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SANA-WM：基于混合线性扩散Transformer的高效分钟级世界建模
我们提出了SANA-WM，一个专为一分钟生成原生训练的2.6B参数开源世界模型，可合成高保真、720p分辨率、分钟级视频，并具备精确的相机控制能力。SANA-WM在视觉质量上可媲美LingBot-World和HY-WorldPlay等大规模工业基线，同时显著提升效率。其架构由四大核心设计驱动：（1）混合线性注意力结合帧级门控Delta网络（GDN）与softmax注意力，实现内存高效的长上下文建模；（2）双分支相机控制确保精确的6自由度轨迹遵循；（3）两阶段生成流水线通过长视频精修器提升第一阶段输出的质量与序列一致性；（4）稳健标注流水线从公开视频中提取精确的6自由度相机位姿，生成高质量时空一致的动作标签。得益于这些设计，SANA-WM在数据、训练计算量和推理硬件方面均表现出卓越效率：仅需约21.3万个带度量级位姿监督的公开视频片段，64块H100显卡上15天完成训练，单GPU即可生成每段60秒视频；其蒸馏版本可在单块RTX 5090显卡上，通过NVFP4量化，在34秒内去噪一段60秒720p视频。在我们的分钟级世界模型基准测试中，SANA-WM在动作跟随精度上优于现有开源基线，且在吞吐量提升36倍的前提下实现视觉质量相当的可扩展世界建模。
