It combines hybrid linear attention for memory-efficient long-context modeling, dual-branch camera control for precise 6-DoF trajectory adherence, a two-stage generation pipeline to enhance quality and consistency, and a robust annotation pipeline to extract metric-scale camera poses from public videos. Experimentally, SANA-WM achieves visual quality comparable to industrial baselines like LingBot-World and HY-WorldPlay while training on only ~213K video clips in 15 days on 64 H100s, and generates each 60s clip in under a minute on a single GPU; its distilled variant runs on an RTX 5090 in 34s, achieving 36x higher throughput than prior open-source models without sacrificing action-following accuracy.
为实现高效高保真分钟级世界建模，SANA-WM提出一种2.6B参数的开源架构，专为一分钟视频生成与精确相机控制设计。其采用混合线性注意力机制结合Gated DeltaNet与softmax注意力以实现长时序建模的内存效率，双分支相机控制确保6自由度轨迹精确性，两阶段生成流程提升序列质量和一致性，并通过从公开视频中提取度量级相机位姿构建鲁棒标注流程。实验表明，该模型仅用约213K视频片段在64张H100显卡上15天完成训练，单GPU即可在1分钟内生成60秒视频，其蒸馏版本在RTX 5090上34秒内完成720p视频去噪，吞吐量达前开源模型的36倍，视觉质量相当且动作跟随精度更优。
OpenDeepThink: Parallel Reasoning via Bradley--Terry Aggregation
Authors: Shang Zhou, Wenhao Chai, Kaiyuan Liu, Huanzhi Mao, Qiuyang Mang, Jingbo Shang
First: 2026-05-14T17:57:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T17:57:40+00:00
Comments: 19 pages, 4 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Test-time compute scaling is a primary axis for improving LLM reasoning. Existing methods primarily scale depth by extending a single reasoning trace. Scaling breadth by sampling multiple candidates in parallel is straightforward, but introduces a selection bottleneck: choosing the best candidate without a ground-truth verifier, since pointwise LLM judging is noisy and biased. To address this, we introduce OpenDeepThink, a population-based test-time compute framework that selects via pairwise Bradley-Terry comparison. Each generation, the LLM judges random pairs of candidates and aggregates votes via Bradley-Terry into a global ranking; top-ranked candidates are preserved and the top three quarters are mutated using the natural-language critiques produced during comparison; the bottom quarter is discarded. OpenDeepThink raises Gemini 3.1 Pro's effective Codeforces Elo by +405 points in eight sequential LLM-call rounds (~27 minutes wall-clock). The pipeline transfers across weaker and stronger models without retuning, and on the multi-domain HLE benchmark, gains appear concentrated in objectively verifiable domains and reverse in subjective ones. We release CF-73, a curated set of 73 expert-rated Codeforces problems with International Grandmaster annotation and 99% local-evaluation agreement against the official verdict.
中文标题/摘要
标题：OpenDeepThink：通过布拉德利-特里聚合实现并行推理
测试时计算扩展是提升大语言模型推理能力的主要途径。现有方法主要通过延长单一推理轨迹来扩展深度。扩展广度可通过并行采样多个候选方案实现，但会引入选择瓶颈：在缺乏真实验证器的情况下，需选择最优候选方案，而点对点LLM判断存在噪声和偏差。为此，我们提出OpenDeepThink，一种基于群体的测试时计算框架，通过成对的布拉德利-特里比较进行选择。每轮生成中，LLM随机判断候选方案对，并通过布拉德利-特里聚合投票形成全局排名；排名靠前的候选方案被保留，排名前3/4的方案使用比较过程中生成的自然语言评论进行变异；排名后1/4的方案被舍弃。OpenDeepThink在八轮连续LLM调用（约27分钟壁钟时间）中，使Gemini 3.1 Pro在Codeforces上的有效Elo提升405分。该流程无需重新调优即可在较弱和较强模型间迁移，并在多领域HLE基准测试中，收益集中在客观可验证领域，而在主观领域则呈现反向趋势。我们发布CF-73，一个包含73个专家评分Codeforces问题的精选数据集，附有国际特级大师标注，且本地评估与官方判决一致率达99%。
Summary / 总结
To overcome the limitations of depth-scaling in LLM reasoning and the selection bottlenecks of parallel breadth-scaling, OpenDeepThink introduces a population-based test-time framework using pairwise Bradley-Terry aggregation to rank candidate solutions without ground-truth verifiers. The method iteratively judges random candidate pairs with the LLM, aggregates pairwise comparisons into a global ranking via Bradley-Terry, preserves top-ranked candidates, mutates the top three quarters using natural-language critiques, and discards the bottom quarter. Experiments show that OpenDeepThink boosts Gemini 3.1 Pro’s effective Codeforces Elo by +405 points across eight sequential LLM calls (~27 minutes), transfers across models without retraining, and yields gains primarily in objectively verifiable domains while reversing in subjective ones.
为解决仅通过深度扩展推理路径和并行采样候选答案时的筛选瓶颈，OpenDeepThink提出一种基于群体的测试时计算框架，利用成对Bradley-Terry模型对候选答案进行排序与进化。每轮迭代中，模型随机比较候选答案对，通过Bradley-Terry模型聚合成全局排名，保留高排名候选，用比较中生成的自然语言批评对前三部分进行变异，淘汰底部四分之一。实验表明，该方法在八轮迭代中使Gemini 3.1 Pro的Codeforces Elo提升405分（耗时约27分钟），无需微调即可跨模型迁移，并在客观验证领域表现提升，主观领域则表现相反。
MetaBackdoor: Exploiting Positional Encoding as a Backdoor Attack Surface in LLMs
Authors: Rui Wen, Mark Russinovich, Andrew Paverd, Jun Sakuma, Ahmed Salem
First: 2026-05-14T17:56:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T17:56:22+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Backdoor attacks pose a serious security threat to large language models (LLMs), which are increasingly deployed as general-purpose assistants in safety- and privacy-critical applications. Existing LLM backdoors rely primarily on content-based triggers, requiring explicit modification of the input text. In this work, we show that this assumption is unnecessary and limiting. We introduce MetaBackdoor, a new class of backdoor attacks that exploits positional information as the trigger, without modifying textual content. Our key insight is that Transformer-based LLMs necessarily encode token positions to process ordered sequences. As a result, length-correlated positional structure is reflected in the model's internal computation and can be used as an effective non-content trigger signal. We demonstrate that even a simple length-based positional trigger is sufficient to activate stealthy backdoors. Unlike prior attacks, MetaBackdoor operates on visibly and semantically clean inputs and enables qualitatively new capabilities. We show that a backdoored LLM can be induced to disclose sensitive internal information, including proprietary system prompts, once a length condition is satisfied. We further demonstrate a self-activation scenario, where normal multi-turn interaction can move the conversation context into the trigger region and induce malicious tool-call behavior without attacker-supplied trigger text. In addition, MetaBackdoor is orthogonal to content-based backdoors and can be composed with them to create more precise and harder-to-detect activation conditions. Our results expand the threat model of LLM backdoors by revealing positional encoding as a previously overlooked attack surface. This challenges defenses that focus on detecting suspicious text and highlights the need for new defense strategies that explicitly account for positional triggers in modern LLM architectures.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MetaBackdoor：利用位置编码作为大语言模型中的后门攻击面
后门攻击对日益部署于安全与隐私关键应用中的大语言模型（LLMs）构成严重安全威胁。现有LLM后门攻击主要依赖基于内容的触发器，需显式修改输入文本。本文表明，该假设不必要且受限。我们提出MetaBackdoor，一种新型后门攻击，利用位置信息作为触发器，无需修改文本内容。核心洞察是：基于Transformer的LLMs必须编码令牌位置以处理有序序列，因此长度相关的位结构反映在模型内部计算中，可作为有效的非内容触发信号。我们证明，即使简单的基于长度的位置触发器也足以激活隐蔽后门。与以往攻击不同，MetaBackdoor作用于视觉与语义干净的输入，实现质变能力。我们展示，当满足长度条件时，被植入后门的LLM可泄露敏感内部信息，包括专有系统提示。进一步，我们演示自激活场景：正常多轮交互可使对话上下文进入触发区域，诱导恶意工具调用行为，无需攻击者提供触发文本。此外，MetaBackdoor与基于内容的后门正交，可组合形成更精确、更难检测的激活条件。我们的结果扩展了LLM后门的威胁模型，揭示位置编码为此前被忽视的攻击面，挑战仅检测可疑文本的防御策略，凸显需开发显式考虑现代LLM架构中位置触发器的新防御策略。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of existing content-based backdoor attacks in LLMs, this work introduces MetaBackdoor, a novel attack that leverages positional encoding as a non-content trigger. By exploiting the inherent need of Transformer models to encode token positions, the method activates backdoors through input length patterns without altering text semantics. Experiments show that simple length-based triggers can induce sensitive disclosures or malicious tool calls, even in self-activation scenarios where normal conversation contexts naturally enter the trigger region. MetaBackdoor is also composable with content-based backdoors, enhancing stealth and precision, and reveals a previously overlooked attack surface that undermines defenses relying solely on text-based anomaly detection.
为突破现有基于内容的LLM后门攻击局限，本研究提出MetaBackdoor，利用Transformer架构中固有的位置编码作为非内容触发机制。通过仅依赖输入长度即可激活后门，无需修改文本内容。实验表明，简单的长度触发器即可诱导敏感信息泄露或恶意工具调用，甚至在正常对话中自动触发（自激活场景）。该方法与内容型后门攻击正交，可组合使用以构建更隐蔽、更难检测的后门条件，扩展了后门威胁模型并挑战现有防御策略。
Evidential Reasoning Advances Interpretable Real-World Disease Screening
Authors: Chenyu Lian, Hong-Yu Zhou, Jing Qin
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-05-14T17:56:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T17:56:07+00:00
Comments: ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Disease screening is critical for early detection and timely intervention in clinical practice. However, most current screening models for medical images suffer from limited interpretability and suboptimal performance. They often lack effective mechanisms to reference historical cases or provide transparent reasoning pathways. To address these challenges, we introduce EviScreen, an evidential reasoning framework for disease screening that leverages region-level evidence from historical cases. The proposed EviScreen offers retrospection interpretability through regional evidence retrieved from dual knowledge banks. Using this evidential mechanism, the subsequent evidence-aware reasoning module makes predictions using both the current case and evidence from historical cases, thereby enhancing disease screening performance. Furthermore, rather than relying on post-hoc saliency maps, EviScreen enhances localization interpretability by leveraging abnormality maps derived from contrastive retrieval. Our method achieves superior performance on our carefully established benchmarks for real-world disease screening, yielding notably higher specificity at clinical-level recall. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/DopamineLcy/EviScreen.
中文标题/摘要
标题：证据推理推动可解释真实世界疾病筛查
疾病筛查对临床实践中的早期检测和及时干预至关重要。然而，当前大多数医学图像筛查模型存在可解释性有限和性能欠佳的问题，常缺乏有效机制引用历史病例或提供透明推理路径。为解决这些问题，我们提出了EviScreen，一种基于证据推理的疾病筛查框架，利用历史病例的区域级证据。EviScreen通过从双知识库检索区域证据，提供回顾性可解释性。借助该证据机制，后续的证据感知推理模块结合当前病例与历史病例证据进行预测，从而提升疾病筛查性能。此外，EviScreen不依赖事后显著性图，而是通过对比检索生成的异常图增强定位可解释性。该方法在我们精心建立的真实世界疾病筛查基准上表现优异，在临床召回率水平下显著提升特异性。代码已公开于https://github.com/DopamineLcy/EviScreen。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for interpretable and high-performance disease screening models in clinical settings, EviScreen introduces an evidential reasoning framework that integrates region-level evidence from historical cases to enhance both retrospective and localization interpretability. The method retrieves relevant regional evidence from dual knowledge banks and combines it with current case features in an evidence-aware reasoning module to improve diagnostic accuracy. Experimental results demonstrate that EviScreen achieves significantly higher specificity at clinically relevant recall levels compared to baseline models, while providing transparent reasoning pathways through contrastive-derived abnormality maps rather than post-hoc saliency techniques.
为提升医学图像筛查的可解释性与性能，EviScreen提出一种基于证据推理的框架，通过从历史病例中检索区域级证据辅助诊断决策。该框架结合双知识库与证据感知推理模块，将当前病例特征与历史证据融合，提升诊断准确性。实验表明，EviScreen在真实世界基准测试中显著提高特异性，同时保持临床召回率，优于现有方法，且不依赖事后显著性图。
Text Knows What, Tables Know When: Clinical Timeline Reconstruction via Retrieval-Augmented Multimodal Alignment
Authors: Sayantan Kumar, Shahriar Noroozizadeh, Juyong Kim, Jeremy C. Weiss
First: 2026-05-14T17:55:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T17:55:27+00:00
Comments: Sayantan Kumar, Shahriar Noroozizadeh, Juyong Kim (authors contributed equally)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reconstructing precise clinical timelines is essential for modeling patient trajectories and forecasting risk in complex, heterogeneous conditions like sepsis. While unstructured clinical narratives offer semantically rich and contextually complete descriptions of a patient's course, they often lack temporal precision and contain ambiguous event timing. Conversely, structured electronic health record (EHR) data provides precise temporal anchors but misses a substantial portion of clinically meaningful events. We introduce a retrieval-augmented multimodal alignment framework that bridges this gap to improve the temporal precision of absolute clinical timelines extracted from text. Our approach formulates timeline reconstruction as a graph-based multistep process: it first extracts central anchor events from narratives to build an initial temporal scaffold, places non-central events relative to this backbone, and then calibrates the timeline using retrieved structured EHR rows as external temporal evidence. Evaluated using instruction-tuned large language models on the i2m4 benchmark spanning MIMIC-III and MIMIC-IV, our multimodal pipeline consistently improves absolute timestamp accuracy (AULTC) and improves temporal concordance across nearly all evaluated models over unimodal text-only reconstruction, without compromising event match rates. Furthermore, our empirical gap analysis reveals that 34.8% of text-derived events are entirely absent from tabular records, demonstrating that aligning these modalities can produce a more temporally faithful and clinically informative reconstruction of patient trajectories than either source alone.
中文标题/摘要
标题：文本知其事，表格知其时：通过检索增强的多模态对齐重建临床时间线
重建精确的临床时间线对于建模患者轨迹并在复杂的异质性状况（如脓毒症）中预测风险至关重要。虽然非结构化临床叙事提供了语义丰富且上下文完整的患者病程描述，但常缺乏时间精度且事件时间模糊；相反，结构化电子健康记录（EHR）数据提供精确的时间锚点，却遗漏大量临床有意义的事件。我们提出一种检索增强的多模态对齐框架，弥合这一差距，以提升从文本中提取的绝对临床时间线的时间精度。我们的方法将时间线重建建模为基于图的多步过程：首先从叙事中提取核心锚点事件构建初始时间骨架，将非核心事件相对该骨架定位，最后利用检索到的结构化EHR行作为外部时间证据校准时间线。在涵盖MIMIC-III和MIMIC-IV的i2m4基准上，通过指令调优的大语言模型评估，我们的多模态管道在绝对时间戳准确性（AULTC）和几乎所有评估模型的时间一致性上均优于仅文本单模态重建，且不降低事件匹配率。此外，我们的经验差距分析表明，34.8%的文本衍生事件在表格记录中完全缺失，证明对齐这些模态可产生比单一来源更符合时间且更具临床信息量的患者轨迹重建。
Summary / 总结
To address the limitation of unstructured clinical text lacking precise timing while structured EHR tables often omit key events, the authors propose a retrieval-augmented multimodal alignment framework that combines narrative and tabular data. The method first extracts anchor events from text to form a temporal scaffold, then places other events relative to this backbone, and finally refines the timeline using retrieved EHR records as external temporal anchors. Evaluated on the i2m4 benchmark using instruction-tuned LLMs, the approach consistently outperforms text-only methods in absolute timestamp accuracy (AULTC) and temporal concordance without sacrificing event match rates, while empirical analysis shows that 34.8% of text-derived events are absent from tables, underscoring the value of multimodal alignment.
为解决脓毒症等复杂疾病中临床时间线重建的精度问题，该研究旨在弥合语义丰富但时间模糊的临床文本与精确但信息缺失的结构化电子病历之间的鸿沟。作者提出一种基于检索增强的多模态对齐方法：首先从文本中提取关键锚点事件构建时间骨架，再将非核心事件相对定位，最后利用检索到的结构化EHR数据校准时间线。在i2m4基准测试中，该方法在绝对时间戳准确率（AULTC）和时间一致性上显著优于纯文本基线，且未降低事件匹配率。实证分析表明，34.8%的文本事件在表格记录中完全缺失，证明多模态对齐能生成比单一模态更精确、更临床相关的患者轨迹重建。
Does Synthetic Layered Design Data Benefit Layered Design Decomposition?
Authors: Kam Man Wu, Haolin Yang, Qingyu Chen, Yihu Tang, Jingye Chen, Qifeng Chen
First: 2026-05-14T17:55:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T17:55:11+00:00
Comments: 22 pages, 10 figures. Code is available at https://github.com/YangHaolin0526/SynLayers
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Recent advances in image generation have made it easy to produce high-quality images. However, these outputs are inherently flattened, entangling foreground elements, background, and text within a fixed canvas. As a result, flexible post-generation editing remains challenging, revealing a clear last-mile gap toward practical usability. Existing approaches either rely on scarce proprietary layered assets or construct partially synthetic data from limited structural priors. However, both strategies face fundamental challenges in scalability. In this work, we investigate whether pure synthetic layered data can improve graphic design decomposition. We make the assumption that, in graphic design, effective decomposition does not require modeling inter-layer dependencies as precisely as in natural-image composition, since design elements are often intentionally arranged as modular and semantically separable components. Concretely, we conduct a data-centric study based on CLD baseline, which is a state-of-the-art layer decomposition framework. Based on the baseline, we construct our own synthetic dataset, SynLayers, generate textual supervision using vision language models, and automate inference inputs with VLM-predicted bounding boxes. Our study reveals three key findings: (1) even training with purely synthetic data can outperform non-scalable alternatives such as the widely used PrismLayersPro dataset, demonstrating its viability as a scalable and effective substitute; (2) performance consistently improves with increased training data scale, while gains begin to saturate at around 50K samples; and (3) synthetic data enables balanced control over layer-count distributions, avoiding the layer-count imbalance commonly observed in real-world datasets. We hope this data-centric study encourages broader adoption of synthetic data as a practical foundation for layered design editing systems.
中文标题/摘要
标题：合成分层设计数据是否有助于分层设计分解？
近年来图像生成技术的进步使得高质量图像的生成变得容易，但这些输出本质上是扁平化的，将前景元素、背景和文字纠缠在固定画布内。因此，生成后的灵活编辑仍具挑战性，揭示了实用性的最后一公里差距。现有方法要么依赖稀缺的专有分层资产，要么从有限结构先验构建部分合成数据。然而，这两种策略在可扩展性上均面临根本性挑战。本文探讨纯合成分层数据是否能提升图形设计分解效果。我们假设，在图形设计中，有效的分解无需像自然图像合成那样精确建模层间依赖，因为设计元素常被有意安排为模块化且语义可分离的组件。具体而言，我们基于CLD基线（当前最先进的分层分解框架）开展数据驱动研究，构建了自己的合成数据集SynLayers，利用视觉语言模型生成文本监督，并用VLM预测的边界框自动化推理输入。研究揭示三个关键发现：(1) 即使仅使用纯合成数据训练，其性能仍优于非可扩展替代方案（如广泛使用的PrismLayersPro数据集），证明其作为可扩展且有效的替代方案的可行性；(2) 性能随训练数据规模增加而持续提升，但约5万样本后收益趋于饱和；(3) 合成数据可平衡层数量分布，避免现实数据集中常见的层数量失衡问题。我们希望此数据驱动研究能推动合成数据在分层设计编辑系统中的更广泛应用。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of flexible post-generation editing in image generation due to flattened outputs, this study investigates whether purely synthetic layered data can improve graphic design decomposition. Building on the CLD baseline, the authors construct SynLayers—a synthetic dataset—using vision-language models to generate textual supervision and automate bounding box inputs. Experimental results show that training with synthetic data outperforms real-world datasets like PrismLayersPro, scales effectively with data volume (with saturation around 50K samples), and enables balanced layer-count distributions, addressing a key limitation in real datasets.
鉴于图像生成输出固有的扁平化问题导致后期编辑困难，本文研究纯合成分层数据是否能提升图形设计分解效果。基于CLD基线，作者构建了SynLayers合成数据集，利用视觉语言模型生成文本监督和自动标注框。实验表明，合成数据训练效果优于真实数据集（如PrismLayersPro），随数据量增加性能持续提升（约50K样本后趋于饱和），且能有效平衡各层数量分布，解决真实数据中常见的层数失衡问题。
Position: Behavioural Assurance Cannot Verify the Safety Claims Governance Now Demands
Authors: Pratinav Seth, Vinay Kumar Sankarapu
First: 2026-05-14T17:54:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T17:54:28+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This position paper argues that behavioural assurance, even when carefully designed, is being asked to carry safety claims it cannot verify. AI governance frameworks enacted between 2019 and early 2026 require reviewable evidence of properties such as the absence of hidden objectives, resistance to loss-of-control precursors, and bounded catastrophic capability; current assurance methodologies (primarily behavioural evaluations and red-teaming) are epistemically limited to observable model outputs and cannot verify the latent representations or long-horizon agentic behaviours these frameworks presume to regulate. We formalize this structural mismatch as the audit gap, the divergence between required and achievable verification access, and introduce the concept of fragile assurance to describe cases where the evidential structure does not support the asserted safety claim. Through an analysis of a 21-instrument inventory, we identify an incentive gradient where geopolitical and industrial pressures systematically reward surface-level behavioral proxies over deep structural verification. Finally, we propose a technical pivot: bounding the weight of behavioral evidence in legal text and extending voluntary pre-deployment access with mechanistic-evidence classes, specifically linear probes, activation patching, and before/after-training comparisons.
中文标题/摘要
标题：立场：行为保障无法验证安全声明，治理现正要求其承担此责任
本文立场认为，即使经过精心设计，行为保障仍被要求验证其无法证实的安全声明。2019年至2026年初颁布的AI治理框架要求对诸如无隐藏目标、抗失控前兆、有界灾难性能力等属性提供可审查的证据；当前保障方法（主要为行为评估与红队测试）在认识论上仅限于可观测的模型输出，无法验证这些框架所假设需监管的潜在表征或长时程代理行为。我们正式将此结构性不匹配定义为“审计缺口”——即所需与可实现的验证访问之间的差距，并引入“脆弱保障”概念，用以描述证据结构不支持所宣称安全声明的情形。通过对21项指标的分析，我们识别出一种激励梯度：地缘政治与工业压力系统性地奖励表面行为代理，而非深层结构验证。最后，我们提出技术转向：在法律文本中限制行为证据的权重，并通过机制性证据类别（如线性探针、激活修补、训练前后对比）扩展自愿部署前的访问权限。
Summary / 总结
This paper argues that current behavioural assurance methods are epistemically insufficient to verify the safety claims mandated by AI governance frameworks enacted between 2019 and 2026, which demand evidence of properties like absence of hidden objectives or bounded catastrophic capability. The authors formalize this mismatch as the 'audit gap'—the gap between what governance requires and what behavioural evaluations can verify—and introduce 'fragile assurance' to describe safety claims unsupported by adequate evidence. Through analysis of 21 instruments, they find that geopolitical and industrial pressures incentivize surface-level behavioural proxies over deep structural verification. The paper proposes a technical pivot: limiting the weight of behavioural evidence in legal texts and expanding voluntary pre-deployment access with mechanistic evidence such as linear probes and activation patching.
本文指出，尽管行为保障方法设计得当，仍无法验证2019至2026年初颁布的AI治理框架所要求的安全属性，如无隐藏目标或灾难性能力受限。作者将此不匹配称为‘审计差距’——治理所需与行为方法可验证能力之间的差距，并提出‘脆弱保障’概念，指证据结构不足以支撑所宣称安全性的案例。通过对21项治理工具的分析，发现地缘政治与产业压力系统性鼓励表面行为代理而非深层结构验证。文章建议技术转向：限制法律文本中行为证据的权重，并扩大自愿部署前的机制性证据访问，如线性探针、激活修补及训练前后对比。
MoMo: Conditioned Contrastive Representation Learning for Preference-Modulated Planning
Authors: Yusuf Syed, Viraj Parimi, Brian Williams
First: 2026-05-08T21:44:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T17:53:54+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Temporally contrastive representation learning induces a latent structure capable of reducing long-horizon planning to inference in a low-dimensional linear system. However, existing contrastive planning work learns a single latent geometry which cannot distinguish multiple valid behaviors trading task efficiency against risk exposure for the same start-goal query. We introduce MoMo, a preference-conditioned contrastive planner allowing a scalar user preference to continuously modulate plan conservativeness at inference time, without retraining. MoMo learns a joint conditioning of the representation geometry and latent prediction operator via Feature-Wise Linear Modulation and low-rank neural modulation, respectively. We show that our formulation preserves the probability density ratio encoded in the representation space that is required for inference-driven contrastive planning, further retaining its inference-time efficiency. Across six environments, MoMo smoothly adapts plan safety according to user preferences, yielding improved temporal and preferential consistency over state augmentation baselines.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MoMo：基于偏好的对比表示学习用于规划
时序对比表示学习诱导出一种潜在结构，可将长时程规划简化为低维线性系统中的推理。然而，现有对比规划方法学习单一潜在几何结构，无法区分针对相同起点-目标查询的多种有效行为（在任务效率与风险暴露之间进行权衡）。我们提出MoMo，一种基于偏好的对比规划器，允许用户通过标量偏好在推理时连续调节规划保守性，无需重新训练。MoMo通过特征级线性调制和低秩神经调制分别学习表示几何结构与潜在预测算子的联合条件化。我们证明，该方法保留了表示空间中用于推理驱动对比规划所需的概率密度比，同时保持推理时的效率。在六个环境中，MoMo根据用户偏好平滑调整规划安全性，相较状态增强基线方法，在时序与偏好一致性方面表现更优。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to adapt planning behavior to user risk preferences without retraining, MoMo introduces a preference-conditioned contrastive planner that modulates plan conservativeness at inference time using a scalar preference input. It achieves this by jointly conditioning the representation geometry and latent prediction operator via Feature-Wise Linear Modulation and low-rank neural modulation, preserving the probability density ratio essential for contrastive planning. Experiments across six environments demonstrate that MoMo smoothly adapts plan safety according to user preferences, outperforming state augmentation baselines in both temporal and preferential consistency.
为实现无需重训练即可根据用户风险偏好调整规划保守性，MoMo提出一种偏好条件对比规划器，通过标量偏好输入在推理时连续调节规划保守程度。该方法通过特征级线性调制和低秩神经调制联合调节表示几何与隐空间预测算子，保留对比规划所需的概率密度比。在六个环境中实验表明，MoMo能平滑适应用户偏好调整规划安全性，相较状态增强基线方法在时序与偏好一致性上表现更优。
Directional Confusions Reveal Divergent Inductive Biases Through Rate-Distortion Geometry in Human and Machine Vision
Authors: Leyla Roksan Caglar, Pedro A. M. Mediano, Baihan Lin
First: 2026-04-23T17:52:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T17:52:52+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
To humans, a robin seems more like a bird than a bird seems like a robin, but does this asymmetry also hold for machine vision? Humans and modern vision models can match each other in accuracy while making systematically different kinds of errors, differing not in how often they fail, but in who gets mistaken for whom. We show these directional confusions reveal distinct inductive biases invisible to accuracy alone. Using matched human and deep neural network responses on a natural-image categorization task under 12 perturbation types, we quantify asymmetry in confusion matrices and link its organization to the geometry of the information--error trade-off - how efficiently, and how gracefully, a system generalizes under distortion. We find that humans exhibit broad but weak asymmetries across many class pairs, whereas deep vision models show sparser, stronger directional collapses into a few dominant categories. Robustness training reduces overall asymmetry magnitude but fails to recover this human-like distributed structure. Generative simulations further show that these two asymmetry organizations shift the trade-off geometry in opposite directions even at matched accuracy, explaining why the same scalar asymmetry score can reflect fundamentally different generalization strategies. Together, these results establish directional confusion structure as a sensitive, interpretable signature of inductive bias that accuracy-based evaluation cannot recover.
中文标题/摘要
标题：方向性混淆揭示人类与机器视觉中通过率失真几何的差异归纳偏置
对人类而言，知更鸟看起来更像鸟，而非鸟看起来像知更鸟，但这种不对称性是否也存在于机器视觉中？人类与现代视觉模型在准确率上可以匹配，但会系统性地产生不同类型的错误，差异不在于失败频率，而在于谁被误认为谁。我们发现这些方向性混淆揭示了仅凭准确率无法察觉的差异归纳偏置。通过在12种扰动类型下，匹配人类与深度神经网络在自然图像分类任务中的响应，我们量化了混淆矩阵的不对称性，并将其组织结构与信息-误差权衡的几何结构联系起来——即系统在失真下泛化效率与优雅程度如何。我们发现，人类在许多类别对之间表现出广泛但较弱的不对称性，而深度视觉模型则表现出更稀疏、更强的方向性坍缩，集中于少数主导类别。鲁棒性训练虽能降低整体不对称性幅度，却无法恢复人类类似的分布式结构。生成模拟进一步表明，这两种不对称性组织即使在准确率匹配时，也会使权衡几何结构朝相反方向偏移，解释了为何相同的标量不对称性得分可反映根本不同的泛化策略。综合来看，这些结果确立了方向性混淆结构作为敏感且可解释的归纳偏置标志，而基于准确率的评估无法捕捉到这一点。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates whether humans and machine vision models exhibit different inductive biases by analyzing directional confusions—systematic errors where one class is mistaken for another in a non-symmetric way. Using matched human and deep neural network performance on a natural-image categorization task under 12 perturbations, the researchers quantify asymmetries in confusion matrices and link them to rate-distortion geometry, which measures how efficiently systems generalize under distortion. Results show humans display broad but weak asymmetries across many class pairs, while deep models show sparse, strong directional collapses toward a few dominant categories. Robustness training reduces asymmetry magnitude but fails to restore the human-like distributed structure. Generative simulations confirm that these asymmetry patterns alter the trade-off geometry differently even at matched accuracy, revealing that directional confusion structure is a sensitive indicator of inductive bias invisible to accuracy alone.
该研究通过分析人类与机器视觉模型在图像扰动下的方向性混淆（即类别间系统性混淆的不对称性），探究两者是否具有不同的归纳偏置。研究在12种扰动类型下，对比人类与深度神经网络在自然图像分类任务中的表现，量化混淆矩阵的不对称性，并将其与信息-误差权衡的几何结构（率失真几何）关联，揭示系统在扰动下泛化效率与鲁棒性的权衡。结果表明，人类在多个类别对间表现出广泛但较弱的方向性混淆，而深度模型则呈现稀疏且强烈的向少数主导类别的坍缩。鲁棒性训练虽降低不对称性幅度，却无法恢复人类式的分布结构，生成模拟进一步表明，这两种不对称结构即使在准确率匹配时也会以不同方式改变权衡几何，证明方向性混淆结构是准确率评估无法捕捉的、敏感且可解释的归纳偏置指标。
Speculative Interaction Agents: Building Real-Time Agents with Asynchronous I/O and Speculative Tool Calling
Authors: Coleman Hooper, Minwoo Kang, Suhong Moon, Nicholas Lee, Eric Wen, John Wawrzynek, Michael W. Mahoney, Yakun Sophia Shao, Amir Gholami, Kurt Keutzer
First: 2026-05-13T11:20:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T17:52:40+00:00
Comments: 17 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
There is a growing demand for agentic AI technologies for a range of downstream applications like customer service and personal assistants. For applications where the agent needs to interact with a person, real-time low-latency responsiveness is required; for example, with voice-controlled applications, under 1 second of latency is typically required for the interaction to feel seamless. However, if we want the LLM to reason and execute an agentic workflow with tool calling, this can add several seconds or more of latency, which is prohibitive for real-time latency-sensitive applications. In our work, we propose Speculative Interaction Agents to enable real-time interaction even for agents with complex multi-turn tool calling. We propose Asynchronous I/O, which decouples the core agent reason-and-act thread from waiting for additional information from either the user or environment, thereby allowing for overlapping agentic processing while waiting on external delays. We also propose Speculative Tool Calling as a method to manage task execution when the agent is still unsure if it has received the full information or if additional user information may later be provided. For strong cloud models, our method can be applied out-of-the-box to existing real-time cloud APIs, providing 1.3-1.7$\times$ speedups with minor accuracy loss. To enable real-time interaction with small edge-scale models, we also present a clock-based training methodology that adapts the model to handle streaming inputs and asynchronous responses, and demonstrate a synthetic data generation strategy for SFT. Altogether, this approach provides 1.6-2.2$\times$ speedups with the Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct and Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct models across multiple tool calling benchmarks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：推测性交互代理：通过异步I/O和推测式工具调用构建实时代理
当前对代理型AI技术的需求日益增长，广泛应用于客户服务和个人助理等下游场景。对于需要与人类交互的应用，要求实时低延迟响应；例如，语音控制应用通常要求交互延迟低于1秒，以确保体验无缝。然而，若让大语言模型（LLM）进行推理并执行包含工具调用的代理工作流，会增加数秒甚至更长的延迟，这对于实时性敏感的应用是不可接受的。在本研究中，我们提出推测性交互代理，即使在复杂多轮工具调用的代理中也能实现实时交互。我们提出异步I/O机制，将核心代理推理与行动线程与等待用户或环境提供额外信息解耦，从而在等待外部延迟时可重叠执行代理处理。我们还提出推测式工具调用方法，用于在代理尚未确定是否已获得完整信息或是否可能后续获得用户补充信息时管理任务执行。对于强云模型，我们的方法可直接应用于现有实时云API，提供1.3-1.7倍加速，伴随轻微精度损失。为支持小型边缘规模模型的实时交互，我们还提出一种基于时钟的训练方法，使模型适应流式输入和异步响应，并展示一种用于监督微调（SFT）的合成数据生成策略。总体而言，该方法在多个工具调用基准测试中，使用Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct和Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct模型，可实现1.6-2.2倍加速。
Summary / 总结
To address the latency bottleneck in real-time agentic AI applications such as voice assistants, this work introduces Speculative Interaction Agents, combining asynchronous I/O and speculative tool calling. Asynchronous I/O decouples the agent’s reasoning thread from waiting for user or environmental inputs, enabling concurrent processing. Speculative Tool Calling allows the agent to proceed with tool execution even when uncertain about full input availability, anticipating future user input. Experiments show that for cloud models, this approach delivers 1.3–1.7x speedups with minimal accuracy loss, and for edge models, a clock-based training method and synthetic data strategy enable 1.6–2.2x speedups on benchmarks using Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct and Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct.
为解决实时交互型AI代理（如语音助手）中因工具调用导致的高延迟问题，该研究提出“推测式交互代理”，通过异步I/O技术将代理推理线程与等待用户或环境输入解耦，实现并行处理；同时引入推测式工具调用机制，使代理在信息未完全接收时仍可提前执行工具。该方法在云端模型上可直接应用，带来1.3–1.7倍加速且精度损失微小；结合面向边缘设备的时钟训练方法与合成数据策略，对Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct和Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct模型实现1.6–2.2倍加速，支持复杂多轮工具调用下的实时交互。
Hand-in-the-Loop: Improving Dexterous VLA via Seamless Interventional Correction
Authors: Zhuohang Li, Liqun Huang, Wei Xu, Zhengming Zhu, Nie Lin, Xiao Ma, Xinjun Sheng, Ruoshi Wen
First: 2026-05-14T17:51:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T17:51:40+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are prone to compounding errors in dexterous manipulation, where high-dimensional action spaces and contact-rich dynamics amplify small policy deviations over long horizons. While Interactive Imitation Learning (IIL) can refine policies through human takeover data, applying it to high-degree-of-freedom (DoF) robotic hands remains challenging due to a command mismatch between human teleoperation and policy execution at the takeover moment, which causes abrupt robot-hand configuration changes, or "gesture jumps". We present Hand-in-the-Loop (HandITL), a seamless human-in-the-loop intervention method that blends human corrective intent with autonomous policy execution to avoid gesture jumps during bimanual dexterous manipulation. Compared with direct teleoperation takeover, HandITL reduces takeover jitter by 99.8% and preserves robust post-takeover manipulation, reducing grasp failures by 87.5% and mean completion time by 19.1%. We validate HandITL on tasks requiring bimanual coordination, tool use, and fine-grained long-horizon manipulation. When used to collect intervention data for policy refinement, HandITL yields policies that outperform those trained with standard teleoperation data by 19% on average across three long-horizon dexterous tasks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：手在环中：通过无缝干预修正提升灵巧视觉-语言-动作模型
视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在灵巧操作中易因高维动作空间和接触密集动态导致误差累积。虽然交互式模仿学习（IIL）可通过人类接管数据优化策略，但将其应用于高自由度（DoF）机械手仍具挑战，因人类遥操作与策略执行在接管时刻存在指令不匹配，导致机器人手部构型突变或“手势跳跃”。我们提出手在环中（HandITL），一种无缝人机协同干预方法，融合人类修正意图与自主策略执行，避免双臂灵巧操作中的手势跳跃。相比直接遥操作接管，HandITL将接管抖动降低99.8%，保持接管后稳健操作，使抓取失败率降低87.5%，平均完成时间缩短19.1%。我们在需双臂协调、工具使用及精细长时程操作任务中验证HandITL。当用于收集干预数据以优化策略时，HandITL在三项长时程灵巧任务中平均性能优于标准遥操作数据训练策略19%。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the tendency of Vision-Language-Action models to accumulate errors in dexterous manipulation due to high-dimensional action spaces and contact-rich dynamics, this work introduces Hand-in-the-Loop, a seamless human-in-the-loop intervention method designed to mitigate abrupt configuration changes—known as 'gesture jumps'—that occur during human takeovers. The method blends human corrective intent with autonomous policy execution to maintain smooth robot-hand transitions during bimanual tasks. Experimental results show that HandITL reduces takeover jitter by 99.8%, cuts grasp failures by 87.5%, and shortens mean completion time by 19.1% compared to direct teleoperation. When used to collect intervention data for policy refinement, HandITL-trained policies outperform those trained with standard teleoperation data by 19% on average across three long-horizon dexterous tasks.
为解决视觉-语言-动作模型在精细操作中因高维动作空间和接触密集动态导致的误差累积问题，本文提出Hand-in-the-Loop方法，通过融合人类修正意图与自主策略执行，避免操作接管时出现的机器人手部突变（gesture jumps）。实验表明，该方法将接管抖动降低99.8%，抓取失败率降低87.5%，平均完成时间缩短19.1%。当用于收集干预数据以优化策略时，HandITL训练的策略在三项长时程精细任务上平均优于标准遥操作数据训练策略19%。
MeMo: Memory as a Model
Authors: Ryan Wei Heng Quek, Sanghyuk Lee, Alfred Wei Lun Leong, Arun Verma, Alok Prakash, Nancy F. Chen, Bryan Kian Hsiang Low, Daniela Rus, Armando Solar-Lezama
First: 2026-05-14T17:51:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T17:51:34+00:00
Comments: This paper introduces MeMo, a framework that augments any LLM with up-to-date or domain-specific knowledge via a trained memory model, avoiding costly retraining, mitigating catastrophic forgetting, and remaining robust to retrieval noise
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) achieve strong performance across a wide range of tasks, but remain frozen after pretraining until subsequent updates. Many real-world applications require timely, domain-specific information, motivating the need for efficient mechanisms to incorporate new knowledge. In this paper, we introduce MeMo (Memory as a Model), a modular framework that encodes new knowledge into a dedicated memory model while keeping the LLM parameters unchanged. Compared to existing methods, MeMo offers several advantages: (a) it captures complex cross-document relationships, (b) it is robust to retrieval noise, (c) it avoids catastrophic forgetting in the LLM, (d) it does not require access to the LLM's weights or output logits, enabling plug-and-play integration with both open and proprietary closed-source LLMs, and (e) its retrieval cost is independent of corpus size at inference time. Our experimental results on three benchmarks, BrowseComp-Plus, NarrativeQA, and MuSiQue, show that MeMo achieves strong performance compared to existing methods across diverse settings.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MeMo：将记忆作为模型
大型语言模型（LLMs）在多种任务中表现出色，但在预训练后直至后续更新前保持不变。许多现实应用需要及时、特定领域的信息，因此亟需高效机制来整合新知识。本文提出MeMo（Memory as a Model），一种模块化框架，通过专用记忆模型编码新知识，同时保持LLM参数不变。与现有方法相比，MeMo具有以下优势：(a) 捕获跨文档的复杂关系，(b) 对检索噪声鲁棒，(c) 避免LLM的灾难性遗忘，(d) 不需要访问LLM的权重或输出logits，支持与开源和闭源专有LLM的即插即用集成，(e) 推理时检索成本与语料库大小无关。我们在三个基准（BrowseComp-Plus、NarrativeQA和MuSiQue）上的实验结果表明，MeMo在多种设置下均优于现有方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to update large language models with timely, domain-specific knowledge without retraining, MeMo introduces a modular framework that encodes new information into a separate memory model while leaving the LLM parameters untouched. This approach enables the model to capture complex cross-document relationships, resist retrieval noise, and avoid catastrophic forgetting, without requiring access to the LLM’s internal weights or logits—allowing seamless integration with both open and closed-source models. Experiments on three benchmarks—BrowseComp-Plus, NarrativeQA, and MuSiQue—demonstrate that MeMo consistently outperforms existing methods across diverse settings, with retrieval costs remaining constant regardless of corpus size.
为了解决大语言模型在预训练后无法及时更新领域知识的问题，MeMo提出一种模块化框架，通过训练独立的记忆模型来编码新知识，而无需修改原始语言模型参数。该方法能捕捉跨文档的复杂关系，抵御检索噪声，避免灾难性遗忘，且无需访问模型权重或输出对数，支持与开源和闭源模型的即插即用集成。在BrowseComp-Plus、NarrativeQA和MuSiQue三个基准测试中，MeMo在多种场景下均显著优于现有方法，且其检索开销与语料库规模无关。
Self-Distilled Agentic Reinforcement Learning
Authors: Zhengxi Lu, Zhiyuan Yao, Zhuowen Han, Zi-Han Wang, Jinyang Wu, Qi Gu, Xunliang Cai, Weiming Lu, Jun Xiao, Yueting Zhuang, Yongliang Shen
First: 2026-05-14T17:51:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T17:51:26+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reinforcement learning (RL) has emerged as a central paradigm for post-training LLM agents, yet its trajectory-level reward signal provides only coarse supervision for long-horizon interaction. On-Policy Self-Distillation (OPSD) complements RL by introducing dense token-level guidance from a teacher branch augmented with privileged context. However, transferring OPSD to multi-turn agents proves problematic: compounding multi-turn instability destabilizes supervision, while skill-conditioned privileged guidance requires asymmetric treatment for negative teacher rejections may arise from imperfect skills retrieval or utilization. We introduce SDAR (Self-Distilled Agentic Reinforcement Learning), which treats OPSD as a gated auxiliary objective while keeping RL as the primary optimization backbone. SDAR maps detached token-level signals into a sigmoid gate, strengthening distillation on teacher-endorsed positive-gap tokens and softly attenuating negative teacher rejections. Across the Qwen2.5 and Qwen3 families on ALFWorld, WebShop, and Search-QA, SDAR substantially improves over GRPO (+9.4% on ALFWorld, +7.0% on Search-QA, +10.2% on WebShop-Acc), avoids the instability of naive GRPO+OPSD, and consistently outperforms hybrid RL--OPSD baselines across model scales.
中文标题/摘要
标题：自蒸馏代理强化学习
强化学习（RL）已成为训练后大语言模型代理的核心范式，但其轨迹级奖励信号仅能粗略监督长时交互。在线自蒸馏（OPSD）通过引入教师分支增强的特权上下文，提供密集的token级指导，补充RL。然而，将OPSD迁移到多轮代理中存在困难：多轮累积的不稳定性会破坏监督，而技能条件化的特权指导可能因技能检索或利用不完美而产生不对称的负向教师拒绝。我们提出SDAR（自蒸馏代理强化学习），将OPSD视为门控辅助目标，同时保留RL作为主要优化骨架。SDAR将脱离的token级信号映射为sigmoid门控，强化对教师认可的正向差距token的蒸馏，同时软性衰减负向教师拒绝。在ALFWorld、WebShop和Search-QA数据集上，SDAR在Qwen2.5和Qwen3系列中显著优于GRPO（ALFWorld+9.4%，Search-QA+7.0%，WebShop-Acc+10.2%），避免了朴素GRPO+OPSD的不稳定性，并在不同模型规模下持续优于混合RL-OPSD基线。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the coarse supervision from trajectory-level rewards in RL for long-horizon agent interactions, the paper proposes SDAR, which integrates On-Policy Self-Distillation (OPSD) as a gated auxiliary objective to RL. SDAR uses a sigmoid gate to modulate token-level teacher guidance, amplifying positive teacher endorsements while softly suppressing negative rejections caused by imperfect skill retrieval. Experiments on Qwen2.5 and Qwen3 across ALFWorld, WebShop, and Search-QA show SDAR outperforms GRPO by +9.4% on ALFWorld, +7.0% on Search-QA, and +10.2% on WebShop-Acc, while avoiding the instability of naive GRPO+OPSD and consistently beating hybrid RL-OPSD baselines.
为解决强化学习在长时交互中奖励信号粗粒度的问题，本文提出SDAR方法，将On-Policy Self-Distillation（OPSD）作为门控辅助目标与RL主优化结合。SDAR将教师模型的词级信号映射为sigmoid门控，强化对教师认可正向词的蒸馏，同时软性抑制负向拒绝。在Qwen2.5和Qwen3模型于ALFWorld、WebShop和Search-QA上的实验表明，SDAR相较GRPO提升9.4%（ALFWorld）、7.0%（Search-QA）、10.2%（WebShop-Acc），避免了直接结合OPSD的不稳定性，并持续优于混合RL-OPSD基线。
RoSHAP: A Distributional Framework and Robust Metric for Stable Feature Attribution
Authors: Lanxin Xiang, Liang Shi, Youhui Ye, Boyu Jiang, Dawei Zhou, Feng Guo
First: 2026-05-14T17:51:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T17:51:09+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Feature attribution analysis is critical for interpreting machine learning models and supporting reliable data-driven decisions. However, feature attribution measures often exhibit stochastic variation: different train--test splits, random seeds, or model-fitting procedures can produce substantially different attribution values and feature rankings. This paper proposes a framework for incorporating stochastic nature of feature attribution and a robust attribution metric, RoSHAP, for stable feature ranking based on the SHAP metric. The proposed framework models the distribution of feature attribution scores and estimates it through bootstrap resampling and kernel density estimation. We show that, under mild regularity conditions, the aggregated feature attribution score is asymptotically Gaussian, which greatly reduces the computational cost of distribution estimation. The RoSHAP summarizes the distribution of SHAP into a robust feature-ranking criterion that simultaneously rewards features that are active, strong, and stable. Through simulations and real-data experiments, the proposed framework and RoSHAP outperform standard single-run attribution measures in identifying signal features. In addition, models built using RoSHAP-selected features achieve predictive performance comparable to full-feature models while using substantially fewer predictors. The proposed RoSHAP approach improves the stability and interpretability of machine learning models, enabling reliable and consistent insights for analysis.
中文标题/摘要
标题：RoSHAP：一种分布框架与稳健度量，用于稳定特征归因
特征归因分析对于解释机器学习模型和支持可靠的基于数据的决策至关重要。然而，特征归因度量常表现出随机性：不同的训练-测试划分、随机种子或模型拟合过程可能导致显著不同的归因值和特征排序。本文提出了一种纳入特征归因随机性的框架及基于SHAP度量的稳健归因度量RoSHAP，用于稳定特征排序。该框架通过自助法重采样和核密度估计建模特征归因分数的分布，并证明在温和正则条件下，聚合特征归因分数渐近服从高斯分布，大幅降低分布估计的计算成本。RoSHAP将SHAP分布总结为一个稳健的特征排序准则，同时奖励活跃、强效且稳定的特征。通过模拟和真实数据实验，该框架及RoSHAP在识别信号特征方面优于标准单次运行归因度量。此外，使用RoSHAP选择的特征构建的模型在预测性能上与全特征模型相当，但使用的预测变量显著更少。所提出的RoSHAP方法提升了机器学习模型的稳定性和可解释性，为分析提供可靠且一致的洞察。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the instability of feature attribution methods under varying experimental conditions, this paper introduces RoSHAP, a distributional framework and robust metric for stable feature ranking based on SHAP values. The method models the distribution of attribution scores using bootstrap resampling and kernel density estimation, leveraging asymptotic Gaussianity to reduce computational cost. Experimental results show RoSHAP consistently identifies signal features better than single-run methods and yields models with comparable predictive performance using fewer features, enhancing both interpretability and reliability.
为解决特征归因方法在不同实验条件下不稳定的问题，本文提出RoSHAP，一种基于SHAP值的分布框架与稳健特征排序指标。该方法通过自助采样和核密度估计建模归因分数分布，并利用渐近正态性降低计算成本。实验表明，RoSHAP比单次运行方法更稳定地识别信号特征，且使用其选中的特征构建的模型性能接近全特征模型，但显著减少特征数量，从而提升模型可解释性与效率。
The Potential of Convolutional Neural Networks for Cancer Detection
Authors: Hossein Molaeian, Kaveh Karamjani, Sina Teimouri, Saeed Roshani, Sobhan Roshani
First: 2024-12-22T20:33:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T17:50:45+00:00

Abstract
Early detection is crucial for successful cancer treatment and increasing survivability rates, particularly in the most common forms. Ten different cancers have been identified in most of these advances that effectively use CNNs (Convolutional Neural Networks) for classification. The distinct architectures of CNNs used in each study concentrate on pattern recognition for different types of cancer across various datasets. The advantages and disadvantages of each approach are identified by comparing these architectures. This study explores the potential of integrating CNNs into clinical practice to complement traditional diagnostic methods. It also identifies the top-performing CNN architectures, highlighting their role in enhancing diagnostic capabilities in healthcare.
