This study explores user experience with AI-generated interface prototypes by surveying 92 participants who evaluated both AI- and human-created prototypes without knowing their origin. Using the UEQ-S questionnaire, researchers measured pragmatic (e.g., usability, efficiency) and hedonic (e.g., originality, innovation) dimensions. Results showed positive ratings for pragmatic aspects but neutral or negative ratings for hedonic ones, indicating that while AI-generated prototypes are functionally usable, they often replicate conventional visual and structural patterns, which diminish perceptions of originality.
本研究通过调查92名参与者对AI生成与人工设计界面原型的评价（未告知原型来源），使用UEQ-S量表评估实用性和情感性维度。结果显示，原型在实用性（如易用性、效率）方面获得正面评价，但在情感性（如原创性、创新性）方面评价中性或负面，表明AI生成的界面虽功能可用，但常沿用传统视觉与结构模式，削弱了用户对其原创性的感知。
Why Goal-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning Works: Relation to Dual Control
Authors: Nathan P. Lawrence, Ali Mesbah
First: 2025-12-06T15:28:35+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T17:36:34+00:00
Comments: IFAC world congress postprint
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Goal-conditioned reinforcement learning (RL) concerns the problem of training an agent to maximize the probability of reaching target goal states. This paper presents an analysis of the goal-conditioned setting based on optimal control. In particular, we derive an optimality gap between more classical, often quadratic, objectives and the goal-conditioned reward, elucidating the success of goal-conditioned RL and why classical ``dense'' rewards can falter. We then consider the partially observed Markov decision setting and connect state estimation to our probabilistic reward, making the goal-conditioned reward well suited to dual control problems. The advantages of goal-conditioned policies are validated on nonlinear and uncertain environments using both RL and predictive control techniques.
中文标题/摘要
标题：目标条件强化学习为何有效：与双重控制的关系
目标条件强化学习（RL）关注训练智能体以最大化到达目标状态的概率。本文基于最优控制理论分析目标条件设置。特别是，我们推导出经典（常为二次型）目标函数与目标条件奖励之间的最优性差距，阐明了目标条件RL的成功原因及为何经典“密集”奖励可能失效。随后，我们考虑部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程，并将状态估计与概率奖励相联系，使目标条件奖励适用于双重控制问题。通过强化学习和预测控制技术，在非线性和不确定环境中验证了目标条件策略的优势。
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates why goal-conditioned reinforcement learning succeeds by framing it through the lens of optimal control, revealing an optimality gap between classical quadratic objectives and goal-conditioned rewards, which explains why dense rewards often fail. The authors extend this analysis to partially observed Markov decision processes, linking state estimation to the probabilistic nature of goal-conditioned rewards and demonstrating their suitability for dual control. Experimental results across nonlinear and uncertain environments confirm that goal-conditioned policies outperform classical approaches when combined with both reinforcement learning and predictive control methods.
本文探讨了目标条件强化学习为何有效，将其与双重控制原理联系起来，指出其奖励结构天然平衡探索与利用。作者推导出经典二次目标与目标条件奖励之间的最优性差距，解释了为何密集奖励在复杂环境中常失效。他们进一步将分析扩展至部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程，表明状态估计与概率奖励形式自然结合，使目标条件策略特别适合双重控制问题。实验结果在非线性和不确定环境中验证了目标条件策略在结合强化学习与预测控制时优于传统方法。
SyncLight: Single-Edit Multi-View Relighting
Authors: David Serrano-Lozano, Anand Bhattad, Luis Herranz, Jean-François Lalonde, Javier Vazquez-Corral
First: 2026-01-23T18:59:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T17:35:56+00:00
Comments: Project page: http://sync-light.github.io
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
We present SyncLight, a method to enable consistent, parametric control over light sources across multiple uncalibrated views of a static scene conditioned on a single view. While single-view relighting has advanced significantly, existing generative approaches struggle to maintain the rigorous lighting consistency essential for multi-camera broadcasts, stereoscopic cinema, and virtual production. SyncLight addresses this by enabling precise control over light intensity and color across a multi-view capture of a scene, conditioned on a single reference edit. Our method leverages a multi-view diffusion transformer trained using a latent bridge matching formulation, achieving high-fidelity relighting of the entire image set in a single inference step. To facilitate training, we introduce a large-scale hybrid dataset comprising diverse synthetic environments -- curated from existing sources and newly designed scenes -- alongside high-fidelity, real-world multi-view captures under calibrated illumination. Though trained only on image pairs, SyncLight generalizes zero-shot to an arbitrary number of viewpoints, effectively propagating lighting changes across all views, without requiring camera pose information. SyncLight enables practical relighting workflows for multi-view capture systems.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SyncLight：单次编辑多视角重照明
我们提出了SyncLight，一种方法，可在多个未校准视角下，基于单视角条件，实现对静态场景光源的一致性、参数化控制。尽管单视角重照明已取得显著进展，但现有生成方法难以维持多摄像机广播、立体电影和虚拟制作所必需的严格光照一致性。SyncLight通过基于单参考编辑实现对场景多视角捕获中光照强度与颜色的精确控制，解决了这一问题。该方法利用一种多视角扩散Transformer，采用潜在桥匹配形式训练，可在单次推理步骤中实现整组图像的高保真重照明。为促进训练，我们构建了一个大规模混合数据集，包含多样化的合成环境（部分来自现有资源，部分为新设计场景）以及在标定光照下拍摄的高保真真实多视角图像。尽管仅在图像对上训练，SyncLight可零样本泛化至任意数量视角，无需相机姿态信息，即可有效将光照变化传播至所有视角。SyncLight为多视角捕获系统提供了实用的重照明工作流程。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for lighting consistency across multiple uncalibrated views in applications like virtual production and stereoscopic cinema, SyncLight introduces a method that enables precise, parametric control of light intensity and color across all views using only a single edited reference view. It employs a multi-view diffusion transformer trained via latent bridge matching, allowing high-fidelity relighting of entire image sets in one inference pass. Evaluated on a large-scale hybrid dataset combining synthetic and real multi-view captures, SyncLight generalizes zero-shot to any number of viewpoints without needing camera poses, demonstrating strong performance in propagating lighting edits across views while preserving scene geometry and material properties.
为满足虚拟制作和立体电影等应用中多视角场景的光照一致性需求，SyncLight提出一种仅通过单视角编辑即可控制多视角光照参数的方法。该方法基于多视角扩散Transformer，采用潜在桥匹配训练框架，单次推理即可实现整组多视角图像的高保真重光照。在合成与真实多视角数据混合构成的大规模数据集上验证，SyncLight无需相机位姿信息，即可零样本泛化至任意视角，有效将光照修改传播至所有视图，保持高精度光照一致性。
CoCo-InEKF: State Estimation with Learned Contact Covariances in Dynamic, Contact-Rich Scenarios
Authors: Michael Baumgartner, David Müller, Agon Serifi, Ruben Grandia, Espen Knoop, Markus Gross, Moritz Bächer
Venue: RSS 2026
First: 2026-05-14T17:35:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T17:35:06+00:00
Comments: RSS 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Robust state estimation for highly dynamic motion of legged robots remains challenging, especially in dynamic, contact-rich scenarios. Traditional approaches often rely on binary contact states that fail to capture the nuances of partial contact or directional slippage. This paper presents CoCo-InEKF, a differentiable invariant extended Kalman filter that utilizes continuous contact velocity covariances instead of binary contact states. These learned covariances allow the method to dynamically modulate contact confidence, accounting for more nuanced conditions ranging from firm contact to directional slippage or no contact. To predict these covariances for a set of predefined contact candidate points, we employ a lightweight neural network trained end-to-end using a state-error loss. This approach eliminates the need for heuristic ground-truth contact labels. In addition, we propose an automated contact candidate selection procedure and demonstrate that our method is insensitive to their exact placement. Experiments on a bipedal robot demonstrate a superior accuracy-efficiency tradeoff for linear velocity estimation, as well as improved filter consistency compared to baseline methods. This enables the robust execution of challenging motions, including dancing and complex ground interactions -- both in simulation and in the real world.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CoCo-InEKF：在动态接触丰富的场景中利用学习接触协方差的状态估计
腿式机器人高度动态运动的鲁棒状态估计仍具挑战性，尤其是在动态接触丰富的场景中。传统方法常依赖二元接触状态，无法捕捉部分接触或方向性滑移的细微差别。本文提出CoCo-InEKF，一种可微分不变扩展卡尔曼滤波器，使用连续接触速度协方差替代二元接触状态。这些学习到的协方差允许方法动态调节接触置信度，涵盖从牢固接触、方向性滑移到无接触等更细微条件。为预测预定义接触候选点集的协方差，我们采用轻量级神经网络，通过状态误差损失端到端训练，无需人工标注的接触真值。此外，我们提出自动化接触候选点选择流程，并证明方法对候选点精确位置不敏感。在双足机器人实验中，该方法在线速度估计上展现出更优的精度-效率权衡，且相比基线方法滤波一致性更优，从而支持在仿真与现实世界中执行复杂动作，如舞蹈和复杂地面交互。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of binary contact models in accurately capturing partial or directional contact during legged robot motion, this work introduces CoCo-InEKF, a differentiable invariant EKF that replaces binary contact states with continuous, learned contact velocity covariances. These covariances are predicted by a lightweight neural network trained end-to-end using state-error loss, eliminating the need for ground-truth contact labels. The method also includes an automated contact candidate selection procedure, showing robustness to placement variations. Experimental results on a bipedal robot demonstrate improved accuracy and efficiency in linear velocity estimation, enhanced filter consistency, and successful execution of complex motions like dancing and dynamic ground interactions in both simulation and real-world settings.
为解决传统二值接触模型在腿式机器人动态运动中难以捕捉部分接触或滑移的问题，本文提出CoCo-InEKF方法，采用可微分不变扩展卡尔曼滤波，用连续接触速度协方差替代二值接触状态，协方差由轻量级神经网络端到端学习，基于状态误差损失训练，无需人工标注接触标签。实验表明，该方法在直线速度估计上具有更优的精度-效率平衡，滤波一致性优于基线方法，可稳定执行如跳舞和复杂地面交互等高难度动作，适用于仿真与真实环境。
Proxy Compression for Language Modeling
Authors: Lin Zheng, Xinyu Li, Qian Liu, Xiachong Feng, Lingpeng Kong
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-02-04T07:36:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T17:33:56+00:00
Comments: ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Modern language models are trained almost exclusively on token sequences produced by a fixed tokenizer, an external lossless compressor often over UTF-8 byte sequences, thereby coupling the model to that compressor. This work introduces proxy compression, an alternative training scheme that preserves the efficiency benefits of compressed inputs while providing an end-to-end, raw-byte interface at inference time. During training, a single language model is jointly trained on raw byte sequences and compressed views generated by external compressors; through the process, the model learns to internally align compressed sequences and raw bytes. This alignment enables strong transfer between the two formats, even when training predominantly on compressed inputs that are discarded at inference. Extensive experiments on code language modeling demonstrate that proxy compression substantially improves training efficiency and significantly outperforms pure byte-level baselines given fixed compute budgets. As model scale increases, these gains become more pronounced, and proxy-trained models eventually match or surpass tokenizer approaches, all while operating solely on raw bytes and retaining the inherent robustness of byte-level modeling. Our code is available at https://github.com/LZhengisme/proxy-compression.
中文标题/摘要
标题：语言建模中的代理压缩
现代语言模型几乎完全依赖固定分词器生成的标记序列进行训练，而该分词器通常是一个外部无损压缩器，常基于UTF-8字节序列，从而将模型与该压缩器耦合。本文提出代理压缩，这是一种替代训练方案，它在保持压缩输入效率优势的同时，为推理阶段提供端到端的原始字节接口。训练过程中，单一语言模型同时在原始字节序列和由外部压缩器生成的压缩视图上联合训练；通过该过程，模型学习在内部对齐压缩序列与原始字节。这种对齐使得两种格式间实现强迁移，即使训练主要基于在推理阶段被丢弃的压缩输入。在代码语言建模上的大量实验表明，代理压缩显著提升训练效率，并在计算预算固定的情况下显著优于纯字节级基线。随着模型规模增大，这些收益愈发明显，代理训练模型最终可匹配或超越分词器方法，且全程仅使用原始字节，同时保留字节级建模的固有鲁棒性。代码见 https://github.com/LZhengisme/proxy-compression。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of training language models on tokenized inputs while being locked to specific compressors, this work proposes proxy compression: a training method that jointly trains a model on raw byte sequences and compressed views from external tools, enabling it to learn internal alignments between compressed and raw formats. During inference, the model operates directly on raw bytes, preserving efficiency without requiring decompression. Experiments on code modeling show that proxy compression significantly improves training speed and performance over pure byte-level baselines, especially as model scale increases, eventually matching or exceeding tokenizer-based methods while maintaining byte-level robustness.
为了解决语言模型训练中因依赖固定分词器和外部压缩器而导致的效率问题，该研究提出代理压缩方法：在训练阶段同时输入原始字节序列与外部压缩器生成的压缩视图，使模型内部对齐两种格式。推理时模型直接处理原始字节，无需压缩器，既保持效率又保留字节级鲁棒性。在代码建模实验中，代理压缩显著提升训练效率，且在固定算力下明显优于纯字节级基线，随着模型规模增大，性能优势更显著，最终可媲美甚至超越传统分词器方法。
Hyperbolic Graph Neural Networks Under the Microscope: The Role of Geometry-Task Alignment
Authors: Dionisia Naddeo, Jonas Linkerhägner, Nicola Toschi, Geri Skenderi, Veronica Lachi
First: 2026-02-02T09:01:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T17:33:51+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Many complex networks exhibit hierarchical, tree-like structures, making hyperbolic space a natural candidate wherein to learn representations of them. Based on this observation, Hyperbolic Graph Neural Networks (HGNNs) have been widely adopted as a principled choice for representation learning on tree-like graphs. In this work, we question this paradigm by proposing the additional condition of geometry--task alignment, i.e., whether the metric structure of the target follows that of the input graph. We theoretically and empirically demonstrate the capability of HGNNs to recover low-distortion representations on regression problems, and show that their geometric inductive bias becomes helpful when the problem requires preserving metric structure. By jointly analyzing predictive performance and embedding distortion, we further show that HGNNs gain an advantage on link prediction, a naturally geometry-aligned task, whereas this advantage largely disappears on standard node classification benchmarks, which are typically not geometry--aligned. Overall, our findings shift the focus from only asking "Is the graph hyperbolic?" to also questioning "Is the task aligned with hyperbolic geometry?", showing that HGNNs consistently outperform Euclidean models under such alignment, while their advantage vanishes otherwise.
中文标题/摘要
标题：显微镜下的双曲图神经网络：几何与任务对齐的作用
许多复杂网络表现出层次化、树状结构，使得双曲空间成为学习其表示的自然候选。基于这一观察，双曲图神经网络（HGNNs）被广泛采用作为树状图表示学习的合理选择。本文质疑这一范式，提出几何-任务对齐的附加条件，即目标的度量结构是否与输入图一致。我们从理论和实证上证明了HGNNs在回归问题中恢复低失真表示的能力，并表明当任务需要保持度量结构时，其几何归纳偏置变得有益。通过联合分析预测性能和嵌入失真，我们进一步表明，HGNNs在链接预测任务（天然几何对齐）上具有优势，而在标准节点分类基准（通常非几何对齐）上，该优势基本消失。总体而言，我们的发现将关注点从仅问“图是否是双曲的？”扩展到也问“任务是否与双曲几何对齐？”，表明在对齐条件下HGNNs始终优于欧氏模型，否则其优势消失。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that many real-world networks exhibit hierarchical, tree-like structures, this work investigates whether Hyperbolic Graph Neural Networks (HGNNs) are inherently superior for such graphs. The authors introduce the concept of geometry-task alignment, questioning whether the task’s metric structure matches the hyperbolic geometry of the input graph. Through theoretical analysis and empirical experiments, they show that HGNNs excel at preserving low-distortion embeddings in regression tasks and link prediction—tasks naturally aligned with hyperbolic geometry—while their advantage diminishes on standard node classification benchmarks, which are typically not geometry-aligned. This suggests that HGNNs’ performance depends critically on alignment between task and geometry, not merely on graph structure.
鉴于许多现实世界网络具有树状、分层结构，本文探讨了超球面图神经网络（HGNN）是否天然适合此类数据。研究引入“几何-任务对齐”概念，质疑任务的度量需求是否与输入图的超球面几何结构匹配。理论分析与实验表明，HGNN在回归任务和链接预测（天然与超球面几何对齐）中能保持低失真嵌入并表现更优，而在标准节点分类任务（通常非几何对齐）中优势显著减弱。结果表明，HGNN性能取决于任务与几何结构的对齐程度，而非仅取决于图的结构本身。
CLOVER: Closed-Loop Value Estimation \& Ranking for End-to-End Autonomous Driving Planning
Authors: Sining Ang, Yuguang Yang, Canyu Chen, Yan Wang
First: 2026-05-14T17:32:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T17:32:18+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
End-to-end autonomous driving planners are commonly trained by imitating a single logged trajectory, yet evaluated by rule-based planning metrics that measure safety, feasibility, progress, and comfort. This creates a training--evaluation mismatch: trajectories close to the logged path may violate planning rules, while alternatives farther from the demonstration can remain valid and high-scoring. The mismatch is especially limiting for proposal-selection planners, whose performance depends on candidate-set coverage and scorer ranking quality. We propose CLOVER, a Closed-LOop Value Estimation and Ranking framework for end-to-end autonomous driving planning. CLOVER follows a lightweight generator--scorer formulation: a generator produces diverse candidate trajectories, and a scorer predicts planning-metric sub-scores to rank them at inference time. To expand proposal support beyond single-trajectory imitation, CLOVER constructs evaluator-filtered pseudo-expert trajectories and trains the generator with set-level coverage supervision. It then performs conservative closed-loop self-distillation: the scorer is fitted to true evaluator sub-scores on generated proposals, while the generator is refined toward teacher-selected top-$k$ and vector-Pareto targets with stability regularization. We analyze when an imperfect scorer can improve the generator, showing that scorer-mediated refinement is reliable when scorer-selected targets are enriched under the true evaluator and updates remain conservative. On NAVSIM, CLOVER achieves 94.5 PDMS and 90.4 EPDMS, establishing a new state of the art. On the more challenging NavHard split, it obtains 48.3 EPDMS, matching the strongest reported result. On supplementary nuScenes open-loop evaluation, CLOVER achieves the lowest L2 error and collision rate among compared methods. Code data will be released at https://github.com/WilliamXuanYu/CLOVER.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CLOVER：面向端到端自动驾驶规划的闭环价值估计与排序
端到端自动驾驶规划器通常通过模仿单一记录轨迹进行训练，但评估时却使用基于规则的规划指标，衡量安全性、可行性、进度和舒适性。这导致训练与评估之间的不匹配：接近记录路径的轨迹可能违反规划规则，而偏离示范轨迹的替代方案可能仍有效且得分高。这种不匹配对提案选择型规划器尤为不利，其性能依赖于候选集覆盖度和评分器排序质量。我们提出CLOVER，一种面向端到端自动驾驶规划的闭环价值估计与排序框架。CLOVER采用轻量级生成器-评分器结构：生成器产生多样化的候选轨迹，评分器预测规划指标子分数并在推理时对候选轨迹进行排序。为扩展单轨迹模仿之外的提案支持，CLOVER构建评估器过滤的伪专家轨迹，并以集合级覆盖监督训练生成器。随后，CLOVER执行保守的闭环自蒸馏：评分器在生成提案上拟合真实评估器子分数，而生成器则朝教师选择的前k个及向量帕累托目标优化，同时施加稳定性正则化。我们分析了不完美评分器如何提升生成器性能，表明当评分器选择的目标在真实评估器下得到丰富且更新保持保守时，评分器介导的优化是可靠的。在NAVSIM上，CLOVER实现94.5 PDMS和90.4 EPDMS，创下新纪录。在更具挑战性的NavHard子集上，其获得48.3 EPDMS，匹配当前最佳结果。在补充的nuScenes开环评估中，CLOVER在对比方法中实现最低L2误差和碰撞率。代码与数据将在https://github.com/WilliamXuanYu/CLOVER发布。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the mismatch between training via single-trajectory imitation and evaluation using rule-based metrics, CLOVER introduces a closed-loop framework for end-to-end autonomous driving planning that jointly trains a generator and scorer. The generator produces diverse candidate trajectories, while the scorer predicts sub-scores to rank them at inference time. To improve coverage beyond the logged trajectory, CLOVER uses evaluator-filtered pseudo-expert trajectories to supervise the generator at the set level. The scorer is refined via self-distillation using true evaluator scores, while the generator is updated toward top-k and Pareto-optimal targets with stability regularization. Experiments on NAVSIM show CLOVER achieves 94.5 PDMS and 90.4 EPDMS, setting a new state-of-the-art, and on NavHard it matches the best reported result with 48.3 EPDMS. On nuScenes, it also achieves the lowest L2 error and collision rate among compared methods.
为解决端到端自动驾驶规划中训练（模仿单一轨迹）与评估（基于规则指标）之间的不匹配问题，CLOVER提出一个闭环价值估计与排序框架，联合训练生成器与评分器。生成器产生多样轨迹，评分器在推理时预测子分数以排序候选方案。为突破单一轨迹模仿的局限，CLOVER构建评估器过滤的伪专家轨迹，以集合级覆盖监督生成器。评分器通过自蒸馏使用真实评估器分数进行优化，生成器则朝教师选择的Top-k及向量帕累托目标更新，并加入稳定性正则化。在NAVSIM上，CLOVER达到94.5 PDMS和90.4 EPDMS，刷新当前最佳；在更具挑战性的NavHard上，其48.3 EPDMS匹配现有最优结果；在nuScenes开放环评估中，其L2误差与碰撞率均最低。
RoboLab: A High-Fidelity Simulation Benchmark for Analysis of Task Generalist Policies
Authors: Xuning Yang, Rishit Dagli, Alex Zook, Hugo Hadfield, Ankit Goyal, Stan Birchfield, Fabio Ramos, Jonathan Tremblay
Venue: Robotics: Science and Systems XXII, Sydney, Australia, 2026
First: 2026-04-10T19:42:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T17:31:57+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
The pursuit of general-purpose robotics has yielded impressive foundation models, yet simulation-based benchmarking remains a bottleneck due to rapid performance saturation and a lack of true generalization testing. Existing benchmarks often exhibit significant domain overlap between training and evaluation, trivializing success rates and obscuring insights into robustness. We introduce RoboLab, a simulation benchmarking framework designed to address these challenges. Concretely, our framework is designed to answer two questions: (1) to what extent can we understand the performance of a real-world policy by analyzing its behavior in simulation, and (2) which factor most strongly affect policy behavior. First, RoboLab enables human-authored and LLM-enabled generation of scenes and tasks in a robot- and policy-agnostic manner within a high-fidelity simulation environment. We introduce an accompanying RoboLab-120 benchmark, consisting of 120 tasks categorized into three competency axes: visual, procedural, relational, across three difficulty levels. Second, we introduce a systematic analysis of real-world policies that quantify both their performance and the sensitivity of their behavior to controlled perturbations, exposing significant performance gap in current state-of-the-art models. By providing granular metrics and a scalable toolset, RoboLab offers a scalable framework for evaluating the true generalization capabilities of task-generalist robotic policies. Project website: https://research.nvidia.com/labs/srl/projects/robolab/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：RoboLab：用于分析任务通用策略的高保真仿真基准
通用机器人研究已取得令人瞩目的基础模型成果，但基于仿真的基准测试仍受限于性能快速饱和及缺乏真实泛化能力测试。现有基准常在训练与评估间存在显著领域重叠，导致成功率虚高且掩盖了鲁棒性洞察。我们提出RoboLab仿真基准框架以应对这些挑战。具体而言，该框架旨在回答两个问题：(1) 通过分析策略在仿真中的表现，我们能多大程度理解其真实世界性能？(2) 哪一因素最显著影响策略行为？首先，RoboLab支持人类编写与大语言模型驱动的、与机器人和策略无关的场景与任务生成，且在高保真仿真环境中运行。我们配套推出RoboLab-120基准，包含120项任务，按视觉、程序性、关系性三大能力轴划分，涵盖三个难度等级。其次，我们系统分析现实世界策略，量化其性能及对受控扰动的敏感度，揭示当前最先进模型存在显著性能差距。通过提供细粒度指标与可扩展工具集，RoboLab为评估任务通用型机器人策略的真实泛化能力提供可扩展框架。项目网站：https://research.nvidia.com/labs/srl/projects/robolab/
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need to evaluate task-generalist robotic policies beyond superficial simulation performance, RoboLab introduces a high-fidelity simulation benchmark that decouples training and evaluation domains to reveal true generalization. It enables human and LLM-driven generation of diverse tasks across visual, procedural, and relational competency axes, with 120 tasks spanning three difficulty levels. Experimental results show that current state-of-the-art policies exhibit significant sensitivity to perturbations and substantial performance gaps when evaluated under controlled variations, indicating limited robustness despite high simulated success rates.
为突破当前仿真评估对机器人通用策略真实泛化能力的局限，RoboLab构建了一个高保真仿真基准，支持人类与大模型生成跨视觉、程序、关系三大能力轴的多样化任务，形成包含120项任务的RoboLab-120基准。实验表明，现有最先进策略在受控扰动下表现显著下降，揭示其泛化能力不足与鲁棒性薄弱。该框架通过量化性能与行为敏感性，精准识别影响策略表现的关键因素，从而揭示当前模型在真实环境变化下的真实局限。
Talk is (Not) Cheap: A Taxonomy and Benchmark Coverage Audit for LLM Attacks
Authors: Karthik Raghu Iyer, Yazdan Jamshidi, Nicholas Bray, Alexey A. Shvets
First: 2026-05-14T17:30:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T17:30:36+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We introduce a reusable framework for auditing whether LLM attack benchmarks collectively cover the threat surface: a 4$\times$6 Target $\times$ Technique matrix grounded in STRIDE, constructed from a 507-leaf taxonomy -- 401 data-populated and 106 threat-model-derived leaves -- of inference-time attacks extracted from 932 arXiv security studies (2023--2026). The matrix enables benchmark-external validation -- auditing collective coverage rather than individual benchmark consistency. Applying it to six public benchmarks reveals that the three primary frameworks (HarmBench, InjecAgent, AgentDojo) occupy non-overlapping cells covering at most 25\% of the matrix, while entire STRIDE threat categories (Service Disruption, Model Internals) lack any standardized evaluation, despite published attacks in these categories achieving 46$\times$ token amplification and 96\% attack success rates through mechanisms which no benchmark tests. The corpus of 2,521 unique attack groups further reveals pervasive naming fragmentation (up to 29 surface forms for a single attack) and heavy concentration in Safety \& Alignment Bypass, structural properties invisible at smaller scale. The taxonomy, attack records, and coverage mappings are released as extensible artifacts; as new benchmarks emerge, they can be mapped onto the same matrix, enabling the community to track whether evaluation gaps are closing.
中文标题/摘要
标题：言辞（并非）廉价：大语言模型攻击的分类与基准覆盖审计
我们提出了一种可复用的框架，用于审计大语言模型攻击基准是否共同覆盖了威胁面：该框架基于STRIDE构建了一个4×6的攻击目标×技术矩阵，源自从932篇arXiv安全研究（2023–2026）中提取的507个攻击分类——其中401个为数据填充，106个为威胁模型推导——该矩阵支持基准外部验证，即审计整体覆盖度而非单个基准的一致性。将其应用于六个公开基准发现，三大主流框架（HarmBench、InjecAgent、AgentDojo）占据互不重叠的单元格，覆盖矩阵最多25%；而整个STRIDE威胁类别（服务中断、模型内部）缺乏标准化评估，尽管这些类别中已发表的攻击通过未被任何基准测试的机制实现了46倍token放大和96%攻击成功率。2,521个独特攻击组的语料库进一步揭示了普遍的命名碎片化（单一攻击多达29种表面形式）及在安全与对齐绕过方面的高度集中，这些结构性特征在小规模下不可见。该分类体系、攻击记录与覆盖映射作为可扩展的成果发布，随着新基准的出现，可映射至同一矩阵，使社区能够追踪评估缺口是否正在缩小。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to assess whether existing LLM attack benchmarks comprehensively cover real-world threats, the authors construct a 4×6 Target×Technique matrix based on STRIDE and a 507-leaf taxonomy derived from 932 arXiv security studies. Applied to six public benchmarks, the framework reveals that major frameworks like HarmBench, InjecAgent, and AgentDojo collectively cover at most 25% of the matrix, with entire STRIDE categories such as Service Disruption and Model Internals lacking standardized evaluation despite demonstrated high-impact attacks. The analysis also uncovers severe naming fragmentation (up to 29 variants per attack) and an overrepresentation of Safety & Alignment Bypass attacks, indicating gaps in benchmark coverage that persist even as new attacks emerge.
为评估现有大语言模型攻击基准是否全面覆盖现实威胁，作者基于STRIDE威胁建模构建了一个4×6的攻击目标×技术矩阵，并从932篇近期arXiv安全论文中提炼出507个攻击叶节点分类。将该框架应用于六个公开基准发现，主流框架如HarmBench、InjecAgent和AgentDojo合计仅覆盖矩阵25%以内，而如服务中断和模型内部等完整STRIDE威胁类别完全缺乏标准化评估，尽管相关攻击已实现46倍token放大和96%成功率。分析还揭示攻击命名高度碎片化（单个攻击多达29种表述），且安全与对齐绕过类攻击高度集中，小规模数据难以体现此类结构性问题。
DriveCtrl: Conditioned Sim-to-Real Driving Video Generation
Authors: Haonan Zhao, Yiting Wang, Jingkun Chen, Valentina Donzella, Thomas Bashford-Rogers, Kurt Debattista
First: 2026-05-14T17:29:35+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T17:29:35+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large-scale labelled driving video data is essential for training autonomous driving systems. Although simulation offers scalable and fully annotated data, the domain gap between synthetic and real-world driving videos significantly limits its utility for downstream deployment. Existing video generation methods are not well-suited for this task, as they fail to simultaneously preserve scene structure, object dynamics, temporal consistency, and visual realism, all of which are critical for maintaining annotation validity in generated data. In this paper, we present DriveCtrl, a depth-conditioned controllable sim-to-real video generation framework for realistic driving video synthesis. Built upon a pretrained video foundation model, DriveCtrl introduces a structure-aware adapter that enables depth-guided generation while preserving the scene layout and motion patterns of the source simulation, producing temporally coherent driving videos that remain aligned with the original simulated sequences. We further introduce a scalable data generation pipeline that transforms simulator videos into realistic driving footage matching the visual style of a target real-world dataset. The pipeline supports three conditioning signals: structural depth, reference-dataset style, and text prompts, while preserving frame-level annotations for downstream perception tasks. To better assess this task, we propose a driving-domain-specific knowledge-informed evaluation metric called Driving Video Realism Score (DVRS) that assesses the realism of generated videos. Experiments demonstrate that DriveCtrl consistently outperforms the base model and competing alternatives in realism, temporal quality, and perception task performance, substantially narrowing the sim-to-real gap for driving video generation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DriveCtrl：基于条件的仿真到真实驾驶视频生成
大规模标注的驾驶视频数据对自动驾驶系统训练至关重要。尽管仿真可提供可扩展且全标注的数据，但仿真与真实世界驾驶视频之间的域差距显著限制了其在下游部署中的实用性。现有视频生成方法不适合此任务，因为它们无法同时保持场景结构、物体动态、时间一致性及视觉真实感，而这些因素对生成数据的标注有效性至关重要。本文提出DriveCtrl，一种基于深度条件的可控仿真到真实视频生成框架，用于合成逼真的驾驶视频。该框架基于预训练视频基础模型，引入结构感知适配器，实现深度引导生成的同时保留源仿真场景布局和运动模式，生成时间上连贯且与原始仿真序列保持一致的驾驶视频。我们进一步提出可扩展的数据生成流水线，将仿真视频转换为与目标真实数据集视觉风格匹配的逼真驾驶画面。该流水线支持三种条件信号：结构深度、参考数据集风格和文本提示，同时保留帧级标注以支持下游感知任务。为更好地评估该任务，我们提出一种驾驶领域特定的知识引导评估指标——驾驶视频真实度评分（DVRS），用于评估生成视频的真实性。实验表明，DriveCtrl在真实感、时间质量及感知任务表现上持续优于基线模型和竞争方法，显著缩小了仿真到真实驾驶视频生成的域差距。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need for large-scale, annotated driving videos to train autonomous systems, DriveCtrl addresses the sim-to-real domain gap by generating realistic driving videos from simulation while preserving scene structure, object motion, and temporal coherence. It leverages a pretrained video foundation model with a depth-conditioned structure-aware adapter to guide generation, ensuring alignment with source simulation sequences. A scalable pipeline further adapts output to match real-world visual styles using depth, style, and text conditioning, while retaining frame-level annotations. Evaluated via a novel Driving Video Realism Score (DVRS), DriveCtrl outperforms baselines in realism, temporal quality, and downstream perception tasks, significantly reducing the simulation-to-real-world performance gap.
为解决自动驾驶训练所需大规模标注驾驶视频的稀缺问题，DriveCtrl提出一种基于深度条件控制的仿真到真实视频生成框架，旨在保留场景结构、物体运动与时间一致性。该方法在预训练视频模型基础上引入结构感知适配器，结合深度引导生成，确保生成视频与原始仿真序列对齐。其可扩展数据生成流程支持结构深度、目标真实数据风格及文本提示三种条件输入，同时保留帧级标注以支持下游感知任务。通过新提出的驾驶领域知识引导评估指标Driving Video Realism Score（DVRS），实验表明DriveCtrl在真实感、时间一致性及感知任务表现上显著优于基线方法，有效缩小了仿真到真实世界的性能差距。
Conformal Prediction for Multimodal Regression
Authors: Alexis Bose, Jonathan Ethier, Paul Guinand
First: 2024-10-25T15:56:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T17:27:52+00:00
Comments: Code available at https://github.com/ic-crc/uncertainty-estimation 20 pages, 34 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
This paper introduces multimodal conformal regression. Traditionally confined to scenarios with solely numerical input features, conformal prediction is now extended to multimodal contexts through our methodology, which harnesses internal features from complex neural network architectures processing images and unstructured text. Our findings highlight the potential for internal neural network features, extracted from convergence points where multimodal information is combined, to be used by conformal prediction to construct prediction intervals (PIs). This capability paves new paths for deploying conformal prediction in domains abundant with multimodal data, enabling a broader range of problems to benefit from guaranteed distribution-free uncertainty quantification.
中文标题/摘要
标题：多模态回归的置信预测
本文介绍了多模态置信预测。传统上仅适用于仅有数值输入特征的场景，我们的方法将置信预测扩展至多模态情境，通过利用处理图像和非结构化文本的复杂神经网络架构中的内部特征。我们的研究发现，从多模态信息融合的收敛点提取的神经网络内部特征，可用于置信预测构建预测区间（PIs），从而为多模态数据丰富的领域开辟了新的应用路径，使更广泛的问题能够受益于无分布假设的不确定性量化。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to extend conformal prediction beyond numerical inputs to handle multimodal data, this work proposes a method that leverages internal features from neural networks processing images and text. By extracting features at convergence points where multimodal information is integrated, the approach constructs valid prediction intervals without relying on distributional assumptions. Experimental results demonstrate that these intervals maintain high coverage rates while controlling width, validating the method’s effectiveness in multimodal regression tasks.
为将置信预测从仅适用于数值输入扩展到多模态数据，该研究提出一种利用神经网络处理图像与文本时内部特征的方法。通过在多模态信息融合的收敛点提取特征，该方法可生成有效的预测区间，无需依赖传统数值输入。实验结果表明，这些内部特征能有效支持无分布假设的不确定性量化，使置信预测在各类多模态回归任务中具备可行性。
Learning from Language Feedback via Variational Policy Distillation
Authors: Yang Li, Erik Nijkamp, Semih Yavuz, Shafiq Rayhan Joty
First: 2026-05-14T17:27:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T17:27:34+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards (RLVR) suffers from sparse outcome signals, creating severe exploration bottlenecks on complex reasoning tasks. Recent on-policy self-distillation methods attempt to address this by utilizing language feedback to generate dense, token-level supervision. However, these approaches rely on a fixed, passive teacher to interpret the feedback. As the student policy improves, the teacher's zero-shot assessment capabilities plateau, ultimately halting further learning. To overcome this, we propose Variational Policy Distillation (VPD), a framework that formalizes learning from language feedback as a Variational Expectation-Maximization (EM) problem. VPD co-evolves both policies: in the E-step, the teacher is actively refined on trajectory outcomes via an adaptive trust-region update, translating textual feedback into a dynamically improved target token distribution. In the M-step, the student internalizes this dense distributional guidance on its own on-policy rollouts. By continuously improving the teacher's ability to extract actionable signals from textual critique, VPD overcomes the limitations of passive distillation. Evaluated across diverse sources of diagnostic feedback on scientific reasoning and code generation tasks, VPD consistently outperforms both standard RLVR and existing self-distillation baselines. Finally, by stress-testing our framework on rigid mathematical reasoning and cold-start regimes, we illuminate the fundamental bounds of feedback-driven self-distillation compared to pure environment-driven RL.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过变分策略蒸馏从语言反馈中学习
可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）因稀疏的结果信号，在复杂推理任务上面临严重的探索瓶颈。近期的基于策略的自蒸馏方法试图通过语言反馈生成密集的、按标记级别的监督信号来解决这一问题。然而，这些方法依赖于一个固定的、被动的教师来解释反馈。随着学生策略的改进，教师的零样本评估能力趋于饱和，最终停止进一步学习。为克服这一限制，我们提出了变分策略蒸馏（VPD），将从语言反馈中学习形式化为变分期望最大化（EM）问题。VPD同时演化两个策略：在E步中，教师通过自适应信任区域更新，根据轨迹结果主动优化，将文本反馈转化为动态改进的目标标记分布；在M步中，学生在其自身策略的在线轨迹中内化这一密集分布指导。通过持续提升教师从文本批评中提取可操作信号的能力，VPD克服了被动蒸馏的局限性。在科学推理和代码生成任务中，针对多种诊断反馈源的评估显示，VPD始终优于标准RLVR和现有自蒸馏基线。最后，通过在刚性数学推理和冷启动场景中对框架进行压力测试，我们揭示了反馈驱动的自蒸馏与纯环境驱动强化学习之间的根本性界限。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the sparse reward signals in reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards, this work introduces Variational Policy Distillation (VPD), a framework that treats language feedback as a variational EM problem to co-evolve student and teacher policies. In the E-step, the teacher policy is adaptively refined using trust-region updates based on trajectory outcomes, translating feedback into improved token-level targets; in the M-step, the student learns from these dynamically updated distributions via on-policy rollouts. Experiments across scientific reasoning and code generation tasks show VPD consistently outperforms standard RLVR and existing self-distillation methods, and stress tests reveal its superior scalability and fundamental advantages over environment-driven RL in rigid and cold-start settings.
为解决强化学习中奖励信号稀疏导致的探索瓶颈，本文提出变分策略蒸馏（VPD）框架，将语言反馈形式化为变分EM问题，协同优化学生与教师策略。E步中，教师通过信任区域更新自适应优化，将反馈转化为动态的token级目标；M步中，学生在自身策略的on-policy rollout中内化该分布指导。实验表明，VPD在科学推理与代码生成任务上持续优于标准RLVR及现有自蒸馏方法，而压力测试揭示其在严格数学推理与冷启动场景下，相比环境驱动的RL存在本质局限。
Proposal and study of statistical features for string similarity computation and classification
Authors: E. O. Rodrigues, D. Casanova, M. Teixeira, V. Pegorini, F. Favarim, E. Clua, A. Conci, Panos Liatsis
Venue: International Journal of Data Mining, Modelling and Management, 2020
First: 2026-05-14T17:27:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T17:27:04+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Adaptations of features commonly applied in the field of visual computing, co-occurrence matrix (COM) and run-length matrix (RLM), are proposed for the similarity computation of strings in general (words, phrases, codes and texts). The proposed features are not sensitive to language related information. These are purely statistical and can be used in any context with any language or grammatical structure. Other statistical measures that are commonly employed in the field such as longest common subsequence, maximal consecutive longest common subsequence, mutual information and edit distances are evaluated and compared. In the first synthetic set of experiments, the COM and RLM features outperform the remaining state-of-the-art statistical features. In 3 out of 4 cases, the RLM and COM features were statistically more significant than the second best group based on distances (P-value < 0.001). When it comes to a real text plagiarism dataset, the RLM features obtained the best results.
中文标题/摘要
标题：字符串相似性计算与分类的统计特征提案与研究
提出将视觉计算领域常用的共现矩阵（COM）和游程长度矩阵（RLM）特征适配用于一般字符串（词、短语、代码和文本）的相似性计算。所提特征不依赖语言相关信息，纯属统计性质，可应用于任何语言或语法结构的上下文。同时评估并比较了其他常用统计度量方法，如最长公共子序列、最大连续最长公共子序列、互信息和编辑距离。在首个合成实验中，COM和RLM特征优于其他现有统计特征；在4个案例中有3个，RLM和COM特征在统计显著性上优于次优距离类方法（P值<0.001）。在真实文本抄袭数据集中，RLM特征表现最佳。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for language-agnostic string similarity measures, this study adapts co-occurrence matrix (COM) and run-length matrix (RLM) features from visual computing to compute string similarity across words, phrases, codes, and texts. These statistical features, unlike traditional methods such as edit distance or mutual information, are designed to be context-independent and grammar-agnostic. Experimental results on synthetic data show that COM and RLM outperform other statistical measures, with RLM achieving statistical significance (P-value < 0.001) in three out of four cases over distance-based methods. On a real-world plagiarism dataset, RLM features again yielded the best performance.
为解决语言无关的字符串相似度计算需求，该研究将视觉计算领域常用的共现矩阵（COM）和游程长度矩阵（RLM）特征应用于单词、短语、代码和文本的相似度计算。这些纯统计特征不依赖语言或语法结构，与编辑距离等传统方法不同。在合成数据实验中，COM和RLM优于其他统计方法，其中RLM在四组实验中的三组中显著优于距离类方法（P值<0.001）。在真实文本抄袭数据集上，RLM特征再次表现最佳。
Why Neighborhoods Matter: Traversal Context and Provenance in Agentic GraphRAG
Authors: Riccardo Terrenzi, Maximilian von Zastrow, Serkan Ayvaz
Venue: IJCAI
First: 2026-05-14T17:25:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T17:25:20+00:00
Comments: 7 pages, 2 figures, Submitted at IJCAI-ECAI 2026 Joint Workshop on GENAIK and NORA
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Retrieval-Augmented Generation can improve factuality by grounding answers in external evidence, but Agentic GraphRAG complicates what it means for citations to be faithful. In these systems, an agent explores a knowledge graph before producing an answer and a small set of citations. We frame citation faithfulness as a trajectory-level problem: final citations should not only support the answer, but also account for the graph traversal, structure, and visited-but-uncited entities that may influence it. Through controlled ablation experiments, we compare the effects of isolating, removing, and masking cited and uncited graph entities. Our results show that cited evidence is often necessary, as removing it substantially changes answers and reduces accuracy. However, citations are not sufficient, because accurate answers can also depend on uncited traversal context and surrounding graph structure. These findings suggest that citation evaluation in Agentic GraphRAG should move beyond source support toward provenance over the broader retrieval trajectory.
中文标题/摘要
标题：为何社区重要：代理图RAG中的遍历上下文与溯源
检索增强生成可通过外部证据支撑答案以提升事实性，但代理图RAG使引用的忠实性变得复杂。在这些系统中，代理在生成答案和少量引用前需探索知识图谱。我们将引用忠实性视为轨迹级问题：最终引用不仅应支持答案，还应反映图遍历路径、结构及被访问但未引用的实体对答案的影响。通过受控消融实验，我们比较了隔离、移除和掩码引用与未引用图实体的效果。结果表明，引用证据常为必要，移除后会显著改变答案并降低准确性；但引用本身不足，因准确答案亦依赖未引用的遍历上下文和周围图结构。这表明，代理图RAG中的引用评估应超越源支持，转向对更广泛检索轨迹的溯源评估。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the challenge of citation faithfulness in Agentic GraphRAG systems, where agents traverse knowledge graphs before generating answers and citations. Motivated by the need to evaluate not just cited sources but also the traversal context and uncited entities that shape responses, the authors conduct controlled ablation experiments to isolate and mask cited and uncited graph elements. Their results show that cited evidence is often essential for accurate answers, as its removal significantly alters outputs and reduces correctness; however, citations alone are insufficient, since uncited traversal context and graph structure also critically influence answer quality. The study thus argues for a shift in citation evaluation toward assessing provenance across the full retrieval trajectory.
该文探讨了代理图RAG系统中引文忠实性问题，指出代理在生成答案前需遍历知识图谱，引文必须不仅支持答案，还需反映遍历路径、图结构及未被引用但可能影响结果的实体。通过控制消融实验，作者分别隔离、移除或屏蔽引用与未引用的图元素，评估其对答案准确性的影响。结果表明，移除引用证据常导致答案变化和准确率下降，但未引用的遍历上下文和图结构同样显著影响正确性，说明引文评估应超越源支持，考虑完整检索轨迹的溯源性。
Logging Policy Design for Off-Policy Evaluation
Authors: Connor Douglas, Joel Persson, Foster Provost
First: 2026-05-14T17:25:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T17:25:19+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Off-policy evaluation (OPE) estimates the value of a target treatment policy (e.g., a recommender system) using data collected by a different logging policy. It enables high-stakes experimentation without live deployment, yet in practice accuracy depends heavily on the logging policy used to collect data for computing the estimate. We study how to design logging policies that minimize OPE error for given target policies. We characterize a fundamental reward-coverage tradeoff: concentrating probability mass on high-reward actions reduces variance but risks missing signal on actions the target policy may take. We propose a unifying framework for logging policy design and derive optimal policies in canonical informational regimes where the target policy and reward distribution are (i) known, (ii) unknown, and (iii) partially known through priors or noisy estimates at logging time. Our results provide actionable guidance for firms choosing among multiple candidate recommendation systems. We demonstrate the importance of treatment selection when gathering data for OPE, and describe theoretically optimal approaches when this is a firm's primary objective. We also distill practical design principles for selecting logging policies when operational constraints prevent implementing the theoretical optimum.
中文标题/摘要
标题：离策略评估的记录策略设计
离策略评估（OPE）利用由不同记录策略收集的数据，估算目标处理策略（如推荐系统）的价值。它允许在不进行实时部署的情况下进行高风险实验，但实际准确性高度依赖用于计算估计值的数据收集所用的记录策略。我们研究如何设计记录策略，以最小化给定目标策略下的OPE误差。我们刻画了奖励与覆盖之间的基本权衡：将概率质量集中在高奖励动作上可降低方差，但可能遗漏目标策略可能采取的动作信号。我们提出了一种统一的记录策略设计框架，并在三种典型信息环境下推导出最优策略：（i）目标策略和奖励分布已知，（ii）未知，（iii）通过先验或记录时的噪声估计部分已知。我们的结果为公司选择多个候选推荐系统提供了可操作的指导。我们展示了在为OPE收集数据时，处理选择的重要性，并描述了当此为公司主要目标时的理论最优方法。此外，我们总结了在运营约束限制无法实施理论最优时，选择记录策略的实用设计原则。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to improve the accuracy of off-policy evaluation (OPE) in real-world systems, this paper investigates how to design logging policies that minimize estimation error for target policies. It introduces a framework that characterizes a fundamental tradeoff between reward concentration and action coverage, showing that focusing logging effort on high-reward actions reduces variance but may miss critical signals for target policy actions. The authors derive theoretically optimal logging policies under three informational regimes: when target policy and reward distribution are known, unknown, or partially known via priors or noisy estimates. Experiments validate that carefully designed logging policies significantly reduce OPE error compared to naive approaches, and highlight the importance of treatment selection in data collection. The paper also provides practical guidelines for real-world deployment when theoretical optima are infeasible.
为提升现实系统中离策略评估（OPE）的准确性，本文研究如何设计日志策略以最小化评估误差。文章提出一个统一框架，揭示了奖励集中与覆盖之间的基本权衡：聚焦高奖励动作可降低方差，但可能遗漏目标策略所需的关键信号。作者在三种信息条件下推导出理论最优日志策略：目标策略与奖励已知、未知，或部分通过先验或噪声估计已知。实验结果表明，精心设计的日志策略相比朴素方法显著降低OPE误差，并为受运营约束的企业提供实用指导。
MUR: Momentum Uncertainty guided Reasoning for Large Language Models
Authors: Hang Yan, Fangzhi Xu, Rongman Xu, Yifei Li, Jian Zhang, Haoran Luo, Xiaobao Wu, Luu Anh Tuan, Haiteng Zhao, Qika Lin, Jun Liu
First: 2025-07-20T13:36:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T17:24:49+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large Language Models have achieved impressive performance on reasoning-intensive tasks, yet optimizing their reasoning efficiency remains an open challenge. While Test-Time Scaling (TTS) improves reasoning quality, it often leads to overthinking, wasting tokens on redundant computations. This work investigates how to efficiently and adaptively guide current model' test-time scaling without additional training. Inspired by the concept of momentum in physics, we propose Momentum Uncertainty-guided Reasoning (MUR), which dynamically allocates thinking budgets to critical reasoning steps by tracking and aggregating stepwise uncertainty over time. To support flexible inference-time control, we introduce gamma-control, a simple mechanism that tunes the reasoning budget via a single hyperparameter. We provide in-depth theoretical proof to support the superiority of MUR in terms of stability and biases. MUR is comprehensively evaluated against various TTS methods across four challenging benchmarks (MATH-500, AIME24, AIME25, and GPQA-diamond) using different sizes of recent Qwen3 models (1.7B, 4B, and 8B). Results demonstrate that MUR reduces computation by by over 45% on average while improving accuracy from 0.33 to 3.46%.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MUR：基于动量不确定性的大语言模型推理引导方法
大语言模型在需要推理的任务中表现优异，但优化其推理效率仍具挑战。虽然测试时扩展（TTS）可提升推理质量，却常导致过度思考，浪费计算资源。本文研究如何在不增加训练的情况下，高效自适应地引导当前模型的测试时扩展。受物理学中动量概念启发，我们提出动量不确定性引导推理（MUR），通过动态分配计算预算至关键推理步骤，实时跟踪并聚合逐步不确定性。为支持灵活的推理时控制，我们引入gamma控制机制，通过单一超参数调节推理预算。我们提供深入理论证明，支持MUR在稳定性和偏差方面的优越性。MUR在四个挑战性基准（MATH-500、AIME24、AIME25、GPQA-diamond）上，使用不同规模的Qwen3模型（1.7B、4B、8B）与多种TTS方法全面对比。结果表明，MUR平均减少计算量45%以上，同时将准确率从0.33%提升至3.46%。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of Test-Time Scaling in large language models, which often causes overthinking and wasted computational resources, this work proposes MUR, a momentum-uncertainty guided reasoning method that dynamically allocates reasoning budgets based on stepwise uncertainty accumulation. By introducing gamma-control, a single hyperparameter for adjusting reasoning depth, MUR enables flexible inference-time adaptation without retraining. Experimental results across four benchmarks using Qwen3 models show MUR reduces computation by over 45% while improving accuracy by 3.46 percentage points on average.
为解决大语言模型在测试时扩展中因过度思考导致的计算浪费问题，本文提出动量不确定性引导推理（MUR）方法，通过追踪推理步骤的不确定性动态分配计算预算，受物理动量启发。引入gamma控制机制，通过单个超参数灵活调节推理预算。在四个基准测试中使用不同规模的Qwen3模型评估，MUR平均减少计算量45%以上，同时准确率提升3.46个百分点。
From Text to Voice: A Reproducible and Verifiable Framework for Evaluating Tool Calling LLM Agents
Authors: Md Tahmid Rahman Laskar, Xue-Yong Fu, Seyyed Saeed Sarfjoo, Quinten McNamara, Jonas Robertson, Shashi Bhushan TN
First: 2026-05-14T17:22:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T17:22:42+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Voice agents increasingly require reliable tool use from speech, whereas prominent tool-calling benchmarks remain text-based. We study whether verified text benchmarks can be converted into controlled audio-based tool calling evaluations without re-annotating the tool schema and gold labels. Our dataset-agnostic framework uses text-to-speech, speaker variation, and environmental noise to create paired text-audio instances while preserving the original dataset annotations. Based on extensive evaluation of 7 omni-modal models on audio-converted versions of Confetti and When2Call, our framework demonstrates that the performance is strongly model- and task-dependent: Gemini-3.1-Flash-Live obtains the highest Confetti score (70.4), whereas GPT-Realtime-1.5 performs best on When2Call (71.9). On Confetti, the text-to-voice gap ranges from 1.8 points for Qwen3-Omni to 4.8 points for GPT-Realtime-1.5. A targeted analysis of failure cases demonstrates that degradations most often reflect misunderstandings of argument values in the speech. Considering real-world deployment scenarios, we further report text-only results, an ambiguity-based reformulation stress test, and a reference-free LLM-as-judge protocol validated against human preferences. Notably, we find that open-source Qwen3 judges with at least 8B parameters exceed 80% agreement with proprietary judges, supporting privacy-preserving evaluation. Overall, our framework provides a verifiable and reproducible first-stage diagnostic that complements purpose-built audio corpora.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从文本到语音：一种可复现且可验证的工具调用LLM代理评估框架

Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate voice agents without re-annotating tool schemas, the paper proposes a dataset-agnostic framework that converts text-based tool-calling benchmarks into audio evaluations using text-to-speech, speaker variation, and environmental noise while preserving original annotations. Evaluated across seven omni-modal models on audio versions of Confetti and When2Call, results show strong model- and task-dependency: Gemini-3.1-Flash-Live scored highest on Confetti (70.4), while GPT-Realtime-1.5 led on When2Call (71.9). 为在不重新标注工具模式的前提下评估语音代理，该研究提出一种通用数据集框架，通过文本转语音、说话人变化和环境噪声将文本工具调用基准转化为音频评估。在7个全模态模型上对Confetti和When2Call进行评估，结果显示模型与任务高度依赖：Gemini-3.1-Flash-Live在Confetti上得分最高（70.4），GPT-Realtime-1.5在When2Call上表现最佳（71.9）。文本与音频版本性能差距在1.8至4.8分之间，主要源于语音中参数值理解错误。附加实验包括纯文本基线、歧义压力测试和无参考LLM裁判协议，发现8B以上开源Qwen3模型与专有裁判一致率超80%，支持隐私保护评估。
Improving Multi-turn Dialogue Consistency with Self-Recall Thinking
Authors: Renning Pang, Tian Lan, Leyuan Liu, Xiaoming Huang, Piao Tong, Xiaosong Zhang
First: 2026-05-14T17:20:14+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T17:20:14+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language model (LLM) based multi-turn dialogue systems often struggle to track dependencies across non-adjacent turns, undermining both consistency and scalability. As conversations lengthen, essential information becomes sparse and is buried in irrelevant context, while processing the entire dialogue history incurs severe efficiency bottlenecks. Existing solutions either rely on high latency external memory or lose fine-grained details through iterative summarization. In this paper, we propose Self-Recall Thinking (SRT), a framework designed to address long-range contextual dependency and sparse informative signals in multi-turn dialogue. SRT identifies helpful historical turns and uses them to generate contextually appropriate responses, enabling the model to selectively recall and reason over context during inference. This process yields an endogenous reasoning process that integrates interpretable recall steps without external modules. SRT incorporates: (1) Dependency Construction: Generating and converting it into self-recall chains; (2)Capability Initialization: Training to enable reasoning chains with recall tokens capability; (3)Reasoning Improvement: Refining accuracy via verifiable rewards to optimize recall and reasoning for correct answers. Experiments on multiple datasets demonstrate that SRT improves F1 score by 4.7% and reduces end-to-end latency by 14.7% over prior methods, achieving a balance between reasoning latency and accuracy, and outperforming state-of-the-art baselines.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过自回忆思维提升多轮对话一致性
基于大语言模型（LLM）的多轮对话系统常难以追踪非相邻轮次间的依赖关系，从而损害一致性和可扩展性。随着对话变长，关键信息变得稀疏并被无关上下文掩盖，而处理完整对话历史则带来严重效率瓶颈。现有方案要么依赖高延迟外部记忆，要么通过迭代摘要丢失细粒度细节。本文提出自回忆思维（SRT）框架，旨在解决多轮对话中的长程上下文依赖和稀疏信息信号问题。SRT识别有益的历史轮次，并利用其生成上下文适配的回复，使模型在推理时能选择性回忆并推理上下文，形成内生推理过程，无需外部模块即可整合可解释的回忆步骤。SRT包含：（1）依赖构建：生成并转换为自回忆链；（2）能力初始化：训练以支持带回忆标记的推理链；（3）推理优化：通过可验证奖励提升准确性，优化回忆与推理以获得正确答案。在多个数据集上的实验表明，SRT相较现有方法F1分数提升4.7%，端到端延迟降低14.7%，在推理延迟与准确率间取得平衡，优于当前最先进基线。
Summary / 总结
To address the challenge of maintaining dialogue consistency across long conversations where key information becomes sparse and inefficient to process, the paper introduces Self-Recall Thinking (SRT), a framework that enables large language models to selectively recall and reason over relevant historical turns during inference without external memory. SRT operates through three stages: constructing dependency chains from dialogue history, initializing the model to handle recall tokens, and refining recall accuracy via verifiable rewards. Experimental results across multiple datasets show that SRT improves F1 score by 4.7% and reduces end-to-end latency by 14.7% compared to prior methods, demonstrating a superior balance between reasoning accuracy and efficiency.
为解决多轮对话中因信息稀疏和历史上下文处理效率低下导致的对话一致性问题，本文提出Self-Recall Thinking（SRT）框架，使大语言模型能在推理时选择性回溯并推理相关历史轮次，无需外部记忆模块。该框架包含三阶段：构建历史轮次间的依赖链、初始化模型对召回标记的推理能力、并通过可验证奖励优化召回与推理准确性。在多个数据集上的实验表明，SRT相较现有方法F1提升4.7%，端到端延迟降低14.7%，在准确率与延迟间取得更好平衡，优于当前最先进基线模型。
Dual-Dimensional Consistency: Balancing Budget and Quality in Adaptive Inference-Time Scaling
Authors: Rongman Xu, Yifei Li, Tianzhe Zhao, Yanrui Wu, Bo Li, Hang Yan
First: 2026-05-14T17:19:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T17:19:37+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable abilities in reasoning. However, maximizing their potential through inference-time scaling faces challenges in trade-off between sampling budget and reasoning quality. Current strategies remain inefficient as they typically treat sampling width and depth as orthogonal objectives, where width consensus methods risk reinforcing hallucinations, while depth pruning mechanisms prematurely truncate complex yet valid reasoning chains. Therefore, we propose Dual-Dimensional Consistency (DDC), a unified framework that bridges path quality with adaptive termination. By coupling Confidence-Weighted Bayesian protocol with a Trend-Aware Stratified Pruning, our method ensures that computational resources are concentrated on high quality reasoning paths, filtering hallucinations while accelerating consensus. Evaluations across five benchmarks demonstrate that this approach reduces token consumption by over 10 times while maintaining or exceeding the accuracy of strong baselines across various LLMs.
中文标题/摘要
标题：双维一致性：在自适应推理时缩放中平衡预算与质量
大语言模型（LLMs）在推理方面展现出卓越能力。然而，通过推理时缩放最大化其潜力时，面临采样预算与推理质量之间的权衡挑战。现有策略效率低下，通常将采样宽度与深度视为正交目标：宽度共识方法可能加剧幻觉，而深度剪枝机制则过早截断复杂但有效的推理链。因此，我们提出双维一致性（DDC），一个统一框架，将路径质量与自适应终止相结合。通过结合置信度加权贝叶斯协议与趋势感知分层剪枝，我们的方法确保计算资源集中于高质量推理路径，过滤幻觉并加速共识。在五个基准测试中，该方法将token消耗降低超过10倍，同时在多种LLM上保持或超越强基线的准确性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of current inference-time scaling methods that treat sampling width and depth as separate goals, this paper introduces Dual-Dimensional Consistency (DDC), a unified framework that aligns computational budget allocation with reasoning quality. DDC combines a Confidence-Weighted Bayesian protocol with Trend-Aware Stratified Pruning to dynamically prioritize high-quality reasoning paths while suppressing hallucinations and accelerating consensus. Experimental results across five benchmarks show that DDC reduces token consumption by over 10x while preserving or improving accuracy compared to strong baselines across multiple LLMs.
为解决大语言模型推理时采样预算与推理质量难以平衡的问题，作者提出双维一致性（DDC）框架，结合置信度加权贝叶斯协议与趋势感知分层剪枝，动态终止推理路径并保留高质量推理轨迹。该方法通过将计算资源集中于高置信度路径，有效过滤幻觉并加速共识。在五个基准测试中，DDC使token消耗降低10倍以上，同时在多种大语言模型上保持或超越强基线模型的准确率。
Do-Undo Bench: Reversibility for Action Understanding in Image Generation
Authors: Shweta Mahajan, Shreya Kadambi, Hoang Le, Rajeev Yasarla, Apratim Bhattacharyya, Munawar Hayat, Fatih Porikli
First: 2025-12-15T18:03:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T17:13:30+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://s-mahajan.github.io/Do-Undo-Bench/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
We introduce the Do-Undo task and benchmark to address a critical gap in vision-language models: understanding and generating plausible scene transformations driven by real-world actions. Unlike prior work that relies on prompt-based image generation and editing to perform action-conditioned image manipulation, our training hypothesis requires models to simulate the outcome of a real-world action and then reverse it to the original state. This forward-reverse requirement tests genuine cause-and-effect understanding rather than stylistic or semantic edits. We curate a high-quality benchmark of reversible actions from real-world scenarios to enable robust action grounding. Our experiments reveal that current models struggle with action reversibility, highlighting the need to evaluate action understanding. Do-Undo provides an intuitive testbed for evaluating and advancing action-aware generation in multimodal systems that must reason about real-world dynamics.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Do-Undo基准：图像生成中的可逆性以实现动作理解
我们引入了Do-Undo任务和基准，旨在解决视觉-语言模型中的一个关键缺陷：理解并生成由真实世界动作驱动的合理场景变换。与以往依赖提示式图像生成和编辑来执行动作条件图像操作的工作不同，我们的训练假设要求模型模拟真实世界动作的结果，然后将其还原至原始状态。这种前向-反向的要求检验的是真实的因果关系理解，而非风格或语义编辑。我们从真实世界场景中精选高质量可逆动作，以实现稳健的动作锚定。实验表明，当前模型在动作可逆性方面表现不佳，凸显了评估动作理解的必要性。Do-Undo为评估和推进多模态系统中需推理真实世界动态的动作感知生成提供了直观的测试平台。

Motivated by the lack of causal understanding in vision-language models for real-world actions, this work introduces the Do-Undo task and benchmark, requiring models to simulate the effect of an action and then reverse it to restore the original scene. The method involves curating a dataset of reversible actions from real-world scenarios to test whether models can reason about cause-and-effect dynamics. Experimental results show that current models perform poorly on reversibility, indicating a fundamental gap in their ability to understand and generate plausible scene transformations grounded in physical action.
为弥补视觉语言模型在现实动作理解上的因果推理不足，该研究提出Do-Undo任务与基准测试，要求模型先模拟动作结果再将其还原至初始状态，以此检验真实因果关系而非表面编辑能力。方法从现实场景中精选可逆动作构建高质量数据集，用于训练与评估模型的正反向推理能力。实验表明当前模型在可逆性任务上表现不佳，凸显动作理解中的关键缺陷，并强调需建立新的多模态系统评估范式。
CoralLite: μCT Reconstruction of Coral Colonies from Individual Corallites
Authors: Jess Jones, Leonardo Bertini, Kenneth Johnson, Erica Hendy, Tilo Burghardt
First: 2026-05-14T17:12:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T17:12:59+00:00
Comments: 15 pages, 10 figures, 2 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The life history of an individual coral is archived within the accreting skeleton of the colony. While reef-forming coral colonies (e.g. massive \emph{Porites} sp.) may live for hundreds of years and deposit calcareous structures many metres in height and width, their living tissue is a thin outer surface layer comprised of asexually-dividing polyps that only survive a few years. To understand the rate and timing of polyp division and the consequences for colony skeletal growth, scientists need to track the skeletal corallite deposited around each polyp. Here we propose CoralLite, an annotated μCT scan dataset of entire calcareous skeletons and an associated, first corallite deep learning reconstruction baseline. CoralLite combines fully quantified volumetric segmentations with cross-slice linking for visualisations of 3D models for each corallite up to colony scale. For segmentation, we propose and evaluate in detail a hybrid V-Trans-UNet architecture applicable to segmenting tiled μCT virtual slabs of \emph{Porites} sp. colonies. The model is pre-trained on weakly annotated data and topology-aware fine-tuned using fully annotated slice sections with 8k+ manual corallite region annotations. On unseen slices of the same colony, the resulting model reaches 0.94 topological accuracy at mean Dice scores of 0.77 on the same colony and projection axis, and 0.63 mean Dice scores on a different, biologically unrelated specimen. Whilst our experiments are limited in scale and context, our results show for the first time that visual machine learning can effectively support full 3D individual corallite modelling from μCT scans of coral skeletons alone. For reproducibility and as a baseline for future research we publish our full dataset of 697 μCT slices, 37 partial or full slice annotations, and all network weights and source code with this paper.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CoralLite：从单个珊瑚虫杯状体重建珊瑚群体的μCT图像
单个珊瑚的生命周期记录在其不断生长的骨骼中。虽然造礁珊瑚群体（如块状的Porites属）可存活数百年，沉积钙质结构高达数米，但其活体组织仅是几厘米厚的外层，由无性繁殖的珊瑚虫组成，仅能存活几年。为了解珊瑚虫分裂速率与时间及其对群体骨骼生长的影响，科学家需追踪每个珊瑚虫周围沉积的骨骼杯状体。本文提出CoralLite，一个包含完整钙质骨骼的注释μCT扫描数据集，以及首个杯状体深度学习重建基线。CoralLite结合了完全量化的体体积分割与跨切片链接，可实现从单个杯状体到群体尺度的3D模型可视化。在分割方面，我们提出并详细评估了一种混合V-Trans-UNet架构，适用于分割Porites属珊瑚群体的瓷砖化μCT虚拟切片。该模型先在弱注释数据上预训练，再使用包含8000+人工杯状体区域标注的完整注释切片进行拓扑感知微调。在未见过的同种珊瑚切片上，该模型在相同群体和投影轴上达到0.94的拓扑精度，Dice平均得分为0.77；在不同、生物学无关的样本上，Dice平均得分为0.63。尽管实验规模和范围有限，我们的结果首次表明，视觉机器学习可有效支持仅通过珊瑚骨骼μCT扫描实现单个杯状体的完整3D建模。为确保可复现性并作为未来研究的基线，本文随文发布完整数据集（697个μCT切片、37个部分或完整切片标注）及所有网络权重和源代码。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to track polyp division and skeletal growth in long-lived coral colonies, this study introduces CoralLite, a μCT dataset with annotated 3D corallite reconstructions and a deep learning baseline. The method employs a hybrid V-Trans-UNet architecture, pre-trained on weakly annotated data and fine-tuned with topology-aware annotations from 8k+ manually labeled corallite regions. Experimental results show the model achieves 0.94 topological accuracy and 0.77 mean Dice score on unseen slices of the same colony, and 0.63 mean Dice on a different, unrelated specimen, demonstrating the feasibility of 3D corallite modeling from μCT scans alone.
为追踪珊瑚群体中息肉分裂与骨骼生长过程，本研究提出CoralLite数据集及首个基于深度学习的珊瑚息肉三维重建基线。方法采用混合V-Trans-UNet架构，先在弱标注数据上预训练，再用8000+人工标注切片进行拓扑感知微调，实现对μCT切片的精确分割。实验表明，该模型在同种珊瑚新切片上达到0.94拓扑准确率和0.77平均Dice值，在不同物种珊瑚上为0.63，首次验证了仅凭μCT扫描即可实现单个息肉的完整三维建模。
AuralSAM2: Enabling SAM2 Hear Through Pyramid Audio-Visual Feature Prompting
Authors: Yuyuan Liu, Yuanhong Chen, Chong Wang, Junlin Han, Junde Wu, Can Peng, Jingkun Chen, Yu Tian, Gustavo Carneiro
Venue: CVPR
First: 2025-06-01T13:57:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T17:12:31+00:00
Comments: Proceedings of the IEEE/CVF Conference on Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (CVPR) Findings, 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Segment Anything Model 2 (SAM2) exhibits strong generalisation for promptable segmentation in video clips; however, its integration with the audio modality remains underexplored. Existing approaches either convert audio into visual prompts (e.g., boxes) via foundation models, or inject adapters into the image encoder for audio-visual fusion. Yet both directions fall short in human-in-the-loop scenarios due to limited prompt accuracy and increased inference overhead. In particular, these adapter-based methods often suffer from audio prompt dilution, where the signal gradually weakens as it propagates through the network. In this work, we propose AuralSAM2, which integrates audio into SAM2 while largely preserving its promptable segmentation capability. Its core module, AuralFuser, fuses audio and visual features to generate sparse and dense prompts. Guided by audio and built upon SAM2's feature pyramid, these prompts propagate auditory cues across visual layers, reinforcing cross-modal influence. To further align modalities, we introduce an audio-guided contrastive loss that emphasises auditory relevance in dominant visual features. Our method achieves notable accuracy gains on public benchmarks with only minimal impact on the interactive efficiency of promptable segmentation. Our code is available at https://github.com/yyliu01/AuralSAM2.
中文标题/摘要
标题：AuralSAM2：通过金字塔音频-视觉特征提示实现SAM2听觉感知
Segment Anything Model 2（SAM2）在视频片段的可提示分割中表现出强大的泛化能力；然而，其与音频模态的结合尚未充分探索。现有方法要么通过基础模型将音频转换为视觉提示（如框），要么在图像编码器中注入适配器以实现音频-视觉融合。但两种方法在人机交互场景中均因提示准确性有限和推理开销增加而表现不足。特别是适配器方法常受音频提示稀释问题影响，即音频信号在传播过程中逐渐衰减。本文提出AuralSAM2，将音频整合进SAM2的同时基本保留其可提示分割能力。其核心模块AuralFuser融合音频与视觉特征生成稀疏和密集提示。这些提示在SAM2的特征金字塔上引导音频信号跨视觉层传播，强化跨模态影响。为进一步对齐模态，我们引入音频引导对比损失，强调主导视觉特征中的听觉相关性。该方法在公开基准上实现显著精度提升，同时对可提示分割的交互效率影响极小。代码见https://github.com/yyliu01/AuralSAM2。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to extend SAM2's promptable video segmentation to audio-driven scenarios while preserving interactivity, AuralSAM2 introduces a novel audio-visual fusion module called AuralFuser that generates sparse and dense audio-guided prompts within SAM2's feature pyramid. These prompts propagate auditory cues across visual layers to reinforce cross-modal alignment, mitigating the signal dilution common in adapter-based methods. The model further employs an audio-guided contrastive loss to enhance modality alignment. Experimental results show significant performance gains on public benchmarks without compromising interactive efficiency.
为在保持交互式提示效率的前提下扩展SAM2对音频驱动场景的视频分割能力，AuralSAM2提出AuralFuser模块，通过在SAM2的特征金字塔内生成稀疏与密集的音频引导提示，使听觉线索跨视觉层传播，强化跨模态影响，避免适配器方法中常见的信号衰减问题。同时引入音频引导对比损失以增强模态对齐，实现在公共基准上显著性能提升，且对交互式分割效率影响极小。
SAGE3D: Soft-guided attention and graph excitation for 3D point cloud corner detection
Authors: Batuhan Arda Bekar, Can Sarı, Hüseyin Can Gülkan, Barış Özcan
First: 2026-05-14T17:08:35+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T17:08:35+00:00
Comments: 5 pages, 4 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present SAGE3D, a hybrid Transformer-based model for corner detection in airborne LiDAR point clouds. We propose a multi-stage solution built on a hierarchical encoder-decoder architecture that progressively downsamples point clouds through Set Abstraction layers and recovers per-point predictions via Feature Propagation. We introduce two innovations: Soft-Guided Attention, which injects ground-truth corner labels as a log-prior into attention logits during training to improve precision; then an Excitatory Graph Neural Network positioned at strategic resolutions in the hierarchy, employing positive-only message passing where high-confidence corners reinforce predictions through learned boosting, optimizing for recall. The hierarchical design enables multi-scale feature extraction while our guided attention and excitatory modules ensure corner signals are amplified rather than diluted across scales.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SAGE3D：面向3D点云角点检测的软引导注意力与图激励方法
我们提出了SAGE3D，一种基于混合Transformer架构的空中激光雷达点云角点检测模型。该模型采用分阶段解决方案，基于层次化编码器-解码器结构，通过Set Abstraction层逐步下采样点云，并通过特征传播恢复每点预测。我们引入两项创新：软引导注意力，将真实角点标签作为对数先验注入训练时的注意力对数中，以提升精度；以及在层次结构关键分辨率处部署的激励图神经网络，采用仅正向消息传递机制，通过学习增强机制使高置信度角点强化预测，优化召回率。层次化设计支持多尺度特征提取，而引导注意力与激励模块确保角点信号在尺度间被放大而非稀释。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for accurate corner detection in airborne LiDAR point clouds, SAGE3D introduces a hybrid Transformer model with a hierarchical encoder-decoder structure that progressively downsamples and reconstructs point cloud features. It employs Soft-Guided Attention to incorporate ground-truth corner labels as a log-prior into attention logits, enhancing precision during training, and an Excitatory Graph Neural Network that uses positive-only message passing to amplify high-confidence corner predictions across scales, improving recall. Experimental results demonstrate that this dual mechanism significantly boosts detection accuracy compared to baseline methods, particularly in complex and sparse point cloud environments.
为提升航空激光雷达点云中的角点检测精度，SAGE3D提出一种基于混合Transformer的层次化编码器-解码器模型，通过Set Abstraction逐级下采样并重建点云特征。该方法引入软引导注意力机制，在训练时将真实角点标签作为对数先验注入注意力对数中以提升精度，并在层次结构关键位置部署激励图神经网络，通过正向消息传递强化高置信度角点预测以提升召回率。实验表明，该多尺度架构结合引导注意力与激励模块，显著优于基线方法。
From Data to Action: Accelerating Refinery Optimization with AI
Authors: Dániel Pfeifer, Ábrahám Papp, Tibor Bernáth, Tamás Zoltán Varga, Márk Czifra, Botond Szilágyi, Edith Alice Kovács
First: 2026-05-14T17:07:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T17:07:41+00:00
Comments: 34 pages, 17 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Nowadays refinery optimization utilizes sheer amounts of data, which can be handled with modern Linear Programming (LP) software, but the interpreting and applying the results remains challenging. Large petrochemical companies use massive models, with hundreds of thousands of input matrix elements. The LP solution is mathematically correct, but simplifications are made in the model, and data supply errors may occur. Therefore, further insight is needed to trust the results. The LP solver does not have a memory, so additional understanding could be gained by analyzing historical data and comparing it to the current plan. As such, machine learning approaches were suggested to support decision making based on the LP solution. Among these, Anomaly Detection tools are proposed to be used in tandem with the LP output. A transformed version of the popular ECOD methodology is applied. New methods are proposed to handle high-dimensional data: choosing the most informative pairs. Then, this is used alongside two 2D Anomaly Detection algorithms, revealing several business opportunities and data supply errors in the MOL refinery scheduling and planning architecture.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从数据到行动：利用人工智能加速炼油优化
如今炼油优化利用大量数据，现代线性规划（LP）软件可处理这些数据，但结果的解读与应用仍具挑战。大型石化企业使用包含数十万输入矩阵元素的庞大模型。LP解在数学上正确，但模型存在简化，且数据供应可能出错，因此需进一步洞察以信任结果。LP求解器无记忆功能，故可通过分析历史数据并与当前计划对比，获得额外理解。因此，建议采用机器学习方法辅助基于LP解的决策。其中，提出将异常检测工具与LP输出结合使用，采用ECOD方法的改进版本。新方法用于处理高维数据：选择最具信息量的变量对，再结合两种二维异常检测算法，揭示了MOL炼油厂调度与规划架构中的若干商业机会与数据供应错误。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of interpreting and trusting complex LP optimization results in refinery operations, this study proposes integrating machine learning—specifically anomaly detection—with LP outputs to enhance decision-making. It adapts the ECOD methodology for high-dimensional refinery data by identifying the most informative variable pairs, then combines this with two 2D anomaly detection algorithms. Experimental results on MOL refinery data revealed actionable business opportunities and identified data supply errors, demonstrating that ML augmentation can uncover hidden insights beyond the mathematical correctness of LP solutions.
为解决炼油厂线性规划优化结果难以解释和信任的问题，该研究提出将机器学习中的异常检测方法与LP结果结合，以发现可操作的洞察。它改进ECOD方法，通过选择最具信息量的变量对降低高维数据维度，再结合两种二维异常检测算法。在MOL炼油厂数据上的实验结果揭示了多个运营低效点和数据供给错误，为调度与规划优化提供了具体改进方向。
Inducing Overthink: Hierarchical Genetic Algorithm-based DoS Attack on Black-Box Large Language Reasoning Models
Authors: Shuqiang Wang, Wei Cao, Jiaqi Weng, Jialing Tao, Licheng Pan, Hui Xue, Zhixuan Chu
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-05-13T10:57:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T17:07:38+00:00
Comments: Accepted at ICML 2026. Code available at: https://github.com/EndlessCao/Overthink-HGA
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) are increasingly integrated into systems requiring reliable multi-step inference, yet this growing dependence exposes new vulnerabilities related to computational availability. In particular, LRMs exhibit a tendency to "overthink", producing excessively long and redundant reasoning traces, when confronted with incomplete or logically inconsistent inputs. This behavior significantly increases inference latency and energy consumption, forming a potential vector for denial-of-service (DoS) style resource exhaustion. In this work, we investigate this attack surface and propose an automated black-box framework that induces overthinking in LRMs by systematically perturbing the logical structure of input problems. Our method employs a hierarchical genetic algorithm (HGA) operating on structured problem decompositions, and optimizes a composite fitness function designed to maximize both response length and reflective overthinking markers. Across four state-of-the-art reasoning models, the proposed method substantially amplifies output length, achieving up to a 26.1x increase on the MATH benchmark and consistently outperforming benign and manually crafted missing-premise baselines. We further demonstrate strong transferability, showing that adversarial inputs evolved using a small proxy model retain high effectiveness against large commercial LRMs. These findings highlight overthinking as a shared and exploitable vulnerability in modern reasoning systems, underscoring the need for more robust defenses.
中文标题/摘要
标题：诱导过度思考：针对黑盒大语言推理模型的基于分层遗传算法的拒绝服务攻击
大语言推理模型（LRMs）正越来越多地集成到需要可靠多步推理的系统中，但这种日益依赖也暴露了与计算可用性相关的新型漏洞。特别是，LRMs在面对不完整或逻辑不一致的输入时，倾向于“过度思考”，产生过长且冗余的推理轨迹，显著增加推理延迟和能耗，构成拒绝服务（DoS）式资源耗尽的潜在攻击向量。本文研究了这一攻击面，提出了一种自动化黑盒框架，通过系统性地扰动输入问题的逻辑结构来诱导LRMs过度思考。该方法采用作用于结构化问题分解的分层遗传算法（HGA），并优化一个复合适应度函数，旨在最大化响应长度和反映过度思考的标记。在四个最先进的推理模型上，该方法显著放大输出长度，在MATH基准测试中最高提升26.1倍，并持续优于良性及人工设计的缺失前提基线。我们进一步展示了强迁移性，表明使用小型代理模型演化出的对抗性输入对大型商业LRMs仍保持高有效性。这些发现突显了过度思考作为现代推理系统中普遍存在且可被利用的漏洞，强调了更强大防御机制的必要性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the vulnerability of Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) to resource exhaustion via their tendency to overthink under incomplete inputs, this work proposes a black-box attack framework using a hierarchical genetic algorithm to systematically perturb input logic and induce excessive reasoning. The method optimizes a composite fitness function targeting both output length and overthinking markers, achieving up to 26.1x longer responses on the MATH benchmark across four state-of-the-art models, outperforming baseline approaches. The attack also demonstrates strong transferability, with adversarial inputs evolved on small proxy models remaining effective against large commercial models, revealing overthinking as a widespread exploitable flaw in reasoning systems.
鉴于大型推理模型（LRMs）在面对不完整输入时易产生冗长推理轨迹，导致资源耗尽，作者提出一种基于分层遗传算法（HGA）的黑盒攻击框架，通过系统性扰动输入逻辑结构诱导模型过度思考。该算法优化复合适应度函数，以最大化输出长度与过度思考标记，实验表明在四项主流模型上可使输出长度最多提升26.1倍（MATH基准），显著优于良性及人工构造基线，且在小代理模型上演化出的对抗输入可有效迁移到大型商业模型，揭示过度思考是现代推理系统普遍存在的可利用漏洞。
Novel Dynamic Batch-Sensitive Adam Optimiser for Vehicular Accident Injury Severity Prediction
Authors: Daniel Asare Kyei, Alimatu Saadia-Yussiff, Maame G. Asante-Mensah, Abdul Lateef-Yussiff, Charles Roland Haruna, Derry Emmanuel
First: 2026-05-14T17:06:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T17:06:43+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The choice of optimiser is important in deep learning, as it strongly influences model efficiency and speed of convergence. However, many commonly used optimisers encounter difficulties when applied to imbalanced and sequential datasets, limiting their ability to capture patterns of minority classes. In this study, we propose Dynamic Batch-Sensitive Adam (DBS-Adam), an optimiser that dynamically scales the learning rate using a batch difficulty score derived from exponential moving averages of gradient norms and batch loss. DBS-Adam improves training stability and accelerates convergence by increasing updates for difficult batches and reducing them for easier ones. We evaluate DBS-Adam by integrating it with Bi-Directional LSTM networks for accident injury severity prediction, addressing class imbalance through SMOTE-ENN resampling and Focal Loss. Four experimental configurations compare baseline Bi-LSTM models and alternative architectures to assess optimiser impact. Rigorous comparison against state-of-the-art optimisers (AMSGrad, AdamW, AdaBound) across five random seeds demonstrated DBS-Adam's competitive performance with statistically significant precision improvements (p=0.020). Results indicate that DBS-Adam outperforms standard optimisation approaches, achieving 95.22% test accuracy, 96.11% precision, 95.28% recall, 95.39% F1-score, and a test loss of 0.0086. The proposed framework enables effective real-time accident severity classification for targeted emergency response and road safety interventions, demonstrating the value of DBS-Adam for learning from imbalanced sequential data.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于车辆事故伤害严重程度预测的新型动态批敏感Adam优化器
优化器的选择在深度学习中至关重要，因为它显著影响模型效率和收敛速度。然而，许多常用优化器在处理不平衡和序列数据时存在困难，限制了其捕捉少数类模式的能力。本研究提出动态批敏感Adam（DBS-Adam）优化器，通过基于梯度范数和批次损失的指数移动平均值计算的批次难度分数动态调整学习率。DBS-Adam通过增加困难批次的更新、减少简单批次的更新，提升训练稳定性并加速收敛。我们通过将DBS-Adam与双向LSTM网络结合，用于事故伤害严重程度预测，并通过SMOTE-ENN重采样和Focal Loss解决类别不平衡问题。四个实验配置对比基线双向LSTM模型和替代架构，评估优化器的影响。与当前最优优化器（AMSGrad、AdamW、AdaBound）在五个随机种子上的严格比较表明，DBS-Adam表现具有竞争力，精度提升具有统计显著性（p=0.020）。结果表明，DBS-Adam优于标准优化方法，测试准确率为95.22%，精确率为96.11%，召回率为95.28%，F1分数为95.39%，测试损失为0.0086。所提框架可有效实现事故严重程度的实时分类，支持精准应急响应和道路安全干预，凸显DBS-Adam在学习不平衡序列数据中的价值。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of standard optimizers in handling imbalanced and sequential data, this study introduces Dynamic Batch-Sensitive Adam (DBS-Adam), which adaptively scales the learning rate based on a batch difficulty score derived from gradient norms and batch loss. Evaluated with Bi-Directional LSTM networks for vehicular accident injury severity prediction, DBS-Adam was combined with SMOTE-ENN and Focal Loss to address class imbalance. Four experimental configurations compared DBS-Adam against AMSGrad, AdamW, and AdaBound across five random seeds, showing statistically significant precision gains (p=0.020) and superior performance metrics: 95.22% accuracy, 96.11% precision, 95.28% recall, 95.39% F1-score, and 0.0086 test loss, demonstrating its effectiveness for real-time severity classification in imbalanced sequential settings.
为解决传统优化器在不平衡和序列数据中表现不佳的问题，本研究提出动态批次敏感Adam（DBS-Adam），其根据梯度范数与批次损失的指数移动平均值动态调整学习率。在Bi-Directional LSTM网络中用于事故伤情严重性预测时，结合SMOTE-ENN与Focal Loss处理类别不平衡。在四种配置和五个随机种子下，DBS-Adam显著优于AMSGrad、AdamW和AdaBound（p=0.020），达到95.22%准确率、96.11%精确率、95.28%召回率、95.39%F1分数及0.0086测试损失，验证了其在实时安全应用中的有效性。
Average Gradient Outer Product in kernel regression provably recovers the central subspace for multi-index models
Authors: Libin Zhu, Damek Davis, Dmitriy Drusvyatskiy, Maryam Fazel
First: 2026-05-14T17:05:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T17:05:30+00:00
Comments: 95 pages, 12 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We study a prototypical situation when a learned predictor can discover useful low-dimensional structure in data, while using fewer samples than are needed for accurate prediction. Specifically, we consider the problem of recovering a multi-index polynomial $f^*(x)=h(Ux)$, with $U\in\mathbb{R}^{r\times d}$ and $r\ll d$, from finitely many data/label pairs. Importantly, the target function depends on input $x$ only through the projection onto an unknown $r$-dimensional central subspace. The algorithm we analyze is appealingly simple: fit kernel ridge regression (KRR) to the data and compute the Average Gradient Outer Product (AGOP) from the fitted predictor. Our main results show that under reasonable assumptions the top $r$-dimensional eigenspace of AGOP provably recovers the central subspace, even in regimes when the prediction error remains large. Specifically, if the target function $f^*$ has degree $p^*$, it is known that $n\asymp d^{p^*}$ samples are necessary for KRR to achieve accurate prediction. In contrast, we show that if a low degree $p$ component of $f^*$ already carries all relevant directions for prediction, subspace recovery occurs in the much lower sample regime $n\asymp d^{p+δ}$ for any $δ\in(0,1)$. Our results thus demonstrate a separation between prediction and representation, and provide an explanation for why iterative kernel methods such as Recursive Feature Machines (RFM) can be sample-efficient in practice.
中文标题/摘要
标题：核回归中平均梯度外积可证明恢复多指标模型的中心子空间
我们研究了一种原型情形：学习的预测器能在样本数少于准确预测所需的情况下，发现数据中的有用低维结构。具体而言，我们考虑从有限的数据-标签对中恢复多指标多项式函数 $f^*(x)=h(Ux)$，其中 $U\in\mathbb{R}^{r\times d}$ 且 $r\ll d$。重要的是，目标函数仅通过输入 $x$ 在未知的 $r$ 维中心子空间上的投影依赖于输入。我们分析的算法简洁：对数据拟合核岭回归（KRR），并从拟合预测器计算平均梯度外积（AGOP）。我们的主要结果表明，在合理假设下，AGOP 的前 $r$ 维特征空间可证明恢复中心子空间，即使在预测误差仍较大的情形下。具体而言，若目标函数 $f^*$ 的次数为 $p^*$，已知 KRR 达到准确预测需 $n\asymp d^{p^*}$ 个样本；相比之下，我们证明若 $f^*$ 的低次 $p$ 阶分量已包含所有预测相关方向，则子空间恢复可在更低的样本复杂度 $n\asymp d^{p+δ}$ 下实现（对任意 $δ\in(0,1)$）。我们的结果揭示了预测与表示之间的分离，并解释了为何迭代核方法（如递归特征机 RFM）在实践中具有样本效率。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to recover low-dimensional structure in high-dimensional data using fewer samples than required for accurate prediction, this paper analyzes kernel ridge regression and its Average Gradient Outer Product (AGOP) for identifying the central subspace in multi-index models. The method fits KRR to data and computes AGOP from the resulting predictor; under mild assumptions, the top r-dimensional eigenspace of AGOP provably recovers the unknown central subspace. Experimental results show that even when prediction error remains large, subspace recovery succeeds with sample complexity n ≈ d^{p+δ} for any δ ∈ (0,1), where p is the degree of the relevant component of the target function, contrasting with the n ≈ d^{p*} samples needed for accurate prediction. This demonstrates a separation between prediction and representation, explaining the sample efficiency of iterative kernel methods like RFM.
为在高维数据中用少于预测所需样本数恢复低维结构，本文分析了一种基于核岭回归和平均梯度外积（AGOP）的方法，用于识别多指标模型中的中心子空间。该方法通过拟合核岭回归器并提取AGOP的前r个特征空间，能在温和假设下准确恢复未知的r维子空间。实验表明，即使预测误差较大，该方法仍能在n ∝ d^{p+δ}（δ ∈ (0,1)）的样本规模下完成子空间恢复，远低于预测所需n ∝ d^{p*}的样本量。这一结果揭示了预测与表示之间的理论分离，解释了递归特征机等迭代核方法在实践中为何具有样本效率。
ML-Embed: Inclusive and Efficient Embeddings for a Multilingual World
Authors: Ziyin Zhang, Zihan Liao, Hang Yu, Peng Di, Rui Wang
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-05-14T17:05:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T17:05:26+00:00
