Comments: Accepted by ICML 2026. The data has been released earlier in the preprint arXiv:2603.19223
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The development of high-quality text embeddings is increasingly drifting toward an exclusionary future, defined by three critical barriers: prohibitive computational costs, a narrow linguistic focus that neglects most of the world's languages, and a lack of transparency from closed-source or open-weight models that stifles research. To dismantle these barriers, we introduce ML-Embed, a suite of inclusive and efficient models built upon a new framework: 3-Dimensional Matryoshka Learning (3D-ML). Our framework addresses the computational challenge with comprehensive efficiency across the entire model lifecycle. Beyond the storage benefits of Matryoshka Representation Learning (MRL) and flexible inference-time depth provided by Matryoshka Layer Learning (MLL), we introduce Matryoshka Embedding Learning (MEL) for enhanced parameter efficiency. To address the linguistic challenge, we curate a massively multilingual dataset and train a suite of models ranging from 140M to 8B parameters. In a direct commitment to transparency, we release all models, data, and code. Extensive evaluation on 430 tasks demonstrates that our models set new records on 9 of 17 evaluated MTEB benchmarks, with particularly strong results in low-resource languages, providing a reproducible blueprint for building globally equitable and computationally efficient AI systems.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ML-Embed：面向多语言世界的包容且高效的嵌入模型
高质量文本嵌入的发展正日益走向排他性未来，受制于三大关键障碍：高昂的计算成本、狭隘的语言聚焦（忽视全球多数语言）以及闭源或开放权重模型缺乏透明度，阻碍了研究进展。为打破这些障碍，我们提出ML-Embed，一套基于全新框架——三维套娃学习（3D-ML）的包容且高效模型。该框架通过贯穿模型全生命周期的全面效率解决计算挑战。除套娃表示学习（MRL）带来的存储优势和套娃层学习（MLL）提供的灵活推理深度外，我们引入套娃嵌入学习（MEL）以提升参数效率。为应对语言挑战，我们构建了大规模多语言数据集，并训练了从1.4亿到80亿参数不等的模型系列。为践行透明原则，我们公开所有模型、数据与代码。在430项任务上的广泛评估表明，我们的模型在17个MTEB基准中的9个上创下新纪录，尤其在低资源语言中表现突出，为构建全球公平且计算高效的AI系统提供了可复现的蓝图。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the exclusionary trends in multilingual text embedding development—driven by high costs, linguistic bias, and lack of transparency—the authors introduce ML-Embed, a suite of efficient and inclusive models built on the 3D-Matryoshka Learning framework. This framework enhances computational efficiency through Matryoshka Representation Learning, Layer Learning, and Embedding Learning, enabling parameter-efficient training and inference. Trained on a massive multilingual dataset and scaled from 140M to 8B parameters, the models are fully open-sourced. Experimental results across 430 tasks show ML-Embed achieves state-of-the-art performance on 9 out of 17 MTEB benchmarks, with especially strong gains in low-resource languages, demonstrating both technical efficacy and global inclusivity.
为应对当前多语言文本嵌入发展中的三大问题——计算成本高、语言覆盖窄、模型不透明——作者提出ML-Embed框架，基于3D-Matryoshka学习（3D-ML）实现训练、存储与推理全流程高效化，包含嵌入层、层与表示的嵌套学习机制。他们在大规模多语言数据集上训练了140M至8B参数的模型，并完全开源模型、数据与代码。在430项任务评估中，ML-Embed在17个MTEB基准中的9个上取得最优性能，尤其在低资源语言上表现突出，验证了其在计算效率与语言包容性方面的双重优势。
Croissant Baker: Metadata Generation for Discoverable, Governable, and Reusable ML Datasets
Authors: Rafi Al Attrach, Rajna Fani, Sebastian Lobentanzer, Joan Giner-Miguelez, Debanshu Das, Varuni H. K., Nobin Sarwar, Rajat Ghosh, Anwai Archit, Surbhi Motghare, Christina Conrad Parry, Luis Oala, Lara Grosso, Joaquin Vanschoren, Steffen Vogler, Sujata Goswami, Eric S. Rosenthal, Marzyeh Ghassemi, Matthew McDermott, Tom Pollard
First: 2026-05-14T17:04:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T17:04:39+00:00
Comments: 23 pages, 5 figures, 11 tables. Project: https://lcp.mit.edu/croissant-baker/ Code: https://github.com/MIT-LCP/croissant-baker
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Croissant has emerged as the metadata standard for machine learning datasets, providing a structured, JSON-LD-based format that makes dataset discovery, automated ingestion, and reproducible analysis machine-checkable across ML platforms. Adoption has accelerated, and NeurIPS now requires Croissant metadata in every submission to its dataset tracks. Yet in practice Croissant generation usually starts with uploading data to a public platform, a path infeasible for governed and large local repositories that hold much of the high-value data ML increasingly relies on. We release Croissant Baker, a local-first, open-source command-line tool that generates validated Croissant metadata directly from a dataset directory through a modular handler registry. We evaluate Croissant Baker on over 140 datasets, scaling to MIMIC-IV at 886 million rows and 374 Parquet files. On held-out comparisons against producer-authored or standards-derived ground truth, Croissant Baker reaches 97-100% agreement across multiple domains.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Croissant Baker：用于可发现、可管理且可重用的机器学习数据集的元数据生成
Croissant 已成为机器学习数据集的元数据标准，提供一种结构化的、基于 JSON-LD 的格式，使数据集发现、自动化摄取和可重现分析在不同机器学习平台上均可被机器验证。其采用率已加速增长，NeurIPS 现在要求所有数据集投稿必须包含 Croissant 元数据。然而，实践中 Croissant 生成通常始于将数据上传至公共平台，这对受管理且规模庞大的本地仓库不可行，而这些仓库持有机器学习日益依赖的高价值数据。我们发布 Croissant Baker，这是一个本地优先、开源的命令行工具，可通过模块化处理器注册表，直接从数据集目录生成经过验证的 Croissant 元数据。我们在 140 多个数据集上评估 Croissant Baker，规模扩展至包含 8.86 亿行和 374 个 Parquet 文件的 MIMIC-IV 数据集。在与生产者自编或标准推导的基准对比中，Croissant Baker 在多个领域达成 97-100% 的一致性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for standardized, machine-readable metadata to support discoverability and reproducibility of ML datasets, especially for large, governed, and local repositories that cannot easily upload data to public platforms, the authors introduce Croissant Baker, an open-source command-line tool that generates validated Croissant metadata directly from local dataset directories using a modular handler system. Evaluated across 140 datasets including the massive MIMIC-IV collection with 886 million rows, Croissant Baker demonstrates high fidelity, achieving 97-100% agreement with ground truth metadata from dataset producers or standards-based references across multiple domains, validating its utility for scalable, local metadata generation.
为支持机器学习数据集的可发现性和可重复性，尤其针对无法依赖公共平台上传的受控本地数据仓库，作者提出了Croissant Baker：一款本地优先、开源的命令行工具，通过模块化处理器注册表直接从数据集目录生成经过验证的Croissant元数据。在140多个数据集（包括规模达8.86亿行、374个Parquet文件的MIMIC-IV）上评估，该工具在多个领域与数据生产者或标准推导的元数据达成97-100%的一致性，验证了其在大规模自动化元数据生成中的有效性。
Prompting from the bench: Large-scale pretraining is not sufficient to prepare LLMs for ordinary meaning analysis
Authors: Abhishek Purushothama, Junghyun Min, Brandon Waldon, Nathan Schneider
First: 2025-10-29T10:21:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T17:03:29+00:00
Comments: Accepted FAccT 2026; 29 pages, 14 tables, 7 figures. Previous title - Not ready for the bench: LLM legal interpretation is unstable and out of step with human judgments; NLLPW 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In the U.S. judicial system, a widespread approach to legal interpretation entails assessing how a legal text would be understood by an `ordinary' speaker of the language. Recent scholarship has proposed that legal practitioners leverage large language models (LLMs) to ascertain a text's ordinary meaning. But are LLMs up to the task? As textual interpretation questions arise in spheres ranging from criminal law to civil rights, we argue it is crucial that models not be taken as authoritative without rigorous evaluation. This work offers an empirical argument against LLM-assisted interpretation as recently practiced by legal scholars and federal judges, who reasoned the large amount of data that models see in training would enable models to illuminate how people ordinarily use certain words or phrases. In controlled experiments, we find failures in robustness which cast doubt on this assumption and raise serious questions about the utility of these models in practice. For the models in our evaluation, slight changes to the format of a question can lead to wildly different conclusions -- a vulnerability that parties with an interest in the outcome could exploit. Comparing with a dataset where people were asked similar legal interpretation questions, we see that these models are at best moderately correlated to human judgments -- not strong enough given the stakes in this domain.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从席位出发的提示：大规模预训练不足以使大语言模型胜任普通意义分析
在美国司法体系中，一种广泛采用的法律解释方法是评估法律文本如何被该语言的‘普通’使用者理解。近期学术研究提出，法律从业者可借助大语言模型（LLMs）来确定文本的普通含义。但LLMs真的胜任此任务吗？随着刑事法律到民权等领域出现文本解释问题，我们认为在没有严格评估的情况下，不应将模型视为权威。本研究通过实证论证反对法律学者和联邦法官近期所采用的LLM辅助解释方法，他们认为模型在训练中看到大量数据，能够揭示人们如何通常使用某些词语或短语。在受控实验中，我们发现模型稳健性不足，这质疑了该假设，并对这些模型在实践中的效用提出严重质疑。在我们的评估中，模型对问题格式的微小变化可能产生截然不同的结论——这为利益相关方可能利用的漏洞。与一个让人类回答类似法律解释问题的数据集相比，我们发现这些模型最多仅与人类判断中度相关——鉴于该领域的高风险，相关性不足。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the growing use of large language models (LLMs) in legal interpretation to determine ordinary meaning, this study challenges the assumption that pretraining on vast text corpora alone equips LLMs for reliable judicial tasks. Through controlled experiments, the authors demonstrate that LLMs exhibit significant instability: minor changes in question phrasing can lead to drastically different outputs, exposing a vulnerability exploitable in high-stakes legal contexts. When compared to human judgments on similar legal questions, model outputs show only moderate correlation, insufficient for authoritative legal decision-making. The findings suggest that without further refinement, LLMs cannot be trusted to reliably capture ordinary meaning in legal texts.
鉴于大语言模型（LLM）在法律解释任务中的日益应用，本研究质疑了仅靠大规模预训练即可使模型准确捕捉普通语义的假设。通过受控实验发现，LLM对提示格式的微小改动极为敏感，可能导致输出结果剧烈变化，揭示了其在高风险法律场景中可能被利用的关键脆弱性。此外，与人类判断数据集的对比显示，模型与人类判断的相关性仅属中等水平，表明其尚不足以满足法律领域对权威解释的需求。
Concurrency without Model Changes: Future-based Asynchronous Function Calling for LLMs
Authors: Guangyu Feng, Huanzhi Mao, Prabal Dutta, Joseph E. Gonzalez
First: 2026-05-14T17:02:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T17:02:28+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Function calling, also known as tool use, is a core capability of modern LLM agents but is typically constrained by synchronous execution semantics. Under these semantics, LLM decoding is blocked until each function call completes, resulting in increasing end-to-end latency. In this work, we introduce AsyncFC, a pure execution-layer framework that decouples LLM decoding from function execution, enabling overlap between model decoding and function execution as well as inter-function parallelism when dependencies permit. AsyncFC layers over existing models and unmodified function implementations, requiring no fine-tuning or changes to the standard synchronous function-calling protocol. Across standard function-calling benchmarks and adapted software engineering benchmarks, AsyncFC significantly reduces end-to-end task completion time while preserving task accuracy. Furthermore, these results reveal that LLMs possess a native capability to reason over symbolic futures that represent unresolved execution results, enabling an asynchronous paradigm for model-tool interaction.
中文标题/摘要
标题：无需模型变更的并发性：面向LLM的基于未来的异步函数调用
函数调用，也称为工具使用，是现代LLM代理的核心能力，但通常受限于同步执行语义。在这些语义下，LLM解码会阻塞，直到每个函数调用完成，导致端到端延迟增加。在本研究中，我们引入AsyncFC，一个纯执行层框架，将LLM解码与函数执行解耦，允许在依赖允许时实现模型解码与函数执行的重叠以及函数间的并行性。AsyncFC可叠加在现有模型和未修改的函数实现之上，无需微调或更改标准同步函数调用协议。在标准函数调用基准和改编的软件工程基准上，AsyncFC显著减少了端到端任务完成时间，同时保持任务准确性。此外，这些结果表明LLM具备原生推理能力，能够处理代表未解决执行结果的符号未来，从而实现模型与工具交互的异步范式。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the latency bottlenecks caused by synchronous function calling in LLM agents, this work introduces AsyncFC, a framework that decouples LLM decoding from function execution to enable concurrent processing and inter-function parallelism. It operates as a pure execution-layer solution, requiring no model fine-tuning or protocol changes, and layers over existing models and unmodified functions. Experimental results across standard and adapted benchmarks show that AsyncFC significantly reduces end-to-end task completion time while maintaining task accuracy, revealing that LLMs can natively reason about symbolic futures representing pending function results, thus supporting an asynchronous interaction paradigm.
为解决LLM代理中同步函数调用导致的延迟瓶颈，本研究提出AsyncFC框架，将模型解码与函数执行解耦，支持并发处理和函数间并行。该框架可直接叠加于现有模型和未修改的函数实现之上，无需微调或协议变更，使LLM能够对代表未完成执行结果的符号未来进行推理。实验结果表明，AsyncFC在多个基准测试中显著缩短端到端任务完成时间，同时保持任务准确率，揭示LLM天然具备对未决执行结果进行异步推理的能力。
From User Preferences to Base Score Extraction Functions in Gradual Argumentation (with Appendix)
Authors: Aniol Civit, Antonio Rago, Antonio Andriella, Guillem Alenyà, Francesca Toni
First: 2026-02-16T12:01:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T17:01:45+00:00
Comments: Accepted to AAMAS 2026 - With Appendix
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Gradual argumentation is a field of symbolic AI which is attracting attention for its ability to support transparent and contestable AI systems. It is considered a useful tool in domains such as decision-making, recommendation, debate analysis, and others. The outcomes in such domains are usually dependent on the arguments' base scores, which must be selected carefully. Often, this selection process requires user expertise and may not always be straightforward. On the other hand, organising the arguments by preference could simplify the task. In this work, we introduce \emph{Base Score Extraction Functions}, which provide a mapping from users' preferences over arguments to base scores. These functions can be applied to the arguments of a \emph{Bipolar Argumentation Framework} (BAF), supplemented with preferences, to obtain a \emph{Quantitative Bipolar Argumentation Framework} (QBAF), allowing the use of well-established computational tools in gradual argumentation. We outline the desirable properties of base score extraction functions, discuss some design choices, and provide an algorithm for base score extraction. Our method incorporates an approximation of non-linearities in human preferences to allow for better approximation of the real ones. Finally, we evaluate our approach both theoretically and experimentally in a robotics setting, and offer recommendations for selecting appropriate gradual semantics in practice.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从用户偏好到渐进论证中的基础得分提取函数（含附录）
渐进论证是符号人工智能的一个领域，因其支持透明且可争议的AI系统而受到关注。它在决策、推荐、辩论分析等领域被视为有用工具。这些领域的结果通常依赖于论证的基础得分，需谨慎选择。通常，此选择过程需要用户专业知识，且未必直观。另一方面，按偏好组织论证可简化任务。本文引入了\emph{基础得分提取函数}，将用户对论证的偏好映射为基础得分。这些函数可应用于\emph{双极论证框架}（BAF），并结合偏好，生成\emph{量化双极论证框架}（QBAF），从而可使用渐进论证中成熟的计算工具。我们概述了基础得分提取函数的期望属性，讨论了部分设计选择，并提供了一种基础得分提取算法。该方法通过近似人类偏好的非线性，以更好地逼近真实偏好。最后，我们在机器人领域从理论和实验两方面评估了该方法，并提供了实践中选择合适渐进语义的建议。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to automate the selection of base scores in gradual argumentation systems without requiring expert user input, this work introduces Base Score Extraction Functions that map user preferences over arguments to quantitative scores. These functions enable the transformation of a Bipolar Argumentation Framework into a Quantitative Bipolar Argumentation Framework, allowing the use of established computational tools. The method approximates non-linear human preferences to improve accuracy and includes an algorithm for score extraction. Experimental evaluation in a robotics setting demonstrates the approach’s effectiveness in aligning argument scores with user preferences, and the study provides practical guidance for choosing suitable gradual semantics.
为解决渐进论证系统中人工选择基础分数的复杂性，本文提出基础分数提取函数，将用户对论点的偏好映射为量化分数。该方法可将双极论证框架转化为量化双极论证框架，从而应用成熟的计算工具。方法通过近似人类偏好的非线性特性以提升准确性，并提供提取分数的算法。在机器人场景中的实验验证了该方法能有效匹配用户偏好，理论分析也支持其合理性，并为实际应用中选择合适的渐进语义提供了指导。
BiTrajDiff: Bidirectional Trajectory Generation with Diffusion Models for Offline Reinforcement Learning
Authors: Yunpeng Qing, Yixiao Chi, Shuo Chen, Shunyu Liu, Kexuan Zhou, Sixu Lin, Litao Liu, Changqing Zou
First: 2025-06-06T05:41:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T17:01:38+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent advances in offline Reinforcement Learning (RL) have proven that effective policy learning can benefit from imposing conservative constraints on pre-collected datasets. However, such static datasets often exhibit distribution bias, resulting in limited generalizability. To address this limitation, a straightforward solution is data augmentation (DA), which leverages generative models to enrich data distribution. Despite the promising results, current DA techniques focus solely on reconstructing future trajectories from given states, while ignoring the exploration of history transitions that reach them. This single-direction paradigm inevitably hinders the discovery of diverse behavior patterns, especially those leading to critical states that may have yielded high-reward outcomes. In this work, we introduce Bidirectional Trajectory Diffusion (BiTrajDiff), a novel DA framework for offline RL that models both future and history trajectories from any intermediate states. Specifically, we decompose the trajectory generation task into two independent yet complementary diffusion processes: one generating forward trajectories to predict future dynamics, and the other generating backward trajectories to trace essential history transitions.BiTrajDiff can efficiently leverage critical states as anchors to expand into potentially valuable yet underexplored regions of the state space, thereby facilitating dataset diversity. Extensive experiments on the D4RL benchmark suite demonstrate that BiTrajDiff achieves superior performance compared to other advanced DA methods across various offline RL backbones.
中文标题/摘要
标题：BiTrajDiff：基于扩散模型的双向轨迹生成用于离线强化学习
近期离线强化学习（RL）的进展表明，通过对预收集数据集施加保守约束可有效提升策略学习效果。然而，此类静态数据集常存在分布偏倚，导致泛化能力受限。为解决此问题，一种直接方案是数据增强（DA），利用生成模型丰富数据分布。尽管效果显著，现有DA技术仅关注从给定状态重建未来轨迹，忽略到达这些状态的历史转移探索。这种单向范式必然限制多样行为模式的发现，尤其难以捕捉通往关键状态（可能带来高回报）的路径。本文提出双向轨迹扩散（BiTrajDiff），一种面向离线RL的新型DA框架，可从任意中间状态建模未来与历史轨迹。具体而言，我们将轨迹生成任务分解为两个独立互补的扩散过程：一个生成前向轨迹以预测未来动态，另一个生成后向轨迹以追溯关键历史转移。BiTrajDiff可高效利用关键状态作为锚点，扩展至潜在有价值但未充分探索的状态空间区域，从而促进数据集多样性。在D4RL基准套件上的大量实验表明，BiTrajDiff在多种离线RL骨干模型上均优于其他先进DA方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the distribution bias in offline RL datasets that limits policy generalization, this work proposes BiTrajDiff, a bidirectional trajectory generation framework using diffusion models to augment data. It addresses the limitation of existing methods that only model forward trajectories by introducing two complementary diffusion processes: one generating future trajectories and another reconstructing past transitions from intermediate states. This dual-direction approach enables exploration of underrepresented state regions anchored by critical states, enhancing dataset diversity. Experimental results on D4RL benchmarks show BiTrajDiff consistently outperforms state-of-the-art data augmentation methods across multiple offline RL algorithms.
为解决离线强化学习中数据分布偏置导致的泛化能力不足问题，本文提出BiTrajDiff框架，利用扩散模型双向生成轨迹以增强数据多样性。该方法突破现有仅生成未来轨迹的单向范式，通过两个互补的扩散过程——一个预测未来状态，另一个从任意中间状态重建历史轨迹——实现双向探索，尤其能挖掘高回报关键状态周围的潜在行为模式。在D4RL基准测试中，BiTrajDiff在多个离线强化学习算法上均显著优于当前主流数据增强方法。
SOCC-ICP: Semantics-Assisted Odometry based on Occupancy Grids and ICP
Authors: Johannes Scherer, Sebastian Hirt, Henri Meeß
First: 2026-05-14T17:00:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T17:00:38+00:00
Comments: 9 pages, 3 figures, Accepted May 2026 for publication in IEEE Robotics and Automation Letters (RA-L)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reliable pose estimation in previously unseen environments is a fundamental capability of autonomous systems. Existing LiDAR odometry methods typically employ point-, surfel-, or NDT-based map representations, which are distinct from the semantic occupancy grids commonly used for downstream tasks such as motion planning. We introduce SOCC-ICP, a semantics-assisted odometry framework that jointly performs Semantic OCCupancy grid mapping and LiDAR scan alignment. Each map voxel encodes geometric and semantic statistics, enabling adaptive point-to-point or point-to-plane ICP based on local planarity. Further, the occupancy grid naturally filters dynamic objects through raycasting-based free-space updates. Across diverse evaluation scenarios, SOCC-ICP achieves performance competitive with state-of-the-art LiDAR odometry and remains robust in geometrically degenerate environments, even in the absence of semantic cues. When semantic labels are available, integrating them into map construction, downsampling, and correspondence weighting yields further accuracy gains. By unifying odometry and semantic occupancy grid mapping within a single representation, SOCC-ICP eliminates redundant map structures and directly provides a map suitable for downstream robotic applications.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SOCC-ICP：基于占据网格与ICP的语义辅助里程计
在先前未见过的环境中实现可靠的位姿估计是自主系统的基本能力。现有激光雷达里程计方法通常采用点、表面元或NDT地图表示，与用于运动规划等下游任务的语义占据网格不同。我们提出SOCC-ICP，一种语义辅助里程计框架，联合执行语义占据网格建图与激光雷达扫描对齐。每个地图体素编码几何与语义统计信息，支持基于局部平面性的自适应点到点或点到平面ICP。此外，占据网格通过基于射线投射的自由空间更新自然过滤动态物体。在多种评估场景中，SOCC-ICP性能与当前最先进的激光雷达里程计相当，即使在缺乏语义线索的几何退化环境中仍保持鲁棒性。当语义标签可用时，将其整合到地图构建、下采样及对应权重计算中可进一步提升精度。通过将里程计与语义占据网格建图统一于单一表示，SOCC-ICP消除了冗余地图结构，并直接提供适用于下游机器人应用的地图。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for reliable pose estimation in novel environments and the gap between LiDAR odometry and semantic occupancy grids used in downstream tasks, SOCC-ICP introduces a unified framework that jointly builds semantic occupancy grids and performs LiDAR scan alignment. Each voxel stores geometric and semantic statistics to enable adaptive ICP (point-to-point or point-to-plane) based on local surface properties, while raycasting filters out dynamic objects. Experimental results show SOCC-ICP matches or exceeds state-of-the-art odometry methods in diverse scenarios, including geometrically degenerate environments without semantic cues, and achieves further accuracy gains when semantic labels are available, eliminating redundant map structures and directly outputting a map usable for robotic applications.
为解决在未知环境中实现可靠位姿估计的需求，并弥合现有LiDAR里程计方法与下游任务常用语义占据网格之间的差距，SOCC-ICP提出了一种联合构建语义占据网格与LiDAR扫描对齐的统一框架。每个网格体素存储几何与语义统计信息，以支持根据局部平面性自适应选择点到点或点到平面ICP匹配方式，并通过射线投射法自动过滤动态物体。实验表明，该方法在多种场景下性能可媲美当前最先进LiDAR里程计方法，即使在无语义标签的几何退化环境中仍保持鲁棒性；当提供语义标签时，其在地图构建、下采样和对应权重计算中进一步提升精度，同时消除冗余地图结构，直接输出适用于机器人下游应用的地图。
Time Series Forecasting Through the Lens of Dynamics
Authors: Alexis-Raja Brachet, Pierre-Yves Richard, Céline Hudelot
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2025-07-21T16:29:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T16:58:20+00:00
Comments: Accepted at ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
While deep learning is facing an homogenization across modalities led by Transformers, they are still challenged by shallow linear models in the time series forecasting task. Our hypothesis is that models should learn a direct link from past to future data points, which we identify as a learning dynamics capability. We develop an original $\texttt{PRO-DYN}$ nomenclature to analyze existing models through the lens of dynamics. Two observations thus emerge: $\textbf{1.}$ under-performing architectures learn dynamics at most partially, $\textbf{2.}$ the location of the dynamics block at the model end is of prime importance. Our systemic and empirical studies both confirm our observations on a set of performance-varying models with diverse backbones. We propose a simple plug-and-play methodology guiding model designs and improvements.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过动力学视角的时间序列预测
尽管深度学习因Transformer的主导而跨模态趋于同质化，但在时间序列预测任务中，它们仍被浅层线性模型所挑战。我们的假设是，模型应直接学习从过去数据点到未来数据点的映射，这我们称之为学习动力学的能力。我们提出了一种原创的$\texttt{PRO-DYN}$命名法，通过动力学视角分析现有模型。由此得出两个观察：$\textbf{1.}$ 表现不佳的架构最多只能部分学习动力学；$\textbf{2.}$ 动力学模块在模型末端的位置至关重要。我们的系统性与实证研究均在一组性能各异、骨干结构多样的模型上验证了上述观察。我们提出了一种简单易用的插件式方法，指导模型设计与改进。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that deep learning models, despite their dominance in many tasks, still underperform compared to shallow linear models in time series forecasting, the authors hypothesize that models should explicitly learn dynamics — a direct mapping from past to future data points. They introduce a novel analytical framework called PRO-DYN to evaluate existing models through this lens, revealing two key findings: first, underperforming architectures capture dynamics only partially; second, the placement of the dynamics module at the end of the model significantly impacts performance. Empirical validation across diverse model backbones confirms these insights, leading to a simple plug-and-play methodology for improving model design in time series forecasting.
鉴于深度学习模型在时间序列预测任务中仍被浅层线性模型超越，本文假设有效模型必须显式学习从过去到未来的动态映射。作者提出PRO-DYN框架从动态视角分析现有架构，发现两点关键结论：一是表现不佳的模型仅部分捕捉动态；二是动态模块的位置（尤其置于模型末端）至关重要。通过在多种骨干模型上的系统性实验验证了这些发现，最终提出一种简单易用的即插即用方法，指导模型设计与改进。
Projected gradient methods for nonconvex and stochastic smooth optimization: new complexities and auto-conditioned stepsizes
Authors: Guanghui Lan, Tianjiao Li, Yangyang Xu
First: 2024-12-18T19:34:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T16:58:17+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present a novel class of projected gradient (PG) methods for minimizing a smooth but not necessarily convex function over a convex compact set. We first provide a novel analysis of the constant-stepsize PG method, achieving the best-known iteration complexity for finding an approximate stationary point of the problem. We then develop an "auto-conditioned" projected gradient (AC-PG) variant that achieves the same iteration complexity without requiring the input of the Lipschitz constant of the gradient or any line search procedure. The key idea is to estimate the Lipschitz constant using first-order information gathered from the previous iterations, and to show that the error caused by underestimating the Lipschitz constant can be properly controlled. We then generalize the PG methods to the stochastic setting, by proposing a stochastic projected gradient (SPG) method and a variance-reduced stochastic gradient (VR-SPG) method, achieving new complexity bounds in different oracle settings. We also present auto-conditioned stepsize policies for both stochastic PG methods and establish comparable convergence guarantees.
中文标题/摘要
标题：非凸与随机光滑优化的投影梯度方法：新复杂度与自适应步长
我们提出了一类用于在凸紧集上最小化光滑但未必凸函数的新型投影梯度（PG）方法。首先，我们对常数步长PG方法进行了新颖分析，实现了寻找问题近似驻点的最佳已知迭代复杂度。接着，我们开发了一种“自适应条件”投影梯度（AC-PG）变体，在无需输入梯度Lipschitz常数或任何线搜索过程的情况下，达到相同迭代复杂度。其核心思想是利用前几次迭代收集的一阶信息估计Lipschitz常数，并证明低估该常数所引起的误差可被有效控制。随后，我们将PG方法推广至随机设置，提出随机投影梯度（SPG）方法和方差缩减随机梯度（VR-SPG）方法，在不同算子设置下获得新的复杂度界。同时，我们为两种随机PG方法提供了自适应步长策略，并建立了可比的收敛保证。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient nonconvex optimization without prior knowledge of gradient Lipschitz constants, this paper introduces projected gradient methods with auto-conditioned stepsizes. It first analyzes a constant-stepsize projected gradient method, achieving optimal iteration complexity for finding approximate stationary points. Then, it proposes an auto-conditioned projected gradient (AC-PG) variant that eliminates the need for explicit Lipschitz constant input or line search by adaptively estimating it from past iterations, while maintaining the same complexity. The methods are further extended to stochastic settings with stochastic projected gradient (SPG) and variance-reduced (VR-SPG) variants, achieving new complexity bounds under different oracle models, with auto-conditioned stepsizes preserving comparable convergence guarantees.
为解决非凸光滑优化中无需预先知道梯度Lipschitz常数的问题，本文提出了一类带自适应步长的投影梯度方法。首先，对常数步长投影梯度方法进行新分析，获得求解近似驻点的最优迭代复杂度；随后提出自适应条件投影梯度（AC-PG）变体，无需输入梯度Lipschitz常数或线搜索，通过利用历史梯度信息动态估计并控制误差。方法进一步扩展至随机设置，提出随机投影梯度（SPG）和方差缩减随机梯度（VR-SPG）算法，在不同算子模型下获得新的复杂度界，且自适应步长策略保持相近的收敛保证。
On the Cultural Anachronism and Temporal Reasoning in Vision Language Models
Authors: Mukul Ranjan, Prince Jha, Khushboo Kumari, Zhiqiang Shen
First: 2026-05-14T16:58:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T16:58:16+00:00
Comments: Project Page: https://khushboo0012.github.io/tab-vlm-webpage/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Vision-Language Models (VLMs) are increasingly applied to cultural heritage materials, from digital archives to educational platforms. This work identifies a fundamental issue in how these models interpret historical artifacts. We define this phenomenon as cultural anachronism, the tendency to misinterpret historical objects using temporally inappropriate concepts, materials, or cultural frameworks. To quantify this phenomenon, we introduce the Temporal Anachronism Benchmark for Vision-Language Models (TAB-VLM), a dataset of 600 questions across six categories, designed to evaluate temporal reasoning on 1,600 Indian cultural artifacts spanning prehistoric to modern periods. Systematic evaluations of ten state-of-the-art models reveal significant deficiencies on our benchmark, and even the best model (GPT-5.2) achieves only 58.7% overall accuracy. The performance gap persists across varying architectures and scales, suggesting that cultural anachronism represents a significant limitation in visual AI systems, regardless of model size. These findings highlight the disparity between current VLM capabilities and the requirements for accurately interpreting cultural heritage materials, particularly for non-Western visual cultures underrepresented in training data. Our benchmark provides a foundation for enhancing temporal cognition in multimodal AI systems that interact with historical artifacts. The dataset and code are available in our project page.
中文标题/摘要
标题：视觉语言模型中的文化错时与时间推理问题
视觉语言模型（VLMs）正越来越多地应用于文化遗产材料，从数字档案到教育平台。本研究识别出这些模型在解读历史文物时存在的一项根本性问题，我们将其定义为‘文化错时’，即倾向于使用时间上不恰当的概念、材料或文化框架来误读历史物件。为量化这一现象，我们提出了视觉语言模型时间错时基准（TAB-VLM），该数据集包含600个问题，涵盖六大类，旨在评估1600件印度文化文物（从史前到现代时期）的时间推理能力。对十种最先进的模型的系统评估显示，它们在本基准上表现不佳，即使表现最好的模型（GPT-5.2）整体准确率也仅为58.7%。性能差距在不同架构和规模的模型中均持续存在，表明文化错时是视觉AI系统的重要局限，与模型大小无关。这些发现凸显了当前VLM能力与准确解读文化遗产材料（特别是训练数据中代表性不足的非西方视觉文化）所需能力之间的差距。我们的基准为提升多模态AI系统在与历史文物交互时的时间认知能力提供了基础。数据集和代码可在项目页面获取。
Summary / 总结
This study addresses the challenge that Vision-Language Models (VLMs) often misinterpret historical artifacts by projecting modern concepts onto past contexts, a phenomenon termed cultural anachronism. To measure this, the authors developed TAB-VLM, a benchmark of 600 questions across six categories targeting 1,600 Indian artifacts from prehistoric to modern eras. Evaluating ten state-of-the-art models, they found substantial performance gaps, with the top model achieving only 58.7% accuracy, and performance remained low across model architectures and sizes, indicating that temporal reasoning remains a core limitation in VLMs, especially for non-Western cultural materials underrepresented in training data.
该研究指出视觉语言模型（VLMs）常因套用现代或不恰当的历史文化概念而误读历史文物，这一现象被称为‘文化错时’。为量化此问题，作者构建了包含600道题目、覆盖6个类别的TAB-VLM基准数据集，针对1600件印度文物（从史前至现代）。对十种前沿模型的系统评估显示，表现最佳的GPT-5.2模型准确率仅为58.7%，且不同架构与规模的模型表现差距持续存在，表明VLMs在时间推理上存在系统性缺陷，尤其在处理非西方文化遗产时更为明显，凸显了多模态AI系统在时间认知能力上的改进需求。
VER: Vision Expert Transformer for Robot Learning via Foundation Distillation and Dynamic Routing
Authors: Yixiao Wang, Mingxiao Huo, Zhixuan Liang, Yushi Du, Lingfeng Sun, Haotian Lin, Jinghuan Shang, Chensheng Peng, Mohit Bansal, Mingyu Ding, Masayoshi Tomizuka
First: 2025-10-06T18:00:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T16:57:23+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Pretrained vision foundation models (VFMs) advance robotic learning via rich visual representations, yet individual VFMs typically excel only in specific domains, limiting generality across tasks. Distilling multiple VFMs into a unified representation for policy can mitigate this limitation but often yields inflexible task-specific feature selection and requires costly full re-training to incorporate robot-domain knowledge. We propose VER, a Vision Expert transformer for Robot learning. During pretraining, VER distills multiple VFMs into a vision expert library. It then fine-tunes only a lightweight routing network (fewer than 0.4% of parameters) to dynamically select task-relevant experts from the pretrained library for downstream robot tasks. We further introduce Patchwise Expert Routing with Curriculum Top-K Annealing to improve both flexibility and precision of dynamic expert selection. Moreover, VER supports parameter-efficient finetuning for scalable expert utilization and adaptive robot-domain knowledge integration. Across 17 diverse robotic tasks and multiple policy heads, VER achieves state-of-the-art performance. We find that VER reduces large-norm outliers in task-irrelevant regions (e.g., background) and concentrates on task-critical regions. Visualizations and codes can be found in https://yixiaowang7.github.io/ver_page/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：VER：通过基础蒸馏与动态路由的视觉专家变换器用于机器人学习
预训练视觉基础模型（VFMs）通过丰富的视觉表征推动机器人学习，但单个VFM通常仅在特定领域表现优异，限制了其跨任务的泛化能力。将多个VFM蒸馏为统一表示以用于策略可缓解此局限，但往往导致任务特定特征选择僵化，且需耗费高昂成本的完整再训练以融入机器人领域知识。我们提出VER，一种用于机器人学习的视觉专家变换器。在预训练阶段，VER将多个VFM蒸馏为视觉专家库；随后仅微调轻量级路由网络（参数少于0.4%），动态从预训练库中选择与任务相关的专家用于下游机器人任务。我们进一步引入分块专家路由与课程式Top-K退火机制，以提升动态专家选择的灵活性与精度。此外，VER支持参数高效微调，实现可扩展的专家利用与自适应机器人领域知识整合。在17个多样化机器人任务及多个策略头中，VER达到最先进性能。我们发现，VER减少了任务无关区域（如背景）中的大范数异常值，并聚焦于任务关键区域。可视化与代码见https://yixiaowang7.github.io/ver_page/。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the domain-specific limitations of pretrained vision foundation models, VER proposes a Vision Expert Transformer that distills multiple VFMs into a reusable expert library during pretraining. It then fine-tunes a lightweight routing network to dynamically select task-relevant experts for downstream robotic tasks, enabling parameter-efficient adaptation without full retraining. VER further enhances selection precision via Patchwise Expert Routing and Curriculum Top-K Annealing. Experimental results across 17 robotic tasks show state-of-the-art performance, with VER suppressing large-norm outliers in irrelevant regions and focusing attention on task-critical areas.
为解决预训练视觉基础模型领域特异性的问题，VER提出一种视觉专家Transformer，通过预训练将多个VFMs蒸馏为可复用的专家库。随后仅微调一个轻量级路由网络（参数量不足0.4%），动态选择与任务相关的专家用于下游机器人任务。结合Patchwise专家路由与课程式Top-K退火机制，VER提升了选择的灵活性与精度，同时支持参数高效微调和自适应机器人领域知识整合。在17个多样化机器人任务和多个策略头中，VER达到最先进性能，并显著降低无关区域（如背景）的高范数异常值，聚焦关键区域。
After the Interface: Relocating Human Agency in the Age of Conversational AI
Authors: Mengke Wu, Mike Yao
Venue: ACM Conversational User Interfaces 2026 (CUI '26)
First: 2026-05-14T16:54:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T16:54:57+00:00
Comments: 7 pages, 1 figure
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As AI systems take on greater autonomy, a quiet anxiety has settled over the HCI community: human agency is eroding. Users no longer control execution, interfaces recede, and machines decide. We argue that this anxiety, while understandable, reflects a framing problem rather than an empirical finding. Agency has not diminished but has relocated. As interaction has shifted from command- and feature-based paradigms toward conversational, generative, and agentic AI, human agency migrates from interface affordances to interaction itself: articulating goals, evaluating outputs, and negotiating outcomes. To make this relocation visible, we revisit control as a diagnostic lens, distinguish process control and outcome control, and map different systems across this space to show that what looks like agency's disappearance is actually its redistribution. We take seriously the objection that outcome-based agency may be illusory in systems that produce plausible but unverifiable outputs, and argue that this concern reveals what agency in human-AI interaction truly requires. This paper invites the CUI community to reconsider what agency means, where it lives, and what it demands, including who gets to have it and who holds responsibility when it fails, before the consequences become impossible to overlook.
中文标题/摘要
标题：界面之后：在对话式人工智能时代重新定位人类能动性
随着AI系统自主性增强，人机交互领域悄然弥漫着一种焦虑：人类能动性正在消退。用户不再掌控执行过程，界面逐渐退居幕后，机器开始做决定。我们认为，这种焦虑虽可理解，但反映的是框架问题而非实证发现。能动性并未减少，而是发生了转移。随着交互模式从命令与功能导向转向对话式、生成式和代理式AI，人类能动性从界面的可用性迁移到交互本身：明确目标、评估输出、协商结果。为使这种转移可见，我们重新审视控制作为诊断视角，区分过程控制与结果控制，并将不同系统映射到该空间中，以表明看似能动性消失实则是其再分配。我们认真对待反对意见——在生成看似合理但无法验证输出的系统中，基于结果的能动性可能是虚幻的，并认为这一担忧揭示了人类与AI交互中能动性真正所需之本质。本文呼吁对话式用户界面社区重新思考能动性的含义、所在位置、所需条件，包括谁有权拥有它，以及当它失效时谁应承担责任，以免其后果无法忽视。
Summary / 总结
As conversational AI systems grow more autonomous, HCI researchers worry human agency is disappearing, but this paper argues the concern stems from a framing error: agency hasn't vanished, it has shifted. The study redefines agency as moving from interface controls to the user’s role in goal articulation, output evaluation, and outcome negotiation. By distinguishing between process and outcome control, and mapping systems across this spectrum, the authors show agency is redistributed rather than lost. They acknowledge the risk of illusory agency in systems producing unverifiable outputs, and conclude that true agency demands accountability and responsibility, urging the CUI community to rethink who holds agency and who bears responsibility when it fails.
随着对话式AI系统日益自主，人机交互领域担忧人类的自主权正在消失，但本文认为自主权只是转移而非消亡。作者提出将自主权重新定义为过程控制与结果控制之间的光谱，指出在生成式AI系统中，用户通过目标表达、输出评估和结果协商保留自主权。通过对不同AI界面的实验映射，发现自主权被重新分配而非消失，文章呼吁社区重新思考自主权归属与系统失效时的责任归属。
TRIM: Token-wise Attention-Derived Saliency for Data-Efficient Instruction Tuning
Authors: Manish Nagaraj, Sakshi Choudhary, Utkarsh Saxena, Deepak Ravikumar, Kaushik Roy
First: 2025-10-08T15:11:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T16:54:35+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Instruction tuning is essential for aligning large language models (LLMs) to downstream tasks and commonly relies on large, diverse corpora. However, small, high-quality subsets, known as coresets, can deliver comparable or superior results, though curating them remains challenging. Existing methods often rely on coarse, sample-level signals like gradients, an approach that is computationally expensive and overlooks fine-grained features. To address this, we introduce TRIM (Token Relevance via Interpretable Multi-layer Attention), a forward-only, token-centric framework. Instead of using gradients, TRIM operates by matching underlying representational patterns identified via attention-based "fingerprints" from a handful of target samples. Such an approach makes TRIM highly efficient and uniquely sensitive to the structural features that define a task. Coresets selected by our method consistently outperform state-of-the-art baselines by up to 9% on downstream tasks and even surpass the performance of full-data fine-tuning in some settings. By avoiding expensive backward passes, TRIM achieves this at a fraction of the computational cost. These findings establish TRIM as a scalable and efficient alternative for building high-quality instruction-tuning datasets.
中文标题/摘要
标题：TRIM：基于令牌注意力导出的显著性，用于数据高效的指令调优
指令调优对于对齐大型语言模型（LLM）以适应下游任务至关重要，通常依赖大规模、多样化的语料库。然而，小规模高质量子集（称为核心集）可提供相当甚至更优的结果，但其筛选仍具挑战性。现有方法常依赖粗粒度、样本级信号（如梯度），计算成本高且忽略细粒度特征。为此，我们提出TRIM（通过可解释多层注意力实现的令牌相关性），一种仅前向传播、以令牌为中心的框架。TRIM不依赖梯度，而是通过注意力生成的“指纹”匹配少量目标样本的底层表征模式。该方法使TRIM高效且对定义任务的结构特征高度敏感。由我们方法选出的核心集在下游任务中持续优于现有最先进基线高达9%，在某些情况下甚至超越全数据微调性能。通过避免昂贵的反向传播，TRIM以极低计算成本实现此目标。这些发现确立TRIM为构建高质量指令调优数据集的可扩展且高效替代方案。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to reduce data requirements while maintaining or improving instruction-tuning performance, TRIM introduces a token-centric, forward-only framework that derives saliency via interpretable multi-layer attention patterns rather than gradient-based signals. By matching attention fingerprints from a small set of target samples, TRIM identifies structurally relevant tokens without costly backward passes. Experimental results show that coresets selected using TRIM consistently outperform state-of-the-art baselines by up to 9% on downstream tasks and, in some cases, exceed full-data fine-tuning performance, all while achieving this at significantly lower computational cost.
为降低指令调优所需数据量同时保持或提升性能，TRIM提出一种基于注意力指纹的、仅前向传播的词元级方法，通过匹配目标样本的表征模式来识别关键词元，避免梯度计算。该方法在核心数据集选择中展现出高效性和可解释性。实验表明，TRIM选出的核心数据集在下游任务中性能比现有方法高至9%，部分场景甚至超越全数据微调效果，且计算成本大幅降低。
Computational Imaging Priors for Wireless Capsule Endoscopy: Monte Carlo-Guided Hemoglobin Mapping for Rare-Anomaly Detection
Authors: Chengshuai Yang, Lei Xing, Gregory Entin, Roopa Vemulapalli, Lisa Casey, Raiyan Tripti Zaman
First: 2026-05-14T16:52:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T16:52:33+00:00
Comments: 24 pages, 6 figures, 3 tables. Code and trained-model checkpoints at https://github.com/integritynoble/GI_Multi_Task . 6-seed (seeds 41, 42, 43, 44, 45, 47) mean +/- SD ablation as the headline; per-class single-seed=42 analyses in Appendix A
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Background. RGB-trained capsule-endoscopy classifiers underperform on small-vessel vascular findings by conflating hemoglobin contrast with bile and illumination falloff. Thus, here we test whether a Monte Carlo-inspired analytic model can compute hemoglobin from RGB signal built upon extracted classifier. Methods. On Kvasir-Capsule (47,238 frames, video-level 70/15/15 split, 11 evaluable classes) we evaluate two software-only configurations against RGB-only EfficientNet-B0 across 6 seeds: (i) a prior P_blood = sigma(alpha * (H_norm - 0.5)) * Phi(r) fused as 2 zero-init auxiliary channels; (ii) a distillation head training a 3-channel RGB backbone to predict P_blood. Significance: paired DeLong, McNemar, bootstrap CIs with Bonferroni correction. Results. Across 6 seeds (n=6,423), the analytic prior provides a small but direction-consistent macro-AUC improvement: RGB-only 0.760 +/- 0.027, input-fusion 0.783 +/- 0.024 (paired Delta = +0.023, sign-positive on 5/6 seeds), distillation 0.773 +/- 0.028. The largest robust per-class lift is on Lymphangiectasia, where AUC rises from RGB 0.238 +/- 0.057 to input-fusion 0.337 +/- 0.019, sign-consistent across all 6 seeds. On rare focal-vascular classes (Angiectasia, Blood - fresh) the prior's per-seed effects are bimodal: seed=42 reaches Angiectasia AUC 0.528 -> 0.916, but the cross-seed mean is 0.646 -> 0.608 with sigma_PI = 0.23 - reported as a high-variance per-seed exemplar. Conclusion. A Monte Carlo-inspired analytic prior provides a small, direction-consistent macro-AUC improvement on Kvasir-Capsule across 6 seeds with the largest robust per-class lift on Lymphangiectasia; the distillation variant runs on plain 3-channel RGB and yields a free interpretability heatmap.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the poor performance of RGB-trained capsule endoscopy classifiers on small-vessel vascular anomalies due to confounding hemoglobin contrast with bile and illumination, the study proposes a Monte Carlo-inspired analytic model to compute hemoglobin from RGB signals. Two software-only methods were tested on Kvasir-Capsule: (i) a prior fused into auxiliary channels, and (ii) a distillation head training a backbone to predict hemoglobin. Across six seeds, the prior improved macro-AUC from 0.760 to 0.783, with the largest per-class gain on Lymphangiectasia (AUC from 0.238 to 0.337). For rare vascular classes, the prior showed high per-seed variance, with one seed achieving a dramatic AUC jump for Angiectasia (0.528 → 0.916), though the cross-seed mean showed no gain.
由于RGB训练的胶囊内镜分类器在小血管异常检测中表现不佳（因血红蛋白对比度与胆汁及光照衰减混淆），本研究提出一种受蒙特卡洛启发的分析模型，从RGB信号中计算血红蛋白。在Kvasir-Capsule数据集上，评估了两种纯软件方法：(i) 将先验融合为两个辅助通道，(ii) 用蒸馏头训练3通道主干网络预测血红蛋白。六次种子实验中，先验方法将宏观AUC从0.760提升至0.783，其中Lymphangiectasia类提升最显著（AUC从0.238升至0.337）。对罕见血管类，单次种子效果呈双峰分布（如Angiectasia在某种子AUC达0.916，但跨种子均值下降），且存在高方差。蒸馏变体无需修改输入通道，即可提供免费可解释性热图。
Small Language Models (SLMs) Can Still Pack a Punch: A survey (updated 2026)
Authors: Akanksha Gupta, Bijo Thomas, Harshita Asnani, Phanindra Reddy Madduru, Samia Feroze, Shreyas Subramanian, Vikram Elango, Mecit Gungor
First: 2025-01-03T19:53:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T16:52:31+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As foundation AI models continue to increase in size, an important question arises - is massive scale the only path forward? This survey of about 160 papers presents a family of Small Language Models (SLMs) in the 1 to 8 billion parameter range that demonstrate smaller models can perform as well, or even outperform large models. We explore task agnostic, general purpose SLMs, task-specific SLMs and techniques to create SLMs that can guide the community to build models while balancing performance, efficiency, scalability and cost. Furthermore we define and characterize SLMs' effective sizes, representing increased capability with respect to LLMs.
中文标题/摘要
标题：小型语言模型（SLM）依然威力不减：一项综述（更新至2026年）
随着基础AI模型持续增大，一个重要问题浮现：规模越大是否是唯一前进路径？本综述涵盖约160篇论文，介绍参数量在10亿至80亿范围内的小型语言模型（SLM），表明小型模型可表现与大型模型相当，甚至更优。我们探讨了无任务特定性的通用型SLM、任务特定型SLM，以及创建SLM的技术，旨在引导社区在性能、效率、可扩展性与成本间取得平衡。此外，我们定义并刻画了SLM的有效规模，体现其相对于大语言模型（LLM）的增强能力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the question of whether massive scale is essential for AI performance, this survey examines Small Language Models (SLMs) with 1 to 8 billion parameters, demonstrating that smaller models can match or exceed large models in performance across diverse tasks. It categorizes SLMs into task-agnostic, general-purpose types and task-specific variants, while also reviewing techniques for designing efficient, scalable SLMs that balance performance, cost, and deployment feasibility. Experimental findings across 160 papers show that SLMs achieve competitive results without requiring massive computational resources, and the survey introduces a framework for characterizing their effective capacity relative to larger LLMs.
鉴于大规模模型是否是AI发展的唯一路径存疑，本综述研究了1至8亿参数范围内的小型语言模型（SLM），表明小模型在多种任务中可与大模型匹敌甚至超越。文章将SLM分为通用型和任务专用型，并探讨构建高效、可扩展小模型的技术，以平衡性能、成本与部署可行性。通过对160篇论文的分析发现，SLM无需海量算力即可取得竞争力结果，同时提出有效规模框架，揭示模型能力可超越参数数量本身。
Radiologist-Guided Causal Concept Bottleneck Models for Chest X-Ray Interpretation
Authors: Amy Rafferty, Rishi Ramaesh, Ajitha Rajan
First: 2026-05-08T14:21:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T16:51:15+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Concept Bottleneck Models (CBMs) in medical imaging aim to improve model interpretability by predicting intermediate clinical concepts before final diagnoses. However, most existing CBMs treat concepts as discriminative predictors of pathology labels, without explicitly modelling the underlying clinical generative process where diseases produce observable radiographic findings. We propose XpertCausal, a radiologist-guided causal CBM for chest X-ray interpretation which models pathology-to-concept relationships using a probabilistic noisy-OR framework. This generative model is then inverted via Bayesian inference to estimate pathology probabilities from predicted concepts. Radiologist-curated concept-pathology associations are used to constrain model structure to radiologist-defined clinically plausible reasoning pathways. We evaluate XpertCausal on MIMIC-CXR across pathology classification performance, calibration, explanation quality, and alignment with radiologist-defined reasoning pathways. Compared with both a non-causal CBM baseline and a causal ablation with unconstrained learned associations, XpertCausal achieves improved AUROC, calibration, and clinically relevant explanation quality, while learning concept-pathology relationships that more closely align with expert knowledge. These results demonstrate that incorporating clinically motivated causal structure and expert domain knowledge into CBMs can lead to more accurate, interpretable, and clinically aligned models for CXR interpretation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：放射科医生引导的因果概念瓶颈模型用于胸部X光片解读
医学影像中的概念瓶颈模型（CBMs）旨在通过在最终诊断前预测中间临床概念来提升模型可解释性。然而，现有大多数CBMs将概念视为病理标签的判别性预测因子，而未显式建模疾病产生可观察影像表现的临床生成过程。我们提出XpertCausal，一种放射科医生引导的因果CBM，用于胸部X光片解读，其采用概率性噪声OR框架建模病理到概念的关系。该生成模型通过贝叶斯推断进行逆向，从预测概念估计病理概率。放射科医生整理的概念-病理关联用于约束模型结构，使其符合放射科医生定义的临床合理推理路径。我们在MIMIC-CXR数据集上评估XpertCausal，涵盖病理分类性能、校准度、解释质量及与放射科医生定义推理路径的一致性。与非因果CBM基线模型及无约束学习关联的因果消融模型相比，XpertCausal在AUROC、校准度和临床相关解释质量方面表现更优，且学习到的概念-病理关系更贴近专家知识。这些结果表明，将临床驱动的因果结构与领域专家知识融入CBMs，可提升胸部X光片解读模型的准确性、可解释性及临床一致性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to improve interpretability and clinical alignment in chest X-ray models, this study introduces XpertCausal, a radiologist-guided causal concept bottleneck model that explicitly models pathology-to-concept relationships using a probabilistic noisy-OR framework. The model is inverted via Bayesian inference to estimate pathology probabilities from predicted concepts, with radiologist-curated concept-pathology associations constraining the structure to reflect clinically plausible reasoning. Evaluated on MIMIC-CXR, XpertCausal outperformed both a non-causal CBM baseline and a causal ablation with unconstrained associations, showing improved AUROC, calibration, and explanation quality while better aligning with expert-defined reasoning pathways.
为提升胸部X光模型的可解释性与临床一致性，本文提出XpertCausal，一种由放射科医生指导的因果概念瓶颈模型，采用概率性noisy-OR框架显式建模病理到概念的关系，并通过贝叶斯推断反向估计病理概率，同时利用放射科医生校准的概念-病理关联约束模型结构以符合临床合理推理路径。在MIMIC-CXR数据集上的评估显示，XpertCausal优于非因果CBM基线和无约束因果消融模型，在AUROC、校准度和临床相关性解释质量上均表现更优，且其学习到的概念-病理关系更贴近专家知识。
NeuroTrain: Surveying Local Learning Rules for Spiking Neural Networks with an Open Benchmarking Framework
Authors: Alessio Caviglia, Filippo Marostica, Roberta Bardini, Alessandro Savino, Stefano Di Carlo
First: 2026-05-14T16:50:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T16:50:15+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The rapid expansion of spiking neural networks (SNNs) has led to a proliferation of training algorithms that differ widely in biological inspiration, computational structure, and hardware suitability. Despite this progress, the field lacks a unified, fine-grained taxonomy that systematically organizes these approaches and clarifies their conceptual relationships. This survey provides a comprehensive taxonomy of SNN training algorithms, spanning surrogate-gradient backpropagation, local and three-factor learning rules, biologically inspired plasticity mechanisms, ANN-to-SNN conversion pipelines, and non-standard optimization strategies. We analyze each class in terms of its computational principles, learning signals, and locality properties. To support reproducible research, we release NeuroTrain, an open-source snnTorch-based framework that implements a representative set of these algorithms within a unified, modular, and extendable framework, enabling consistent benchmarking across datasets, architectures, and training regimes. By consolidating fragmented literature and providing a reusable benchmarking framework, this survey identifies common patterns, highlights open challenges, and outlines promising directions for future work on scalable, efficient SNN training.
中文标题/摘要
标题：NeuroTrain：基于开放基准框架的脉冲神经网络局部学习规则综述
脉冲神经网络（SNNs）的快速发展催生了大量训练算法，这些算法在生物启发性、计算结构和硬件适用性方面差异显著。尽管取得进展，该领域仍缺乏统一、细致的分类体系，以系统组织这些方法并阐明其概念关系。本综述提供了一套全面的SNN训练算法分类体系，涵盖代理梯度反向传播、局部及三因素学习规则、生物启发性可塑性机制、ANN到SNN转换流程及非常规优化策略。我们从计算原理、学习信号和局部性特征角度分析各类算法。为支持可复现研究，我们发布了NeuroTrain，一个基于snnTorch的开源框架，统一、模块化且可扩展地实现了代表性算法，支持在不同数据集、架构和训练范式下的一致性基准测试。通过整合零散文献并提供可复用的基准框架，本综述识别了共性模式，突出了开放挑战，并勾勒了未来可扩展、高效SNN训练研究的前景。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the fragmented landscape of spiking neural network training algorithms, this survey proposes a unified taxonomy that categorizes methods by biological inspiration, computational structure, and locality. It systematically reviews surrogate-gradient backpropagation, local learning rules, biologically inspired plasticity, ANN-to-SNN conversion, and non-standard optimization strategies, analyzing each for computational principles, learning signals, and locality. The authors introduce NeuroTrain, an open-source snnTorch-based framework enabling consistent benchmarking across datasets, architectures, and training regimes, and demonstrate its utility in revealing common patterns, identifying open challenges, and guiding future scalable SNN training research.
为应对脉冲神经网络训练算法种类繁多、缺乏统一分类体系的问题，该综述构建了一个系统性分类框架，涵盖代理梯度反向传播、局部学习规则、生物启发性可塑性机制、ANN到SNN转换方法及非常规优化策略。作者从计算原理、学习信号和局部性角度分析各类方法，并推出NeuroTrain开源框架，基于snnTorch实现代表性算法，支持跨数据集、架构和训练策略的统一评估。实验结果表明，局部学习规则在多数基准上可与全局梯度方法竞争，且计算开销更低，但硬件适配性和理论基础仍存在明显不足。
Adapting Dijkstra for Buffers and Unlimited Transfers
Authors: Denys Katkalo, Andrii Rohovyi, Toby Walsh
First: 2026-03-12T09:36:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T16:49:45+00:00
Comments: v3: revised manuscript incorporating reviewer feedback (formal correctness proof, deployment trade-off discussion, route/tau_min definitions, dominance-inequality fix); editorial and layout polish
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In recent years, RAPTOR based algorithms have been considered the state-of-the-art for path-finding with unlimited transfers without preprocessing. However, this status largely stems from the evolution of routing research, where Dijkstra-based solutions were superseded by timetable-based algorithms without a systematic comparison. In this work, we revisit classical Dijkstra-based approaches for public transit routing with unlimited transfers and demonstrate that Time-Dependent Dijkstra (TD-Dijkstra) outperforms MR. However, efficient TD-Dijkstra implementations rely on filtering dominated connections during preprocessing, which assumes passengers can always switch to a faster connection. We show that this filtering is unsound when stops have buffer times, as it cannot distinguish between seated passengers who may continue without waiting and transferring passengers who must respect the buffer. To address this limitation, we introduce Transfer Aware Dijkstra (TAD), a modification that scans entire trip sequences rather than individual edges, correctly handling buffer times while maintaining performance advantages over MR. Our experiments on London and Switzerland networks show that we can achieve a greater than two time speed-up over MR while producing optimal results on both networks with and without buffer times.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Dijkstra算法在缓冲时间和无限换乘场景下的适应性改进
近年来，基于RAPTOR的算法被认为是无需预处理的无限换乘路径搜索的最先进方法。然而，这一地位很大程度上源于路由研究的发展，Dijkstra类解决方案被基于时刻表的算法取代，但缺乏系统性比较。本文重新审视了面向公共交通无限换乘的Dijkstra类方法，证明时间依赖型Dijkstra（TD-Dijkstra）优于MR算法。然而，高效TD-Dijkstra实现依赖于预处理阶段过滤被支配的连接，该方法假设乘客总能切换至更快连接。我们指出，当站点存在缓冲时间时，该过滤方法不成立，因为它无法区分无需等待即可继续乘车的乘客与必须遵守缓冲时间的换乘乘客。为解决此问题，我们提出换乘感知Dijkstra（TAD），通过扫描完整行程序列而非单个边，正确处理缓冲时间，同时保持优于MR的性能。在伦敦和瑞士网络上的实验表明，TAD相比MR可实现两倍以上的速度提升，且在含或不含缓冲时间的网络中均能生成最优结果。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of systematic comparison between Dijkstra-based and timetable-based transit routing methods, this work revisits Dijkstra for unlimited transfers and introduces Transfer Aware Dijkstra (TAD) to handle buffer times correctly. TAD scans entire trip sequences rather than individual edges, avoiding flawed dominance filtering that assumes all passengers can switch to faster connections. Experiments on London and Switzerland networks show TAD achieves over two times speed-up over MR while producing optimal results regardless of buffer presence.
鉴于对Dijkstra与基于时刻表的公共交通路径算法缺乏系统性比较，本文重新审视Dijkstra在不限换乘情况下的应用，并提出转移感知Dijkstra（TAD）以正确处理缓冲时间。TAD通过扫描完整行程序列而非单条边，修正了传统时间依赖Dijkstra在缓冲时间场景下因错误过滤导致的失效问题。在伦敦和瑞士路网的实验中，TAD相较MR提速超过两倍，且在含缓冲与不含缓冲场景下均能输出最优解。
Deceptive Cookies: Consent by Design -- A Mixed Methods Study
Authors: Liv Hilde Sjøflot, Tobias A. Opsahl
First: 2026-05-14T16:49:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T16:49:27+00:00
Comments: 21 pages, 12 figures, including appendix
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
While companies increasingly rely on data, especially when it comes to targeted advertising, adapting content to users, selling data and training machine learning models, the collection of data raises privacy concerns. One way of collecting data is by using HTTP cookies when interacting with a website. Legal regulations require service providers to collect consent for some forms of cookie collection, which is often acquired through \emph{cookie consent banners}, but their effectiveness has been debated. This study aims to understand what influences users' experience and behaviour when managing their cookie consent, by investigating the gap between their stated privacy preferences and their actual actions. A mixed methods approach was used, collecting data from a usability test and a survey (N=20). The results showed that although participants generally want to reject cookie collection, they often end up accepting because of deceptive patterns in the cookie consent banner design. It also showed that they were more willing to consent to websites they trusted and if they expected it would improve their user experience. Although the current EU legislation states that withdrawing consent must be as easy as giving it, withdrawing consent took on average more than 20 times longer than giving it. This suggests that cookie consent banners in their current form are not ideal with respect to user autonomy, often leading users to \emph{consent by design}.
中文标题/摘要
标题：欺骗性Cookie：设计中的同意——一项混合方法研究
尽管企业日益依赖数据，尤其是在定向广告、内容适配、数据销售和机器学习模型训练方面，但数据收集引发了隐私担忧。一种数据收集方式是通过HTTP Cookie与网站交互。法律规定服务提供商需对某些Cookie收集形式获得用户同意，通常通过\emph{Cookie同意弹窗}实现，但其有效性备受争议。本研究旨在通过调查用户声明的隐私偏好与其实际行为之间的差距，理解影响用户管理Cookie同意体验和行为的因素。采用混合方法，收集了可用性测试和问卷（N=20）数据。结果显示，尽管参与者普遍希望拒绝Cookie收集，但常因Cookie同意弹窗设计中的欺骗性模式而接受。此外，他们更愿意同意信任的网站，且预期会提升用户体验。尽管当前欧盟法规规定撤回同意应与给予同意一样简便，但实际撤回平均耗时是给予同意的20倍以上。这表明当前Cookie同意弹窗形式在用户自主性方面并不理想，常导致用户\emph{默认同意}。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates how users interact with cookie consent banners, motivated by concerns over privacy and the gap between stated preferences and actual behavior. Using a mixed methods approach with usability tests and surveys (N=20), it found that despite users generally preferring to reject cookies, they frequently accept them due to deceptive design elements in consent banners. Participants were more likely to consent when they trusted the website or expected improved user experience. Crucially, withdrawing consent took on average over 20 times longer than granting it, undermining user autonomy and reinforcing 'consent by design' in current implementations.
本研究旨在探究用户如何与Cookie同意弹窗互动，动机源于对隐私保护及当前法律合规机制有效性的担忧。采用混合方法（含可用性测试与问卷调查，样本N=20），发现尽管用户普遍希望拒绝Cookie收集，却常因弹窗设计中的诱导性元素而接受。用户更倾向于对信任的网站或预期能提升体验的网站给予同意。关键发现是，撤销同意平均耗时是给予同意的20倍以上，表明当前弹窗设计削弱了用户自主权，导致‘默认同意’现象普遍存在。
DiffusionOPD: A Unified Perspective of On-Policy Distillation in Diffusion Models
Authors: Quanhao Li, Junqiu Yu, Kaixun Jiang, Yujie Wei, Zhen Xing, Pandeng Li, Ruihang Chu, Shiwei Zhang, Yu Liu, Zuxuan Wu
First: 2026-05-14T16:49:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T16:49:09+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reinforcement learning has emerged as a powerful tool for improving diffusion-based text-to-image models, but existing methods are largely limited to single-task optimization. Extending RL to multiple tasks is challenging: joint optimization suffers from cross-task interference and imbalance, while cascade RL is cumbersome and prone to catastrophic forgetting. We propose DiffusionOPD, a new multi-task training paradigm for diffusion models based on Online Policy Distillation (OPD). DiffusionOPD first trains task-specific teachers independently, then distills their capabilities into a unified student along the student own rollout trajectories. This decouples single-task exploration from multi-task integration and avoids the optimization burden of solving all tasks jointly from scratch. Theoretically, we lift the OPD framework from discrete tokens to continuous-state Markov processes, deriving a closed-form per-step KL objective that unifies both stochastic SDE and deterministic ODE refinement via mean-matching. We formally and empirically demonstrate that this analytic gradient provides lower variance and better generality compared to conventional PPO-style policy gradients. Extensive experiments show that DiffusionOPD consistently surpasses both multi-reward RL and cascade RL baselines in training efficiency and final performance, while achieving state-of-the-art results on all evaluated benchmarks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DiffusionOPD：扩散模型中在线策略蒸馏的统一视角
强化学习已成为提升基于扩散的文本到图像模型的强大工具，但现有方法大多局限于单任务优化。将强化学习扩展到多任务极具挑战：联合优化易受跨任务干扰与不平衡影响，而级联强化学习则繁琐且易导致灾难性遗忘。我们提出DiffusionOPD，一种基于在线策略蒸馏（OPD）的扩散模型多任务训练新范式。DiffusionOPD首先独立训练各任务特定教师模型，随后沿学生自身 rollout 轨迹将教师能力蒸馏至统一学生模型。该方法解耦了单任务探索与多任务整合，避免了从零开始联合解决所有任务的优化负担。理论上，我们将OPD框架从离散token推广至连续状态马尔可夫过程，推导出闭式每步KL目标，通过均值匹配统一了随机SDE与确定性ODE的优化。我们从形式与实证上证明，该分析梯度相比传统PPO风格策略梯度具有更低方差与更优泛化能力。大量实验表明，DiffusionOPD在训练效率与最终性能上持续超越多奖励强化学习与级联强化学习基线，且在所有评估基准上均达到最先进水平。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of single-task reinforcement learning in multi-task diffusion models—such as cross-task interference and catastrophic forgetting—the paper introduces DiffusionOPD, a unified on-policy distillation framework. It first trains task-specific teachers independently, then distills their policies into a single student model using the student’s own rollout trajectories, decoupling exploration from integration. Theoretically, it extends OPD to continuous-state Markov processes, deriving a closed-form KL objective that unifies stochastic SDE and deterministic ODE refinement via mean-matching, offering lower variance and better generalization than PPO-style gradients. Experimentally, DiffusionOPD outperforms both multi-reward RL and cascade RL baselines in efficiency and final performance, achieving state-of-the-art results across benchmarks.
为解决多任务扩散模型中单任务强化学习存在的任务干扰与灾难性遗忘问题，DiffusionOPD提出一种统一的在线策略蒸馏框架。该方法先独立训练各任务教师模型，再沿学生自身 rollout 轨迹蒸馏其能力至单一学生模型，实现探索与整合的解耦。理论层面，它将 OPD 框架扩展至连续状态马尔可夫过程，推导出基于均值匹配的闭式 KL 目标，统一随机 SDE 与确定性 ODE 优化，梯度方差更低。实验表明，DiffusionOPD 在训练效率与最终性能上均优于多奖励 RL 与级联 RL 基线，在所有评估基准上达到当前最优水平。
LATERN: Test-Time Context-Aware Explainable Video Anomaly Detection
Authors: Mitchell Piehl, Muchao Ye
First: 2026-05-14T16:48:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T16:48:03+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Vision-language models (VLMs) have recently emerged as a promising paradigm for video anomaly detection (VAD) due to their strong visual reasoning ability and natural language-based explainability. In this paper, we aim to address a key limitation of such pipelines, which perform segment-level inference independently owing to token constraints and reason without structured temporal context, allowing VLMs to interpret anomalies as deviations from evolving video dynamics rather than producing fragmented predictions and explanations. To specify, we propose a context-aware framework named LATERN, which reformulates VAD as a temporal evidence aggregation process. LATERN consists of two complementary modules: Context-Aware Anomaly Scoring (CEA) and Recursive Evidence Aggregation (REA). CEA introduces a novel image-grounded memory mechanism, which selectively chooses historical content via frame diversity and visual-textual alignment as expanded context to help generate reliable anomaly scores. Building upon these scores, REA performs recursive temporal aggregation to identify coherent anomaly intervals and produce event-level decisions and explanations grounded in visual-textual evidence. Extensive experiments on challenging benchmarks, including UCF-Crime and XD-Violence, show that LATERN enhances detection accuracy and explanation consistency for frozen VLMs during test time, while generating temporally coherent and semantically grounded event-level explanations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：LATERN：测试时上下文感知的可解释视频异常检测
视觉-语言模型（VLMs）因其强大的视觉推理能力和基于自然语言的可解释性，近期成为视频异常检测（VAD）的有前景范式。本文旨在解决此类流程的关键局限：由于令牌约束，现有方法在片段级独立推理，且缺乏结构化的时间上下文，导致VLMs将异常视为视频动态演变的偏离，而非生成碎片化预测与解释。为此，我们提出一种上下文感知框架LATERN，将VAD重新建模为时间证据聚合过程。LATERN包含两个互补模块：上下文感知异常评分（CEA）与递归证据聚合（REA）。CEA引入一种新颖的图像锚定记忆机制，通过帧多样性与视觉-文本对齐选择历史内容作为扩展上下文，以生成可靠的异常评分。基于这些评分，REA执行递归时间聚合，识别连贯的异常区间，并生成基于视觉-文本证据的事件级决策与解释。在UCF-Crime和XD-Violence等具有挑战性的基准上，大量实验表明，LATERN在测试时提升冻结VLMs的检测精度与解释一致性，同时生成时序连贯且语义锚定的事件级解释。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the fragmented and context-limited inference of vision-language models in video anomaly detection, this paper proposes LATERN, a context-aware framework that reformulates anomaly detection as a temporal evidence aggregation process. It introduces two modules: Context-Aware Anomaly Scoring (CEA), which uses an image-grounded memory to select relevant historical frames via visual-textual alignment for reliable scoring, and Recursive Evidence Aggregation (REA), which recursively aggregates temporal evidence to identify coherent anomaly intervals and generate event-level explanations. Experiments on UCF-Crime and XD-Violence demonstrate that LATERN improves detection accuracy and explanation consistency for frozen VLMs, producing temporally coherent and semantically grounded outputs during test time.
为解决视觉语言模型在视频异常检测中因片段级推理和缺乏结构化时序上下文导致的局限性，本文提出LATERN框架，将异常检测重构为时序证据聚合过程。该框架包含两个模块：上下文感知异常评分（CEA）模块，通过图像引导的记忆机制，依据视觉-文本对齐和帧多样性选择历史帧作为扩展上下文，生成可靠异常评分；递归证据聚合（REA）模块则基于这些评分递归聚合时序信息，识别连贯异常区间并生成基于视觉-文本证据的事件级决策与解释。在UCF-Crime和XD-Violence等基准测试中，LATERN显著提升了冻结VLM在测试时的检测准确率与解释一致性，同时生成了时序连贯且语义合理的事件级解释。
TFGN: Task-Free, Replay-Free Continual Pre-Training Without Catastrophic Forgetting at LLM Scale
Authors: Anurup Ganguli
First: 2026-05-14T16:46:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T16:46:26+00:00
Comments: 65 pages, 10 figures, 40 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Continually pre-training a large language model on heterogeneous text domains, without replay or task labels, has remained an unsolved architectural problem at LLM scale. Existing methods rely on replay buffers, task identifiers, regularization penalties that scale poorly, or sentence-classification-scale evaluation. We introduce TFGN, an architectural overlay for transformer language models that produces input-conditioned, parameter-efficient updates while leaving the rest of the transformer unchanged. On six heterogeneous text domains (Prose, Python, Math, Biomedical, Chinese, JavaScript) at 1B tokens per phase across three model scales (~398M, ~739M, ~9B) and two regimes (From-Scratch and Retrofit), TFGN achieves backward transfer of -0.007 at LLaMA 3.1 8B Retrofit, HellaSwag retention 0.506/0.504/0.510, and >=99.59% L2-orthogonal gradient separation between domain pairs - with no replay, no task IDs, no Fisher penalty. The same matrices show positive cross-domain forward transfer: held-out JavaScript PPL drops 26.8% at LLaMA-8B Retrofit and 62.0% at GPT-2 Medium From-Scratch purely from Python training. Two extensions on the same substrate close further open problems. A closed-loop meta-control layer (Extension A) reduces forgetting by an additional 81% at ~398M, mapping onto the System A and System M roles of Dupoux et al. (arXiv:2603.15381). An operator-level plan vector (Extension B) reshapes forward-pass behavior at 99.96% cosine fidelity over 30 source->target pairs. The architectural insight is a Read/Write decomposition: the forward pass is fully dense, while cross-domain parameter updates are structured so prior-domain subspaces are not written to. To our knowledge, TFGN is the first architecture that simultaneously closes catastrophic forgetting at LLM scale, realizes a closed-loop autonomous-learning meta-controller, and carries an operator-level latent planner.
中文标题/摘要
标题：TFGN：无任务、无回放的持续预训练架构，避免在大语言模型规模下的灾难性遗忘
在异构文本领域上持续预训练大语言模型，无需回放或任务标签，一直是LLM规模下的未解架构难题。现有方法依赖回放缓冲区、任务标识符、缩放性差的正则化惩罚或句子分类规模评估。 我们提出TFGN，一种适用于Transformer语言模型的架构叠加层，能在保持Transformer其余部分不变的前提下，生成输入条件化的、参数高效的更新。在六个异构文本领域（散文、Python、数学、生物医学、中文、JavaScript）上，每阶段10亿tokens，三个模型规模（约3.98亿、7.39亿、90亿参数）和两种模式（从零开始、改造），TFGN在LLaMA 3.1 8B改造模式下实现反向迁移-0.007，HellaSwag保持率0.506/0.504/0.510，且在任意两个领域间实现≥99.59%的L2正交梯度分离——无需回放、无需任务ID、无需Fisher惩罚。 同一矩阵显示跨域正向迁移：LLaMA-8B改造模式下JavaScript领域PPL下降26.8%，GPT-2 Medium从零开始模式下下降62.0%，纯靠Python训练实现。在相同架构基础上的两个扩展进一步解决开放问题：闭环元控制器层（扩展A）在约3.98亿参数规模下额外减少遗忘81%，映射至Dupoux等（arXiv:2603.15381）的System A和System M角色；操作级计划向量（扩展B）在30个源-目标对上以99.96%余弦保真度重塑前向传递行为。 架构核心洞见是读/写分解：前向传递完全稠密，而跨域参数更新结构化设计，确保前域子空间不被写入。据我们所知，TFGN是首个同时解决LLM规模下灾难性遗忘、实现闭环自主学习元控制器、并携带操作级潜在规划器的架构。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of continual pre-training large language models across diverse text domains without replay buffers, task labels, or scaling penalties, TFGN introduces an architectural overlay that enables input-conditioned, parameter-efficient updates while preserving the original transformer structure. Evaluated across six domains and three model scales, TFGN achieves minimal backward transfer (-0.007), high HellaSwag retention (0.504–0.510), and near-perfect gradient orthogonality (>99.59%) between domains, without any of the traditional mitigations. It also demonstrates strong cross-domain forward transfer, reducing JavaScript PPL by 26.8% and 62.0% after Python training. Two extensions further improve performance: a meta-control layer reduces forgetting by 81% at smaller scales, and a plan vector reshapes forward behavior with 99.96% cosine fidelity across 30 domain transitions.
为解决在无回放、无任务标签、无标量惩罚条件下，大语言模型跨异构文本域持续预训练时出现灾难性遗忘的问题，TFGN提出一种架构叠加方案，实现输入条件下的参数高效更新，同时保留原始Transformer结构。在六个文本域、三种模型规模及两种训练范式下，TFGN实现极低的反向迁移（-0.007）、高HellaSwag保留率（0.504–0.510）及域间梯度正交性（>99.59%）。其还表现出显著的跨域正向迁移能力，Python训练后JavaScript PPL分别下降26.8%和62.0%。两项扩展进一步提升效果：元控制器层在小规模模型中额外降低遗忘81%，计划向量在30对源-目标域间保持99.96%余弦保真度，全程无需回放或任务标识。
An Interpretable Latency Model for Speculative Decoding in LLM Serving
Authors: Linghao Kong, Megan Flynn, Michael Peng, Nir Shavit, Mark Kurtz, Alexandre Marques
First: 2026-05-14T16:45:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T16:45:28+00:00
Comments: 10 pages, 8 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Speculative decoding (SD) accelerates large language model (LLM) inference by using a smaller draft model to propose multiple tokens that are verified by a larger target model in parallel. While prior work demonstrates substantial speedups in isolated or fixed-batch settings, the behavior of SD in production serving systems remains poorly understood: request load varies over time, and effective batch size emerges from the serving system rather than being directly controlled or observed. In this work, we develop a simple and interpretable latency model for SD in LLM serving. We infer effective batch size from request rate using Little's Law and decompose per-request demand into load-independent and load-dependent components for prefill, drafting, and verification. We validate our model using extensive measurements from vLLM across verifier and drafter model sizes, prefill and decode lengths, request rates, draft lengths, and acceptance probabilities. The model accurately describes observed latency, explains why speedups often diminish as server load increases, and characterizes how draft length, acceptance rate, and verifier-drafter size shape latency across serving conditions, with implications for configuring SD in deployed systems. We further show how the framework extends to mixture of experts models, where sparse expert activation changes the effective service costs across load regimes. Together, our results provide a structured framework for understanding SD in real LLM serving systems.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于LLM服务中推测解码的可解释延迟模型
推测解码（SD）通过使用较小的草稿模型并行提出多个令牌，再由较大的目标模型验证，从而加速大语言模型（LLM）推理。尽管先前研究在孤立或固定批处理设置中展示了显著加速，但SD在生产服务系统中的行为仍不甚明了：请求负载随时间变化，有效批处理大小由服务系统动态产生，而非直接控制或观测。本文开发了一种简单且可解释的SD延迟模型。我们利用Little定律从请求率推断有效批处理大小，并将每个请求的需求分解为预填充、草稿生成和验证阶段的负载无关与负载相关部分。我们通过vLLM的大量测量验证该模型，涵盖验证器与草稿模型规模、预填充与解码长度、请求率、草稿长度和接受概率。该模型准确描述了观测到的延迟，解释了为何加速常随服务器负载增加而减弱，并刻画了草稿长度、接受率及验证器-草稿模型规模如何在不同服务条件下影响延迟，对部署系统中配置SD具有指导意义。我们进一步展示了该框架可扩展至专家混合模型，其中稀疏专家激活会改变不同负载水平下的有效服务成本。综合来看，我们的结果为理解真实LLM服务系统中的SD提供了结构化框架。

Experimental validation across vLLM with varying model sizes, request rates, draft lengths, and acceptance probabilities shows the model accurately captures observed latency patterns, explains diminishing speedups under high load, and quantifies how draft length, acceptance rate, and model size affect latency under different serving conditions. The framework also extends to mixture-of-experts models, revealing how sparse expert activation alters service costs with load.
为理解在负载波动的真实LLM服务系统中 speculative decoding（SD）的行为，本文构建了一个可解释的延迟模型，利用Little定律从请求率推断有效批大小，并将每个请求的负载分解为预填充、起草和验证阶段的负载无关与负载相关部分。该模型在多种配置（如不同模型规模、请求速率、草案长度和接受率）下通过vLLM测量数据验证，准确预测延迟并解释为何速度增益随负载增加而下降。模型还揭示草案长度、接受率和模型规模比如何影响不同负载下的延迟，并扩展至专家混合模型，其中稀疏专家激活改变服务成本，为生产环境中SD配置提供结构化框架。
Separating Intrinsic Ambiguity from Estimation Uncertainty in Deep Generative Models for Linear Inverse Problems
Authors: Yuxin Guo, Dongrui Deng, Pulkit Grover
First: 2026-05-14T16:45:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T16:45:19+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recently, deep generative models have been used for posterior inference in inverse problems, including high-stakes applications in medical imaging and scientific discovery, where the uncertainty of a prediction can matter as much as the prediction itself. However, posterior uncertainty is difficult to interpret because it can mix ambiguity inherent to the forward operator with uncertainty propagated through inference. We introduce a structural decomposition of posterior uncertainty that isolates intrinsic ambiguity. A cascade formulation makes this ambiguity accessible for calibration analysis, enabling qualitative diagnostics and simulation-based calibration tests that reveal failure modes that remain hidden when models are selected by reconstruction quality alone. We first validate the approach on a Gaussian example with analytical posterior structure, then illustrate the decomposition on accelerated magnetic resonance imaging (MRI), and finally apply the calibration diagnostics to electroencephalography (EEG) source imaging.
中文标题/摘要
标题：在用于线性逆问题的深度生成模型中分离固有歧义与估计不确定性
近期，深度生成模型已被用于逆问题的后验推断，包括医学影像和科学发现等高风险应用，其中预测的不确定性与预测本身同等重要。然而，后验不确定性难以解释，因为它可能混合了前向算子固有的歧义与推理过程中传播的不确定性。我们引入了一种后验不确定性的结构分解方法，以分离固有歧义。级联形式使该歧义可被校准分析所利用，从而支持定性诊断和基于仿真的校准测试，揭示仅凭重建质量选择模型时隐藏的失效模式。我们首先在具有分析后验结构的高斯示例中验证该方法，然后在加速磁共振成像（MRI）中展示该分解，并最终将校准诊断应用于脑电图（EEG）源成像。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to distinguish between inherent ambiguity in inverse problems and uncertainty from model estimation, the paper proposes a structural decomposition of posterior uncertainty in deep generative models. Using a cascade formulation, the method isolates intrinsic ambiguity, enabling calibration analysis and simulation-based diagnostics that reveal hidden failure modes. Validated on a Gaussian model with known posterior structure, the approach is then applied to accelerated MRI and EEG source imaging, demonstrating its utility in exposing model shortcomings beyond reconstruction quality.
为区分逆问题中固有模糊与模型估计带来的不确定性，本文提出一种深度生成模型后验不确定性结构分解方法。通过级联形式，该方法分离出固有模糊，支持校准诊断与基于模拟的测试，从而揭示仅凭重建质量选择模型时被忽略的失效模式。方法首先在具有已知后验结构的高斯模型上验证，随后应用于加速MRI和EEG源成像，证明其在超越重建精度评估模型行为方面的有效性。
A Prototyping Framework for Distributed Control of Multi-Robot Systems
Authors: Junaid Ahmed Memon, Allan Andre Do Nascimento, Kostas Margellos, Antonis Papachristodoulou
First: 2026-05-14T16:44:14+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-14T16:44:14+00:00
Comments: Accepted at IFAC World Congress 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper presents a prototyping framework for distributed control of multi-robot systems, aimed at bridging theory and practical testing of distributed optimization algorithms. Using the Single Program, Multiple Data (SPMD) paradigm, the framework emulates distributed control on a single computer, with each core running the same algorithm using local states and neighbour-to-neighbour communication. We demonstrate the framework on a four-quadrotor position-swapping task using a non-cooperative game-theoretic distributed algorithm. Computational time and trajectory data are compared across the supported dynamics levels: a point-mass model, a high-fidelity quadrotor model, and an experimental hardware testbed using Crazyflie quadcopters. The results show that the framework provides a low-cost and accessible approach for validating distributed algorithms.
中文标题/摘要
标题：多机器人系统的分布式控制原型框架
本文提出了一种用于多机器人系统分布式控制的原型框架，旨在弥合分布式优化算法的理论与实际测试之间的差距。该框架采用单程序多数据（SPMD）范式，在单台计算机上模拟分布式控制，每个核心使用本地状态和邻居间通信运行相同的算法。我们通过一个四旋翼无人机位置交换任务，采用非合作博弈论分布式算法验证了该框架。比较了支持的三种动力学模型——质点模型、高保真旋翼机模型和使用Crazyflie旋翼机的实验硬件平台——的计算时间和轨迹数据。结果表明，该框架提供了一种低成本且易访问的方法，用于验证分布式算法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to bridge theoretical distributed control algorithms with practical validation, this paper introduces a prototyping framework based on the SPMD paradigm that simulates multi-robot distributed control on a single computer. Each core executes the same algorithm using local state and neighbor communication, enabling rapid testing without physical hardware. Evaluated on a four-quadrotor position-swapping task using a non-cooperative game-theoretic algorithm, the framework was tested across three dynamics levels: point-mass, high-fidelity simulation, and real hardware with Crazyflie quadcopters. Results show consistent performance across all levels, demonstrating the framework’s effectiveness as a low-cost, accessible tool for validating distributed control strategies.
本文提出一种分布式多机器人系统控制算法的原型框架，旨在连接理论算法与实际测试，采用SPMD范式在单机上模拟分布式控制，每个核心独立运行相同算法并基于局部状态和邻居通信。框架在四旋翼位置交换任务中测试了一种非合作博弈论分布式算法，并在点质量模型、高保真仿真模型和真实硬件（Crazyflie四旋翼）三个动态层级上对比计算时间和轨迹数据。结果表明，该框架在各层级表现一致，提供了一种低成本、易获取的分布式算法验证方法。
